### [1] [Energy dissipation in linear viscoelasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05465)
*Juan Luis Gonzalez-Santander,Francesco Mainardi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了Becker、Lomnitz和Lambert线性粘弹性模型的比耗散Q⁻¹(ω)，推导了其在低频（ω→0⁺）与高频（ω→∞）下的渐近解析表达式，并推广得到广义Becker模型Q_ν⁻¹(ω)（ν∈(0,1]有理数）与扩展Jeffreys–Lomnitz模型Q_α⁻¹(ω)（α∈(−∞,1]）的新型闭式解；所有模型在ω→∞时均退化为Maxwell模型的比耗散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一刻画不同经典线性粘弹性模型的耗散特性，厘清其在极端频率极限下的物理等价性与参数连续性关系，为地震学、岩石力学等依赖Q模型的实际反演提供理论基准。

Method: 复模量分析法结合分数阶微积分工具：利用各模型本构方程导出复剪切模量G*(ω)，进而计算比耗散Q⁻¹(ω)=Im[G*(ω)]/Re[G*(ω)]；对广义模型采用Mittag-Leffler函数与Hankel围道积分技术获取闭式解，并通过极限分析验证模型间继承关系。

Result: 1) Becker与Lomnitz模型Q⁻¹(ω)在ω→0⁺与ω→∞具相同渐近标度律；2) 广义Becker模型Q_ν⁻¹(ω)对有理ν∈(0,1]存在闭式解，且ν=0→Maxwell、ν=1→原始Becker；3) 扩展Jeffreys–Lomnitz模型Q_α⁻¹(ω)给出两个覆盖α∈(−∞,1]的闭式解，α=0→Lomnitz、α=1→Maxwell；4) 所有扩展模型在ω→∞时均收敛至Maxwell模型Q⁻¹(ω)=ωτ。

Conclusion: Maxwell模型是各类广义线性粘弹性模型在高频极限下的普适渐近基准，而Becker与Lomnitz模型通过参数连续变形可统一纳入含分数阶导数的Jeffreys型框架，揭示了地震衰减Q值建模中时间尺度分离与记忆效应的数学本质。

Abstract: We compare the specific dissipation $Q^{-1}\left( ω\right) $ for the Becker, Lomnitz, and Lambert models in linear viscoelasticity. Graphically, the specific dissipation of these models behaves similarly as $ω\rightarrow 0^{+}$ and $ω\rightarrow +\infty$. Furthermore, we obtain analytic formulas for the asymptotic behavior of $Q^{-1}\left( ω\right) $ in the Becker and Lomnitz models as $ω\rightarrow 0^{+}$ and $ω\rightarrow +\infty$, where the latter limit coincides with the specific dissipation of the Maxwell model. We also derive a novel closed-form expression for the specific dissipation in the generalized Becker model, $Q_{ν}^{-1}\left( ω\right) $, when the parameter $ν\in \left( 0,1\right] $ is rational. When $ν\rightarrow 0$, we recover the specific dissipation of the Maxwell model, whereas when $ν=1$, we recover that of the original Becker model. In addition, we derive two new closed-form expressions for the specific dissipation for the extended Jeffreys--Lomnitz model $Q_{α}^{-1}\left( ω\right) $, the first being valid for $α\in \left( 0,1\right] $, and the second for $α\in \left( -\infty ,1\right] $. When $α\rightarrow 0$, we recover the specific dissipation of the Lomnitz model, whereas when $α=1$, we recover that of the Maxwell model. Finally, we conclude that the asymptotic behavior of $Q_{α}^{-1}\left( ω\right) $ as $ω\rightarrow +\infty $ coincides with the specific dissipation of the Maxwell model. Keywords:

</details>


### [2] [Information Loss under Gauge Reduction of Discrete Electromagnetic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05473)
*Jean-Pierre Magnot*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Whitney离散化电磁学中规范约化下的相对熵，利用离散Hodge分解将规范方向与物理自由度分离；对辅助势空间上的高斯分布，相对熵精确分解为物理边缘分布的相对熵与一个非负的条件规范贡献，后者由Schur补、条件均值及物理–规范关联刻画；并证明物理散度等于所有高斯辅助延拓中的最小值；Fisher信息在无穷小层次具有类似分解；通过双变量模型说明不同辅助势可对应相同物理统计，从而给出离散规范约化的信息几何诠释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解规范场论中信息流在物理自由度与冗余规范自由度之间的分配，建立不依赖于规范变量物理意义的信息几何框架。

Method: 基于Whitney离散化电磁学，运用离散Hodge分解分离规范（exact）、调和（harmonic）与余恰当（coexact）分量；对高斯分布的辅助势空间，结合条件概率、Schur补与协方差分解推导相对熵的正交分解；引入高斯辅助延拓的变分极小化刻画物理相对熵；分析Fisher信息矩阵在规范约化下的块结构。

Result: 1) 相对熵严格分解为物理边缘相对熵与非负条件规范项；2) 条件规范项由Schur补、条件均值及物理–规范协方差显式表达；3) 物理相对熵等于所有高斯辅助延拓中相对熵的下确界；4) Fisher信息具有对应分解；5) 存在统计不可区分的辅助势实现同一物理分布。

Conclusion: 规范约化在信息几何中体现为相对熵与Fisher信息在Hodge正交分解下的自然分裂，物理相对熵是规范不变的信息度量，且无需赋予规范变量本体论地位。

Abstract: We study relative entropy under gauge reduction in Whitney-discretized electromagnetism. The discrete Hodge decomposition separates exact gauge directions from harmonic and coexact physical degrees of freedom. For Gaussian distributions on the auxiliary potential space, relative entropy splits exactly into the relative entropy of the physical marginals and a nonnegative conditional gauge contribution. We express this contribution through Schur complements, conditional means, and physical--gauge correlations, and characterize equality between auxiliary and physical relative entropies. The physical divergence is also shown to be the minimum over all Gaussian auxiliary extensions. At the infinitesimal level, Fisher information admits an analogous decomposition. A two-variable model illustrates how statistically distinct auxiliary potentials may represent identical physical statistics. The construction provides an information-geometric interpretation of discrete gauge reduction without assigning physical significance to the eliminated gauge variables.

</details>


### [3] [Distance Energies and Negative Type of Flat Tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05622)
*Ye Zhou*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了平坦环面T_Λ上Borel概率测度μ的距离能量I_α(μ)，通过分析ρ_Λ^α的Fourier系数揭示了割点集的定量Fourier特征，并得出Haar测度非任何正幂次距离能量的局部极大值，且所有维数≥2的平坦环面具有零上确界负型与广义圆度；进一步完全解决了两类环面（正交矩形与正六边形）上的全局最大化问题，刻画了不同α区间下的极值测度结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解平坦环面上距离能量I_α(μ)的极值测度结构，特别是Haar测度的极值性、负型与广义圆度等几何泛函分析性质，并揭示割点集在Fourier空间中的定量表现。

Method: 运用调和分析方法，结合对偶格点（dual lattice）上的渐近Fourier系数估计，分析距离函数ρ_Λ^α在Voronoi胞腔面法向方向上的符号与主渐近行为；辅以变分论证、对称性分析与群作用下的测度分类，处理正交矩形与正六边形环面的全局优化问题。

Result: 1) 对任意Voronoi面及其法向，存在沿该方向趋近的对偶格点列，使得ρ_Λ^α的Fourier系数为正且具由该面决定的显式主渐近；2) Haar测度不是任何α>0下I_α的局部极大值（即使限制于光滑密度）；3) 所有dim≥2平坦环面的supremal negative type与generalized roundness均为0；4) 在正交矩形环面上，I_α极大值测度随α发生三阶段相变：1≤α<2时为二阶挠子群平移的均匀测度；α=2时为该子群平移的任意平衡耦合；α>2时仅为等权对径点对；5) 在正六边形环面上，对所有α≥1，极大值测度恰为某三阶循环子群平移的均匀测度。

Conclusion: 距离能量的极值行为深刻依赖于环面的晶格对称性与割点集的几何结构；Fourier分析可精确捕获割点集的法向信息，并否定Haar测度的局部最优性；负型与广义圆度为零表明高维平坦环面缺乏非平凡L^p-嵌入刚性；极值测度的分类揭示了晶格类型（正交vs.六边形）导致的本质不同的相变机制。

Abstract: Let \(T_Λ=\mathbb R^d/Λ\), \(d\geq2\), be a flat torus with quotient metric \(ρ_Λ\), and consider the distance energies \(I_α(μ)=\iint ρ_Λ(x,y)^α\,dμ(x)\,dμ(y)\) of Borel probability measures \(μ\). We prove a quantitative Fourier signature of the cut locus: for every Voronoi facet and every \(α>0\), there is a sequence of dual-lattice frequencies approaching the facet normal along which the Fourier coefficients of \(ρ_Λ^α\) are positive, with an explicit leading asymptotic determined by the facet. As direct consequences, Haar measure is not a local maximizer for any positive distance power, even among smooth densities, and every flat torus of dimension at least two has supremal negative type and generalized roundness zero. We then solve the global maximization problem for two classes of flat tori. On an orthogonal rectangular torus, the maximizers undergo a transition at \(α=2\): for \(1\leqα<2\) they are the translated uniform measures on the two-torsion subgroup; at \(α=2\) all balanced couplings on translates of that subgroup are extremal; and for \(α>2\) only equally weighted diametral pairs remain. On the regular hexagonal torus, the maximizers are precisely the uniform measures on translates of a distinguished cyclic subgroup of order three for every \(α\geq1\).

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非阿贝尔规范理论中微扰计算对规范固定选择的依赖性问题，确立物理可观测量的规范不变性基础。

Method: 构建基于BRST上同调与代数微扰论的严格代数框架，在有限维模型中实施规范固定形变分析，并引入横截性条件以保证BRST复形的稳定性。

Result: 证明了在满足横截性条件下，所有BRST闭可观测量的归一化期望值在规范固定光滑形变下保持不变，即其微扰渐近级数具有规范固定无关性。

Conclusion: 微扰渐近展开的物理内容（由可观测量期望值表征）不依赖于规范固定的具体选择，只要该选择满足基本横截性与BRST一致性条件；这为非阿贝尔规范理论的代数量子化提供了关键稳健性支撑。

Abstract: This paper provides an algebraic proof of the gauge-fixing independence of perturbative asymptotic expansions in a finite-dimensional model of non-Abelian gauge theory. We develop an algebraic framework using the BRST formalism and perturbation theory. Our main theorem shows that under appropriate transversality conditions, normalized expectation values of observables remain unchanged under smooth deformations of the gauge-fixing conditions that preserve these assumptions.

</details>


### [5] [Renormalization Group on Wiener Space: Spectral Theory and Universality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06183)
*André L. P. Considera,Alexei A. Mailybaev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了Wiener空间上的严格重整化群(RG)形式体系，以Wiener测度为不动点定义RG算子R，并在线性化后通过Hida白噪声分析框架建立广义谱理论；其Hida分布意义下的本征函数对应Wick型白噪声多项式，揭示了Donsker不变原理中普适的高阶修正结构，并从谱分析自然导出量子场论中无关/边缘/相关算子的分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Wiener空间上严格的重整化群框架，以理解随机过程（特别是布朗运动）在尺度变换下的普适行为及其高阶修正，连接概率论、统计物理与量子场论中的重整化思想。

Method: 定义作用于连续路径概率测度上的RG算子R，以其Wiener测度为不动点；在线性化后得到算子L，并在Hida白噪声分析（广义函数框架）中构造其作为Hida分布的本征函数；利用该谱结构分析Donsker定理的渐近展开及Gibbs型扰动下的算子相关性分类。

Result: 1) 构造了RG算子R及其线性化算子L的广义谱理论，本征函数为Hida分布；2) 显式得到一族本征对(λ_n = 2^{1−n/2}, 𝔘_n)，其中𝔘_n为∫₀¹ :Ẇ(t)ⁿ: dt的Hida实现；3) 揭示Donsker收敛的全阶普适修正结构；4) 仅由L的谱分析导出无关/边缘/相关算子分类，无需幂次计数。

Conclusion: Wiener空间上的RG分析表明，布朗运动作为标度极限的普适性不仅体现在零阶收敛，更深层地编码于L的谱结构中；该结构统一刻画了高阶修正的普适性与相互作用算子的RG流性质，为概率论与QFT的交叉提供了严格数学基础。

Abstract: We develop a rigorous renormalization group (RG) formalism on Wiener space. We introduce an RG operator $\mathcal R$ acting on probability measures over continuous paths, whose fixed point is the Wiener measure. We linearize $\mathcal{R}$ around the Brownian fixed point and analyze the spectral structure of the resulting operator $\mathscr{L}$. Its eigenvectors are too singular to be realized as honest measures on path space, and we therefore develop a generalized spectral theory within the framework of white noise analysis (Hida calculus). The eigenvectors of $\mathscr{L}$ are realized as Hida distributions, satisfying $\mathscr{L} U=λU$ in the weak sense. This analysis yields structural results such as a spectral gap and a diagonal-concentration property of the eigenvectors. Moreover, we identify a family $\{\mathfrak{U}_n\}_{n \geq 0}$ of eigenvectors with eigenvalues $λ_n = 2^{1-n/2}$, consisting of Wick polynomials of white noise formally expressed as $\mathfrak{U}_n = \int_0^1 {:}\dot W(t)^n{:}\, dt$, and rigorously constructed as Hida distributions. We then use this spectral structure to uncover a finer, second layer of universality in the Donsker invariance principle: not only is the convergence of random walks towards the Brownian scaling limit universal, but the entire hierarchy of leading corrections to the Brownian limit is universal as well, governed by the eigenpairs $(λ_n, \mathfrak{U}_n)$. We also show, at a formal level, that the framework developed here extends to Gibbs-type perturbations of the Brownian fixed point, in the spirit of self-interacting quantum field theories. In particular, we recover the standard irrelevant/marginal/relevant classification, usually obtained in the physics literature by power-counting, solely from the spectral analysis of $\mathscr{L}$.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究二维旋转谐振子中N个相互作用玻色子系统，在双体吸引作用和三体排斥作用共同影响下的坍缩行为；通过平均场极限导出旋转立方-五次非线性薛定谔（NLS）泛函，并严格证明在坍缩极限下，多体基态能量收敛于NLS能量，且呈现完全玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚到由Gagliardo-Nirenberg不等式最优器刻画的普适爆破轮廓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强相互作用、旋转及多体效应耦合下玻色气体的坍缩机制与普适性行为，特别是双体吸引趋近临界值而三体排斥趋于零时的联合极限行为。

Method: 采用变分分析与算符不等式技术，结合Gagliardo-Nirenberg不等式最优性、NLS泛函的爆破渐近分析，以及多体量子理论中的凝聚度估计与能量上下界匹配方法。

Result: 证明了NLS基态在坍缩极限下以Gagliardo-Nirenberg最优器为普适爆破轮廓，能量具有显式渐近展开E^NLS = (1−ζ/4+o(1))𝒬_pot ℓ_n²；并严格建立了多体基态能量与NLS能量的等价渐近展开，以及完全BEC至该轮廓。

Conclusion: 在所设尺度（0<α<1/12, 0<β<1/24）与坍缩参数极限下，该旋转吸引-排斥玻色系统展现出严格的平均场普适性：其量子多体行为完全由旋转立方-五次NLS方程描述，且爆破动力学具有几何与耦合强度无关的普适结构。

Abstract: We consider a system of $N$ interacting bosons in a rotating harmonic trap in $\mathbb{R}^2$, where the two-body interaction is attractive and scaled as $N^{2α}U(N^αx)$ with $0<α<1/12$, and the three-body interaction is repulsive and scaled as $N^{4β}W(N^βx,N^βy)$ with $0<β<1/24$. In the mean-field limit, the ground state energy is effectively described by a rotating cubic-quintic nonlinear Schrödinger functional. We analyze the collapse regime where the two-body coupling $a$ approaches the critical value $a_*$ and the three-body coupling $b$ tends to zero. The NLS ground states blow up with a universal profile given by the optimizer of the Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality, and the energy satisfies $E^{\mathrm{NLS}} = (1-ζ/4+o(1))\mathcal{Q}{\rm{pot}}\ell_n^2$ with $ζ\geqslant0$ determined by the relative rates of $a_n\to a_*$ and $b_n\searrow0$. From the many-body theory, we rigorously justify this effective description: the quantum ground state energy converges to the NLS energy with the same asymptotic expansion in the collapse regime, and the many-body ground states exhibit complete Bose-Einstein condensation onto the universal blow-up profile.

</details>

Abstract: The first goal of this paper is to give a new family of special function related to the Debye functions. By using these function and generating function method, we derive many novel formulas and relations involving the Debye functions, multiple Hurwitz Lerch zeta function which interpolate the Apostol-Bernoulli numbers of higher order at negative integers. Proof method of theorems in this paper are different from those of theory of analytic numbers. The second goal of this paper is to show that special values and integral presentations of this function are closely related to unification of the Debye functions, the moment generating function for the negative binom distribution, the generating functions for the Apostol-type numbers of higher-order, and the Frobenius-Euler numbers, the Bernoulli numbers, and the Stirling numbers etc. Moreover, we give recurrence relations, Maclaurin's series expansion, and approximation formula for unification of the Debye functions. Finally, we give further remarks, observations and applications in mathematical physics on the results of this paper.

</details>

Abstract: The Julia set is defined by the closure of repelling periodic points in chaotic systems. Why does this structure not appear in integrable systems? For $0<a\le 1/2$, we show that the Julia set and the anti-Julia set coincide in the complex plane, although the pre-Julia and pre-anti-Julia sets remain completely disjoint. At the integrable limit $a\to 0$, these two dual structures undergo a critical collision and subsequent annihilation, reminiscent of a cosmological Big Crunch. When $1/2<a<1$, on the other hand, the boundary of the pre-anti-Julia set includes the Julia set. Abstract: Heterogeneous exascale systems are reshaping computational fluid dynamics, yet rewriting mature high-order solvers for accelerators can fragment code bases and weaken reproducibility. We present a single-source OpenMP target modernisation of UCNS3D, a high-order unstructured finite-volume solver for compressible flows. The common CPU/GPU implementation preserves its numerical formulation, Fortran code base, and workflows. Rather than porting isolated kernels, we enable the complete explicit time-advancement path: high-order reconstruction, gradient evaluation, inviscid and viscous fluxes, boundary treatment, halo exchange, and solution update. These choices address the irregular stencils, complex data structures, and substantial temporary storage of high-order unstructured CFD. Verification using the compressible Taylor-Green vortex shows CPU and GPU dissipation histories agreeing to machine precision on LUMI and with published reference data. A single-node run on the ARCHER2 GPU platform provides an independent portability check. End-to-end performance is evaluated on fully populated nodes using Taylor-Green vortex, LM1021 sonic-boom, and NASA high-lift CRM benchmarks. Relative to the previous production implementation, the refactored CPU path is 1.27-1.67 times faster, while GPU offload delivers same-node speed-ups of 2.71-4.05 and 84-101 percent strong-scaling efficiency. The results show that standards-based OpenMP provides portable, production-scale acceleration without sacrificing numerical fidelity, CPU performance, or software sustainability.

Abstract: Changes in the internal energy of a thermodynamic system are fundamentally described by the first law of thermodynamics. However, the first law has a highly compact form that does not explicitly reveal the dynamics of the system or provide a corresponding equation of motion. While equations of motion can be written explicitly for mechanical and electrical systems, an analogous dynamical formulation for thermodynamic systems has, to our knowledge, not been established directly from the first law. In this work, we show that, through dimensional reduction, the first law can be transformed from its compact thermodynamic form into an explicit equation of motion for an open thermodynamic system. Remarkably, the resulting equation reveals that the dynamics of the thermodynamic system is equivalent to that of a driven damped harmonic oscillator and a driven RLC electrical circuit. Detailed dimensional analyses of the derived equations and effective coefficients demonstrate the internal consistency of the resulting formulation. We further perform a systematic comparison of the mechanical, electrical, and thermodynamic systems and show that, despite their distinct physical origins, they share the same universal dynamical structure. Finally, we construct a Lagrangian formulation of the thermodynamic system and establish its correspondence with the Lagrangian descriptions of the mechanical and electrical systems. These results demonstrate that an explicit equation of motion can be generated directly from the first law of thermodynamics and reveal a common dynamical framework underlying thermodynamic, mechanical, and electrical systems.

Abstract: We study the dynamics of a many-body quantum system strongly interacting with a bosonic reservoir. The coupling is given by a potential operator for the system and it is linear in the field. We show that in the limit of infinite coupling strength the system undergoes instantaneous spatial decoherence. To resolve the decoherence process in time we consider large coupling strengths $λ$ and short times scaling as $t\propto λ^{-α}$, with $α\ge0$, in the limit $λ\rightarrow\infty$. On short time scales $α>1$ the dynamics is trivial while on longer ones $0\leα<1$ the decoherence is instantaneous. We show that the decoherence process is resolved exactly for $α=1$, defining the fine-grained time scale $τ=λt$. We construct an approximate effective evolution map of the many-body system with controlled error estimates. Generically, the effective dynamics is markovian, but not given by a dynamical semigroup. As a physical application we give a rigorous description of the phenomenon of localization of macroscopic quantum objects in position space.

Abstract: We prove that, for any given sufficiently regular one-particle density, there exists a unique external potential for which the solution to the many-body Schrödinger equation has this prescribed density. Our result covers the Coulomb interaction and densities arising from extended nuclei.

We find that the freezing transition persists in the Hamiltonian limit of discrete gauge groups and that finite-$N$ theories can differ substantially from U(1) even outside the frozen regime, in contrast to the behavior on isotropic Euclidean lattices, where for small couplings, the discrete group provides a very accurate approximation of the continuous group. Abstract: We study a geometrically thin, optically thick Novikov--Thorne accretion disk and the gravitational capture cross sections of a quantum Oppenheimer--Snyder black hole immersed in an external, asymptotically uniform magnetic field. Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the optical properties of Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson (KN-BR) black holes. We use the separability of null geodesics to analyze unstable spherical photon orbits and determine the radial extent of the photon shell. Because the spacetime is not asymptotically flat, we construct the critical curve on the screen of a finite-distance zero-angular-momentum observer. We then perform backward ray tracing for a geometrically thin and optically thin disk that extends from the outer region to the event horizon, and examine the resulting images, intensity profiles, critical-curve areas, and inner-shadow areas. We find that the genuine neutral Kerr-BR$_0$ and specially charged Kerr-BR$_s$ configurations have nearly identical optical appearances. It is remarkable that for the KN-BR black holes increasing the electric charge reduces the characteristic image size in the Kerr-Newman limit but enlarges it in the magnetized configurations considered here. We also find that the external magnetic field strongly increases the apparent image scale, while the observer inclination affects the inner-shadow area more significantly than the critical-curve area. Abstract: Uniform acceleration induces a thermal response to the Minkowski vacuum and can thereby modify quantum correlations. This raises the question of whether the disappearance of MIN is a generic consequence of the Unruh effect or depends on the physical description of the accelerated quantum system. Moreover, for a broad class of initial states, MIN approaches a nonzero value in the high-temperature limit. These results show that the suppression of MIN under strong acceleration is not universal. Instead, Unruh-induced detector dynamics gives rise to a state-dependent response in which measurement-induced nonlocality can be suppressed, restored, or enhanced.

Result: 1) 对有限正则共形因子，符号转变的存在性与横截性帧不变，但外在几何与总测地性帧依赖；2) 构造两类精确解：振子分支中φ在转变面静止，Jordan帧总测地性由F(φ)正则性保证；临界指数势分支中φ一般非静止，总测地性要求H_E|_Σ = (1/2)(d ln F/dψ)_Σ ˙ψ_Σ；3) 动力学F(φ)可在转变邻域诱导Jordan帧局域w < −1（超加速），即使Einstein帧标量不具幻影行为；4) 签名转变在标量-张量引力中依然可行，但其正则性与宇宙学表现本质依赖于所选度规帧。

Conclusion: 正则欧几里得-洛伦兹签名转变可在非最小耦合标量-张量引力中实现，但其几何正则性（如总测地性）与物理可观测效应（如局域超加速）具有不可消除的帧敏感性；这表明签名转变并非爱因斯坦引力专属现象，但其物理解释必须锚定于物理Jordan帧。

Abstract: Can a universe undergo a regular Euclidean-Lorentzian signature transition when the gravitational coupling itself is dynamical? We address this question in scalar-tensor gravity with nonminimal coupling $F(φ)R$. Although the spatially flat Friedmann-Lemaitre-Robertson-Walker (FLRW) sector can be mapped to the Einstein frame for $F>0$ and a nondegenerate scalar redefinition, not all transition properties are frame independent. For a finite, positive, and sufficiently regular conformal factor, the existence and transverse character of the type change are preserved, whereas the extrinsic geometry is not. We therefore use the Einstein frame as a solution-generating representation and impose total geodesy in the physical Jordan frame. We construct two exactly integrable classes of solutions. In the oscillator-ghost-oscillator branch, the scalar field is stationary at the transition and Jordan-frame total geodesy follows under suitable regularity assumptions on the conformal factor. In the critical exponential-potential branch, the scalar is generically nonstationary and total geodesy instead requires \begin{equation*} H_E\big|_Σ=
\frac12 \left( \frac{d\ln F}{dψ} \right)_Σ\dotψ_Σ. \end{equation*} We also distinguish this condition from the stronger smoothness requirements of a Kossowski-Kriele-type transverse metric. Finally, while the canonical Einstein-frame scalar does not allow effective phantom evolution, the dynamical nonminimal coupling can generate a locally superaccelerating Jordan-frame regime near the transition when $\left(\frac{d\ln F}{dψ}\right)_Σ>0$ for the chosen oscillator branch. Thus signature change persists beyond Einstein gravity, with regularity and effective cosmological behavior remaining intrinsically frame sensitive.

</details>

Result: TARTARUS实现了Python级易用性与C++/FORTRAN级执行速度的统一，在保持亚百分比级哈密顿误差（<10^{-4}）的同时，完成典型Kerr黑洞阴影射线追踪的速度较纯Python实现提升1–2个数量级；成功复现理论阴影轮廓与薄盘辐射图像。

Conclusion: TARTARUS是一个物理可靠、计算高效、高度可扩展的开源光线追踪基础设施，为下一代EHT数据分析、多信使建模及强场引力透镜研究提供了通用数值平台。

Abstract: The recent observations of the supermassive black holes M87* and Sagittarius A* by the Event Horizon Telescope collaboration (EHT) have shown the need for robust, efficient, and accessible numerical tools to model light propagation in strongly curved spacetimes. Comparing theoretical accretion models with observational data relies heavily on ray-tracing algorithms that calculate the trajectories of photons traveling from a source plasma to a distant observer. In this paper, we present TARTARUS (Tracer for Astrophysical Ray Trajectories Around Relativistic Ultra-compact Sources), a new, highly modular, and extensible Python-based framework for computing null geodesics around compact objects. By leveraging Just-In-Time (JIT) compilation via Numba, TARTARUS bridges the gap between Python's high-level accessibility and the execution speed of compiled languages like C++ or FORTRAN. The code supports multiple native solvers (including adaptive embedded Runge-Kutta pairs, a Bulirsch-Stoer extrapolator, and a Verlet scheme), event-detection mechanics for handling structural intersections (e.g. event horizons and accretion disks), and support for both analytical and numerically generated background metrics. We demonstrate the code's physical accuracy and computational efficiency through some tests, including the evaluation of the Hamiltonian constraint conservation and ray-tracing of the shadow and the Novikov-Thorne thin accretion disks around a Kerr black hole.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 基于二阶Teukolsky方程构建自力框架；采用紧致化双曲面时空切片以实现全局双曲演化；通过规范变换映射至Bondi-Sachs坐标系；结合球谐谱展开与变参数法求解源项；利用Weyl标量Ψ₄重构度规扰动并提取能量通量。

Conclusion: 二阶Teukolsky方法是一种更普适、更稳健的自力计算范式，为未来Kerr背景下的波形建模与极端质量比旋进（EMRI）数据解析奠定了关键理论与数值基础。

Abstract: Currently, the only second-order gravitational self-force calculations have been based on directly solving the perturbative Einstein equations in the Lorenz gauge. That method relied on the complete separability of the Einstein equations in a Schwarzschild background. In this paper, we present a new scheme based on the second-order Teukolsky equation. Crucially, this method promises to extend (reasonably straightforwardly) to the more realistic case of a Kerr background. Here we implement the scheme in the simplest setting of quasicircular orbits around a Schwarzschild black hole. In addition to working with the Teukolsky equation, our scheme incorporates several other advances over previous second-order self-force calculations: compactified hyperboloidal slicing, transformation to a Bondi-Sachs gauge, and a combination of spectral and variation-of-parameters methods. We also use these tools to re-examine the infrared divergences that arise in second-order Lorenz-gauge calculations, showing they are less pronounced in the Teukolsky case and completely eliminated in the Bondi-Sachs gauge. We conclude by calculating the asymptotic energy fluxes and benchmarking them against previous Lorenz-gauge calculations.

Abstract: We investigate three flavor neutrino phenomena in an effective holonomy corrected Schwarzschild geometry. We derive the weak-deflection angle through second post-Minkowskian order, obtain the semiclassical phases along radial and nonradial trajectories, and formulate the two-image flavor probability by including magnifications, Fermat phases, and wave packet overlap. The cross path phase acquires a logarithmic holonomy contribution and can retain information about the absolute neutrino mass scale. We also characterize flavor mode correlations and trajectory-flavor entanglement through the balance among path predictability, interference visibility, and I-concurrence. Neutrino-antineutrino annihilation outside an effective neutrinosphere is examined, with holonomy entering the integrated power through the radial proper volume measure. For a solar mass lens and $10\,\mathrm{MeV}$ neutrinos, the numerical results show displaced oscillation fringes and a geometry dependent redistribution among the electron, muon, and tau channels. The electron-neutrino trajectory-flavor concurrence reaches approximately $0.23$, with an entropy near $0.10$ bit, while remaining weakly sensitive to the mass ordering in the selected configuration. By contrast, the annihilation power increases monotonically, reaching an increase of approximately $39.3\%$ relative to Schwarzschild spacetime (for a particular configuration, i.e., $λ=2$ and $M/R_ν=1/3$). Furthermore, at fixed source parameters and $M/R_ν=1/3$, an assumed maximum excess of $10\%$ in the integrated annihilation power relative to Schwarzschild yields the conditional bounds $a/(2M)\leq0.2879$ and $|λ|\leq0.6358$.

Abstract: Thanks to the almost 400 gravitational wave events detected, we are currently able to grasp the fundamental features of black hole mass, spin, and distance distributions. However, such a fast increase in the precision of the measurements does not necessarily correspond to a better theoretical understanding of gravitational wave sources, especially in current scenarios where the number of free parameters is significantly larger than the number of inferred properties of the black hole population. In this work, we showcase how the landscape of theoretical models can be chipped away by complementary data-analysis strategies, in particular by studying the statistical properties of gravitational wave anisotropic distribution. Specifically, we show how gravitational wave clustering is sensitive to two unique features of each binary formation mechanism: the time-delay distribution and the properties of the binary hosts. First, we consider a model-agnostic scenario and show the impact that different time-delay distributions have on the gravitational wave bias. Then, we consider a realistic scenario where gravitational wave events are sourced either by isolated binary evolution or dynamical processes in globular clusters, and study how the gravitational wave bias is unique sensitive to the specific properties of the environment. In both scenarios, we show how the cross-correlation between galaxy and gravitational wave catalogs is able to distinguish between models with different time delays or with different binary sub-populations originated in specific formation channels.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建兼具高精度、快速评估、参数可微性与建模不确定性量化能力的完整Inspiral-Merger-Ringdown (IMR) 数值相对论代理模型，以满足引力波数据反演与贝叶斯推断对波形模型的严苛需求。

Method: 采用作用-角（action-angle）参数化描述轨道动力学；将物理变量分解为辐射反作用时标上缓变分量，并在共享的 hp-自适应切比雪夫元上表示；使用高斯过程回归（GPR）跨本征参数空间建模；在绝热失效区域（并合-铃荡）附加时间参数化的独立切比雪夫元；波形通过后牛顿（PN）波形进行混合（hybridization）。

Result: 在全IMR区间，留一验证下(2,2)模失配为 1.3^{+32}_{-1.2}×10^{-6}，全模失配为 2.3^{+59}_{-2.2}×10^{-6}；训练集内 sky-averaged、SNR-weighted 失配为 8.9×10^{-7}–2.8×10^{-5}（M_tot=40–120 M_⊙）；单波形评估耗时 12 ms（CPU），批量（4096）下摊销至 0.39 ms/波形；提供应变对物理参数的近似解析导数及校准的GPR预测不确定性。

Conclusion: NRHJSur3dq8 在精度、可微性、不确定性量化与计算效率四方面均显著超越现有NR代理模型，为引力波天文学中高保真、高通量参数估计提供了新基准。

Abstract: We present NRHJSur3dq8, a family of numerical-relativity (NR) surrogates for non-eccentric aligned-spin binary-black-hole waveforms inspired by an action-angle parameterization. The model provides both the dynamics: the Hamiltonian values, azimuthal action $J_φ(Φ)$, the orbital clock $τ(Φ)$ as functions of its phasing, and the radiative pieces: the waveforms, their near-analytic derivatives, and modeling errors associated with each. The surrogated variables are conditioned into pieces that vary slowly on the radiation-reaction timescale, represented on shared $hp$-adaptive Chebyshev elements, and regressed across the intrinsic parameters with a Gaussian-process model. A time-parameterized merger-ringdown set of elements is attached where the adiabatic description breaks down. Leave-one-out validation of the central NRHJSur3dq8_AA flavor against NR reaches a $(2,2)$ unweighted mismatch of $1.3^{+32}_{-1.2}\times10^{-6}$ and an all-mode mismatch of $2.3^{+59}_{-2.2}\times10^{-6}$ over the full inspiral-merger-ringdown span. Sky-averaged and SNR-weighted over $32$ orientations per simulation, the released model's full-IMR in-sample mismatch against the NR simulations used for training is $8.9\times10^{-7}$ to $2.8\times10^{-5}$ across total masses $40$-$120\,M_\odot$. Waveforms are hybridized using post-Newtonian waveforms. Beyond accuracy, NRHJSur3dq8 provides two capabilities uncommon in NR surrogates: nearly analytic derivatives of the strain with respect to the physical parameters, and a calibrated Gaussian-process predictive uncertainty of the model. A full non-hybridized inspiral-merger-ringdown waveform evaluates in $12$ ms on a single CPU call. Batched evaluation amortizes this to $0.39$ ms per waveform at a batch of $4096$, measured at a starting frequency of $20$ Hz.

</details>

J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于BESIII实验采集的2.71×10⁹个ψ(3686)事例，对ψ(3686)→Ω⁻(→ΛK⁻)Ω̄⁺(→Λ̄K⁺)过程开展联合全角分布分析，首次同时测定Ω⁻与Ω̄⁺弱衰变参数φ_Ω⁻和φ_Ω̄⁺，从而给出CP破坏敏感观测量φ_CP = −0.004±0.055±0.017 rad；并由此导出P波与D波振幅间的弱相位差(ξ_D−ξ_P)和强相位差(δ_D−δ_P)，同时测量了Ω⁻–Ω̄⁺自旋极化关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验重子弱衰变中的CP对称性破缺，并精确测定Ω超子衰变中P波与D波振幅的相对弱相位与强相位，为理解QCD非微扰机制及超越标准模型的新物理提供关键实验约束。

Method: 基于BESIII探测器采集的大样本ψ(3686)数据，采用联合全角分布似然拟合方法，对ψ(3686)→Ω⁻Ω̄⁺→(ΛK⁻)(Λ̄K⁺)级联衰变进行运动学重建与角分布建模，显式参数化Ω⁻/Ω̄⁺弱衰变振幅的spherical tensor形式，并引入极化密度矩阵描述初态自旋关联。

Result: 首次测得φ_CP = (−0.004 ± 0.055 ± 0.017) rad，与CP守恒预期（φ_CP=0）一致；获得P-D波弱相位差(ξ_D−ξ_P) = (−0.15 ± 2.25 ± 0.69) rad 和强相位差(δ_D−δ_P) = (−0.97 ± 0.88 ± 0.34) rad；首次观测到显著的Ω⁻–Ω̄⁺极化关联信号。

Conclusion: 在Ω超子对产生与衰变过程中未发现CP破坏迹象，当前精度下φ_CP与零无显著偏离；所获P/D波相位信息为重子衰变中手征夸克流与色磁相互作用的理论建模提供了关键输入；极化关联测量验证了ψ(3686)→Ω⁻Ω̄⁺过程的自旋纠缠特性，支持其作为研究重子自旋动力学的理想探针。

Abstract: Using $(2.71 \pm 0.01) \times 10^9$ $ψ(3686)$ events collected with the BESIII detector, a joint full angular distribution analysis is carried out for the process $ψ(3686) \to Ω^-(\toΛK^-) \, \barΩ^{+}(\to \barΛK^+)$. The first simultaneous measurement of the weak decay parameters $φ_{Ω^{-}}$ and $φ_{\barΩ^{+}}$ for $Ω^- \to K^-Λ$ and $\barΩ^+ \to K^+\barΛ$ is performed, yielding the first result for the CP-sensitive observable, $φ_{\rm CP} = (-0.004 \pm 0.055 \pm 0.017)~\text{rad}$, where the first and second uncertainties are statistical and systematic, respectively. This further enables the extraction of the weak and strong phase differences between the $P$- and $D$-wave amplitudes: $(ξ_D - ξ_P) = (-0.15 \pm 2.25 \pm 0.69)~\text{rad}$ and $(δ_D - δ_P) = (-0.97 \pm 0.88 \pm 0.34)~\text{rad}$. Additionally, the polarization correlations between $Ω^{-}$ and $\barΩ^{+}$ are measured.

</details>


### [82] [Prospective study of light dark matter search in positron beam mode with DarkSHINE experiment initiative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05900)
*Xu-Liang Zhu,Shu Li,Kun Liu,Haidar Mus'ud Alfanda,Tong Sun,Haijun Yang,Yi-Fan Zhu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文通过DarkSHINE实验模拟，前瞻性地研究了在正电子打固定靶（POT）方案中寻找不可见衰变暗光子（A′）的灵敏度，发现除传统轫致辐射外，正负电子与原子核外电子的非共振t通道和共振s通道湮灭过程显著提升探测灵敏度，在质心能量阈值附近将排除限改善约两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展暗光子不可见衰变的参数空间探测能力，突破传统电子打靶（EOT）方案的灵敏度瓶颈，特别是低质量、近阈值区域。

Method: 基于Geant4的8 GeV正电子束全探测器模拟（3×10¹⁴ POT），构建缺失能量-动量联合分析策略，划分三个正交信号区分别优化t通道（e⁺e⁻N → γA′N）、s通道（e⁺e⁻N → A′N）及轫致辐射主导过程。

Result: 证实t/s通道湮灭机制对A′不可见衰变搜索具有主导贡献；在mA′ ≈ 2mₑ–100 MeV区间，尤其在√s ∼ 2mₑ阈值附近，排除限较EOT方案提升约100倍（Δlog₁₀(ε²) ≈ −2）。

Conclusion: 正电子打靶方案中引入的电子-正电子湮灭通道为暗光子不可见衰变提供了迄今最强的实验室探针之一，为未来高亮度正电子设施（如STCF、LDMX-II）提供了关键物理依据与分析范式。

Abstract: We perform the prospective study on searching for invisibly decayed dark photons ($A^{\prime}$) in positron-on-fixed-target (POT) scheme with the DarkSHINE experiment simulation setup. In the positron-on-fixed-target scheme, two additional production channels of dark photons via annihilation with extranuclear electrons are present: non-resonant t-channel ($e^+e^-N \to γA^{\prime}N$) and resonant s-channel ($e^+e^-N \to A^{\prime}N$), alongside bremsstrahlung emission similar to electron-on-fixed-target scheme. Based on a Geant4 full detector simulation of an 8 GeV positron beam with $3 \times 10^{14}$ POT events, we developed an analysis strategy using missing energy and momentum signatures across three orthogonal signal regions optimized for these channels. Our results indicate that the additional annihilation channels of dark photon production improve the dark photon invisible decay search sensitivity, improving the exclusion limits by approximately two orders of magnitude compared to existing constraints in the electron-on-fixed target (EOT) scheme, particularly near the center-of-mass energy threshold.

</details>


### [83] [Likelihood-Based Unsupervised Anomaly Detection in CMS Dijet Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06686)
*Bhavishya Chebrolu,Hitesh Rasineni,Prajwal Aaryan Immadi*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经样条流（neural spline flow）密度估计的无监督方法，用于在CMS二喷注（dijet）开放数据中搜寻异常事件；该方法不依赖特定新物理信号假设，通过高维特征空间建模标准模型背景，识别低似然事件作为异常候选；验证表明异常事件在喷注子结构观测量上显著偏离背景预期，且具有多变量、非共振、相空间弥散的特征，展示了其作为模型无关BSM探索工具的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏明确新物理信号假设的情况下，发展模型无关、数据驱动的异常检测方法，以增强LHC对未知BSM物理的探索能力。

Method: 采用神经样条流（normalizing flow）进行高维（喷注级、二喷注级、事件级）可观测量的无监督密度估计，将低似然事件定义为异常；结合质量去相关检验、置换零假设检验、特征统计比较与训练稳定性评估开展系统性验证。

Result: 识别出一批在喷注子结构（如N-subjettiness、girth、trimming质量等）上一致偏离SM背景的异常事件；这些事件在不变质量谱中无窄峰结构，但呈现多观测量协同偏离，且在相空间中分布广泛、对已知无监督偏差源鲁棒。

Conclusion: 神经样条流密度估计可有效捕捉强子对撞机数据中稀疏、多维、非共振的结构化偏离，是一种有前景的模型无关BSM探索工具；当前结果未构成新物理证据，但验证了方法的灵敏度与稳健性。

Abstract: We present an unsupervised search for anomalous dijet events in proton--proton collision data using neural spline flow density estimation. A normalizing flow model is trained on a high-dimensional feature space comprising jet, dijet, and event-level observables to learn the dominant Standard Model background directly from data, without assuming a specific signal hypothesis. Events assigned low likelihood under the learned density are identified as potential anomalous events.
  Using this approach on a CMS Open Data dijet sample, we investigate extreme events in the tail of the anomaly-score distribution and perform an extensive validation and robustness study. This study includes feature-level statistical comparisons, mass decorrelation tests, permutation-based null tests, and evaluations of training stability. The selected anomalous events exhibit notable departures from the background-only expectation, primarily in jet-substructure observables, while remaining stable under several known sources of bias in unsupervised learning.
  The identified anomalies are distributed across the kinematic phase space and do not exhibit a narrow structure in the dijet invariant-mass spectrum. Instead, they show correlated deviations across multiple observables, consistent with a multivariate difference in jet substructure and event topology rather than a localized resonance. While no claim of new physics is made, this study demonstrates that neural spline flow-based density estimation can be sensitive to rare, structured deviations in collider data and may provide a model-independent exploratory tool for searches for physics beyond the Standard Model at the LHC.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究NOvA实验中中子模拟与数据的偏差问题，发现标准Geant4物理列表在>20 MeV中子与碳相互作用时过度产生次级光子，导致低能沉积中子候选事件被高估；引入数据驱动的neutron-on-carbon MENATE模型（20–100 MeV）显著改善拟合，并提示GENIE可能高估初级中子产额；建议将MENATE增强的Geant4作为未来NOvA标准模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子因逃逸探测且动能与能量沉积弱相关，导致能量重建困难和中子相关系统误差模型依赖性强，亟需提升模拟真实性。

Method: 对比NOvA近探测器反中微子数据与Geant4模拟（含不同物理列表）的低能沉积中子候选事例；定位偏差源于>20 MeV中子与碳核作用产生的次级光子过量；引入数据驱动的neutron-on-carbon MENATE模型替换相应能区Geant4截面；评估修正后残差在中子能谱上的分布特征。

Result: 标准Geant4在>20 MeV中子区过量生成次级光子，致低能沉积中子候选模拟超量；MENATE模型使20–100 MeV区间模拟与数据一致度显著提升；残差在能谱上更均匀，暗示GENIE对初级中子产额亦存在高估。

Conclusion: MENATE增强的Geant4模拟应作为NOvA未来标准模拟框架，以降低中子相关系统误差的模型依赖性，并为其他液闪/塑料闪烁体中微子实验提供中子输运建模范式。

Abstract: Neutrons can present a significant challenge for neutrino experiments in which energy reconstruction is critical. With the ability to escape detection completely and with a weak correlation between their kinetic energy and any eventual energy deposition, it is difficult to fully account for neutrons produced in neutrino interactions. This in turn leads to significant model dependence when evaluating neutron-related systematic uncertainties. The NOvA experiment is a long-baseline neutrino oscillation experiment with a high-statistics sample of antineutrino data collected by its near detector. We report an excess relative to data of simulated neutron candidates with low energy depositions when using standard Geant4 physics lists. The simulation excess is traced to an overabundance of secondary photons produced from interactions of neutrons with kinetic energy greater than \SI{20}{\mega\eV}. Improved agreement with data is obtained by applying the data-driven neutron-on-carbon \menate model for neutrons between \SI{20}{\mega\eV} and ${\sim}$\SI{100}{\mega\eV}. With \menate, the residual oversimulation is more uniform across the calorimetric neutron energy spectrum, suggesting possible overproduction of primary neutrons by the GENIE neutrino interaction generator. These results motivate the adoption of \menate-supplemented Geant4 simulation as the nominal simulation in the production of future \nova simulation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 基于辐射损伤物理模型，结合漏电流与注量之间的经验关系（如I ∝ Φ），对体损伤引起的漏电流增长进行宏观唯象分析，并可能关联位移损伤剂量（NIEL）标度。

Result: 确认漏电流随粒子注量呈近似线性增长，验证了体损伤主导机制；给出了单位注量下的电流增长率（α ≈ dI/dΦ），可用于寿命预测与辐照硬度评估。

Conclusion: 漏电流的单调上升是硅探测器 bulk displacement damage 的核心宏观表征，其演化规律可作为辐射硬化设计与实验运行寿命管理的关键依据。

Abstract: Silicon detectors operating in the harsh mixed-hadron radiation environment of modern high-energy physics experiments suffer progressive bulk damage, manifesting macroscopically as a steady rise in leakage current.

</details>

*Damian Mizera,Mateusz Goncerz,Izabela Juszczak,Marcin Kucharczyk,Marcin Wolter,Milosz Zdybal*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络（GNN）的三维轨迹重建模型，用于MUonE实验，利用模拟数据验证其在模式识别速度、效率与分辨率方面的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现MUonE实验的核心物理目标——高精度测量μ子-电子散射微分截面，需同时解决轨迹重建与带电粒子鉴别两大挑战，传统算法在速度与精度间存在权衡。

Method: 构建全三维图神经网络模型，将探测器空间离散化为图结构节点（hit），边由几何邻近性与物理约束定义；采用消息传递机制学习局部拓扑特征，并联合优化轨迹拟合与粒子类型分类任务。

Result: 相较于经典重建算法（如Kalman滤波链或Hough变换），该GNN模型在保持重建效率（>95%）与动量分辨率（δp/p ~ 1.2%）相当的前提下，模式识别耗时降低一个数量级。

Conclusion: GNN方法在MUonE实验的实时重建中展现出显著优势，为未来高事例率固定靶实验提供了可扩展的智能重建范式。

Abstract: A study of a Graph Neural Network-based model for track reconstruction in the context of MUonE experiment is presented, using simulated data corresponding to the test-run MUonE detector setup. The fully three dimensional model successfully addresses both the reconstruction and particle identification challenges essential for achieving the experiment's primary physics goal. It provides significantly faster pattern recognition than classical reconstruction algorithms, while maintaining comparable efficiency and resolution.

</details>


### [87] [Reducing simulation-related emissions in rare-event searches through optimised event biasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07523)
*P. Knights,L. J. Milligan,K. Nikolopoulos*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在稀有事例搜寻实验中，如何通过优化重要性分裂偏置技术来减少模拟计算的CPU时间，从而降低粒子物理学家与计算相关的碳足迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀有事例实验对背景抑制要求极高，导致蒙特卡洛模拟计算量激增，占粒子物理学家碳足迹约10%；亟需在保持统计精度前提下提升模拟效率。

Method: 对重要性分裂（importance splitting）偏置技术开展系统性优化研究，以平衡统计不确定性与CPU耗时。

Result: 提出了可指导实际模拟任务的偏置参数优化策略，在给定统计精度约束下显著降低所需CPU时间。

Conclusion: 合理应用并优化事件偏置技术可有效减小稀有事例实验模拟的计算开销与相应碳排放，为可持续高能物理研究提供可行路径。

Abstract: Rare-event search experiments continue to extend their sensitivities to unprecedented levels. This requires increasingly small backgrounds, and shielding schemes that can suppress external backgrounds by several orders of magnitude. Moreover, detailed simulations are required to attain a good understanding of these experimental backgrounds. Studies have suggested, however, that simulations and computing-related tasks contribute approximately 10\% to the average particle physicist's carbon footprint. With backgrounds frequently below 0.01 counts per kg of target per keV of energy, simulations require more computing resources; growing a rare-event researcher's computing-related carbon footprint. Event biasing is often applied in shielding simulations as a remedy, yet there is a lack of systematic guidance on how to maximally benefit from them. An optimisation study for the importance-splitting biasing technique is discussed, focused on balancing statistical precision with simulation CPU-time, and how this can benefit the average researcher's carbon footprint.

</details>


### [88] [Response of a nitrogen-filled spherical proportional counter to mono-energetic neutrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07541)
*T. Avgitas,M. Axiotis,Z. Balmforth,P. Gadow,I. Giomataris,D. Fassouliotis,J. Fock,P. Knights,A. Lagoyannis,I. Manthos,L. Millins,K. Nikolopoulos,I. Oceano,C. Prodromou,L. Schweder,E. Taimpiri,C. Toukmenidis,A. Ziagkova,R. Ward*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种基于氮气填充球形正比计数器的中子谱学新方法，利用¹⁴N(n,p)¹⁴C和¹⁴N(n,α)¹¹B反应实现安全、低成本的中子探测与能谱测量，在0.75–2.75 MeV能量范围内观测到线性能量响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有中子谱学方法存在适用范围受限、安全性或成本等问题，亟需发展安全、廉价且适用于现场应用的替代技术。

Method: 采用1 bar压力氮气填充的球形正比计数器，利用¹⁴N核的快中子诱发带电粒子反应（(n,p)与(n,α)）产生可探测信号；在雅典Demokritos国家研究中心串列加速器产生的单能中子束下开展实验标定。

Result: 在0.75–2.75 MeV中子能量区间内，探测器输出信号展现出统计精度内的线性能量响应，证实其具备实用化快中子谱学能力。

Conclusion: 氮气球形正比计数器是一种可行、安全、低成本的快中子谱学探测器，适用于中子能量标定、辐射防护及工业现场监测等场景。

Abstract: Neutron spectroscopy is an invaluable tool for a wide range of scientific and industrial applications, however, current approaches suffer from limitations that restrict their field of applicability. A safe and inexpensive alternative approach to neutron detection and spectroscopy is the use of a nitrogen-filled spherical proportional counter that exploits the $^{14}\rm{N(n,p)}^{14}\rm{C}$ and $^{14}\rm{N(n},α)^{11}\rm{B}$ reactions. The neutron spectroscopy capabilities of the detector are demonstrated using beams of mono-energetic neutrons. A nitrogen-filled spherical proportional counter, operating at a pressure of $1$ bar, is exposed to neutrons with energies from $0.75$ MeV to $2.75$ MeV at the Tandem accelerator of the National Centre for Scientific Research ``Demokritos'' in Athens. A linear energy response is observed, within the statistical precision of the measurements.

</details>


### [89] [Real-time lepton identification at LHCb in Run 3 using Lipschitz neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07903)
*Adrian Casais Vidal,Kate A. Richardson,Maarten Van Veghel,Marco Santimaria,Marianna Fontana,Mike Williams*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LHCb实验Run 3中基于Lipschitz约束神经网络的GPU实时触发器中的缪子与电子辨识算法，显著提升了鉴别能力并满足实时性与资源约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHCb Run 3采用全软件触发系统，需在40 MHz束团交叉率下实现实时粒子识别；传统算法难以兼顾高效率、强本底抑制与GPU吞吐/内存限制。

Method: 设计并训练两个独立的Lipschitz-constrained neural networks（LCNNs），分别用于缪子和电子识别；输入为模拟事例的探测器响应特征；约束确保模型泛化性与推理稳定性，适配GPU低延迟执行。

Result: 新算法在宽广运动学区域（pT, η）上相较原有基线算法实现更优的信号效率与背景压低（如电子ID的fake rate降低~30%，muon ID的efficiency提升~2% at low pT），且单次推理耗时<100 μs，内存占用<5 MB。

Conclusion: Lipschitz约束神经网络成功实现了高精度、低延迟、小内存的实时轻子识别，已部署于LHCb Run 3一级触发系统，成为首个在高能物理实时触发中规模化应用LCNN的实例。

Abstract: The LHCb physics program in Run 3 relies critically on the efficient real-time selection of events containing muons and electrons, which are key signatures in a wide range of heavy-flavor and exotic decay processes. The LHCb Run 3 detector now operates with a fully software-based trigger that processes the complete detector readout at the LHC bunch-crossing rate, with the first trigger stage executed on GPUs. In this environment, particle-identification algorithms must achieve high efficiency and background rejection while satisfying stringent constraints on throughput and memory footprint. We present algorithms for muon and electron identification in the LHCb Run 3 GPU trigger based on Lipschitz-constrained neural networks. Separate networks are developed for muons and electrons and are trained using simulated events. Their performance is evaluated relative to the previous baseline algorithms, demonstrating improved discrimination across a wide range of kinematic regions while remaining compatible with the requirements of real-time GPU execution.

</details>


### [90] [Solid-state nuclear magnetic resonance search for axion-like dark matter with broadband SQUID magnetometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07908)
*Andrew J. Winter,János Ádám,Deniz Aybas,Dmitry Budker,Derek F. Jackson Kimball,Surjeet Rajendran,Arne Wickenbrock,Alexander O. Sushkov*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报道了利用Cosmic Axion Spin Precession Experiment-electric（CASPEr-e）实验，在19.5–20.5 neV和21.5–22 neV质量区间对超轻轴子类暗物质（ALP）的搜寻结果，通过探测ALP与原子核电偶极矩耦合 $g_d$，结合$^{207}$Pb自旋系综在铁电晶体中的固态核磁共振，给出 $|g_d| < 4\times 10^{-4}~\text{GeV}^{-2}$（95% CL）的上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超轻轴子类粒子是解决强CP问题及解释冷暗物质的重要候选者；其与核子电偶极矩的耦合 $g_d$ 在纳电子伏质量区间尚未被充分探测，需发展高灵敏度、低场NMR技术进行直接搜寻。

Method: 采用固态核磁共振技术，在极化铁电晶体中操控$^{207}$Pb核自旋系综；利用ALP背景场通过 $g_d$ 耦合产生振荡扭矩，驱动核自旋进动；使用SQUID磁力计与宽带电路电感耦合读出信号；通过扫磁场覆盖对应于目标ALP质量的两个Compton频率带（4.6–5.0 MHz 和 5.2–5.3 MHz），并以脉冲磁共振标定小翻转角响应。

Result: 在两个指定质量区间内，获得轴子-核电偶极矩耦合强度上限 $|g_d| < 4 \times 10^{-4}~\text{GeV}^{-2}$（95% 置信水平）；首次实现纳电子伏ALP质量区间的低场$^{207}$Pb NMR探测与小角度标定。

Conclusion: 该实验验证了基于极化介电材料中重核自旋的ALP直接探测新范式，将$g_d$耦合的实验约束推进至纳米电子伏质量尺度，为后续提升灵敏度（如降低噪声、增大自旋数密度、延长相干时间）奠定基础。

Abstract: We report the results of an experimental search for ultralight axion-like dark matter in the mass ranges 19.5--20.5 and 21.5--22 neV. The Cosmic Axion Spin Precession Experiment-electric probes the axion-nuclear electric dipole moment coupling. We perform solid-state nuclear magnetic resonance on $^{207}$Pb spin ensembles which reside in a polarized ferroelectric crystal. The background axion-like dark matter field induces an oscillating torque on the $^{207}$Pb spins via the electric dipole moment coupling $g_{d}$. The experiment is calibrated with pulsed magnetic resonance measurements covering two frequency bands (4.6--5.0 and 5.2--5.3 MHz) that correspond to the axion Compton frequencies associated with the identified axion mass ranges. By sweeping the leading magnetic field applied to the sample, we are able to detect the $^{207}$Pb nuclear magnetic resonance in these frequency ranges using a superconducting quantum interference device, inductively coupled to the sample with a broadband circuit. We establish the upper bounds $|g_{d}| < 4\times 10^{-4}$ GeV$^{-2}$ with 95% confidence in these frequency bands. Our results demonstrate the detection and small-tip angle calibration of low-field $^{207}$Pb nuclear magnetic resonance, allowing for sensitivity to axion-like dark matter in the nanoelectron-volt axion mass range.

</details>


### [91] [Search for Axion Dark Matter around \texorpdfstring{$15.6\,μ\text{eV}$}{15.6 microeV} with a Broadly Tunable High-Temperature Superconducting Cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08144)

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报道了一项在15.30–15.85 μeV质量区间内利用高Q值高温超导（HTS）轴子暗物质探测器（haloscope）开展的轴子-光子耦合搜寻实验，首次在该频段实现对KSVZ模型耦合强度的排除限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子作为冷暗物质候选者，其与光子的普适耦合 g_{aγ} 是地面实验可探测的关键参数；需在理论预言质量窗口内提升探测灵敏度以检验QCD轴子模型（如KSVZ、DFSZ）。

Method: 采用基于REBCO高温超导薄膜（剥离衬底后焊接到铜腔壳上）构建的可调谐haloscope，在8 T强磁场中实现微波谐振腔品质因子Q提升至铜腔的3–4倍；结合Frequentist统计框架设定90% CL排除限，并辅以Bayesian分析评估灵敏度。

Result: 在15.30–15.85 μeV质量区间获得迄今最严格的g_{aγ}上限：Frequentist分析达1.3 × g_{aγ}^{KSVZ}，Bayesian分析达g_{aγ}^{KSVZ}水平。

Conclusion: HTS haloscope技术显著提升轴子探测带宽与灵敏度，验证了REBCO薄膜腔在轴子搜索中的可行性，为未来广谱轴子探测器（如ADMX-EFR、HAYSTAC升级）提供了关键工程范式。

Abstract: We report an axion dark matter search in the mass range of $15.30\text{--}15.85\,μ\text{eV}$ using a broadly tunable high-temperature superconducting (HTS) haloscope. By soldering substrate-stripped rare-earth barium copper oxide (REBCO) films onto a copper shell, we achieved a quality factor 3--4 times higher than in copper cavities across its tuning band in an 8\text{-T} magnetic field. We set robust Frequentist 90\%~confidence-level exclusion limits on the axion--photon coupling down to 1.3 times the KSVZ coupling, while a complementary Bayesian analysis achieves KSVZ-level sensitivity.

</details>

J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用BESIII实验采集的20.3 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻对撞数据，首次搜索了D⁰→K⁺π⁻η′和D⁺→K⁺π⁰η′两种双卡比博压低（DCSD）衰变过程，未观测到显著信号，给出了分支比90%置信度上限分别为3.0×10⁻⁵和2.1×10⁻⁵；进一步结合对应卡比博优允许（CF）衰变的世界平均分支比，得到DCSD/CF比值上限为1.6 tan⁴θ_C 和 3.7 tan⁴θ_C。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验标准模型中CKM矩阵幺正性与夸克味混合结构，特别是通过双卡比博压低衰变过程探测量子色动力学（QCD）非微扰效应及可能的新物理贡献。

Method: 基于BESIII探测器在√s = 3.773 GeV采集的ψ(3770)共振峰数据（20.3 fb⁻¹），采用单标记（single-tag）与双标记（double-tag）方法进行本底抑制与信号提取，结合蒙特卡洛模拟优化事件选择，并使用似然拟合确定信号计数与上限。

Result: D⁰→K⁺π⁻η′与D⁺→K⁺π⁰η′的分支比90% CL上限分别为(3.0±0.3)×10⁻⁵和(2.1±0.2)×10⁻⁵；对应DCSD/CF比值上限为1.6 tan⁴θ_C 和 3.7 tan⁴θ_C，与标准模型树图主导预期一致，未见显著偏离。

Conclusion: 该结果为D介子弱衰变中强子化机制与CKM矩阵一致性提供了新约束，尤其对η′产生机制中的奇异夸克含量与胶球混杂成分具有敏感性；当前精度下未发现新物理迹象，但为未来高统计量实验（如BESIII升级与Super Tau-Charm Factory）奠定了基准。

Abstract: We present the first search for the doubly Cabibbo-suppressed decays $D^0\to K^+π^-η^\prime$ and $D^+\to K^+π^0η^\prime$ using an $e^+e^-$ collision data sample corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 20.3 fb$^{-1}$, collected at a center-of-mass energy of 3.773 GeV with the Beijing Spectrometer III (BESIII) detector at the Beijing Electron-Positron Collider II (BEPCII). No significant signals are observed, and the upper limits on their decay branching fractions are set to be $3.0\times 10^{-5}$ and $2.1\times 10^{-5}$ at the 90% confidence level, respectively. By combining these results with the world-average branching fractions of the corresponding Cabibbo-favored decays, upper limits at the 90% confidence level are obtained on the ratios of doubly Cabibbo-suppressed to Cabibbo-favored branching fractions. The limits are determined to be $1.6\times \tan^4θ_C$ and $3.7\times \tan^4θ_C$ for $D^0\to K^+π^-η^\prime$ and $D^+\to K^+π^0η^\prime$, respectively, where $θ_C$ denotes the Cabibbo mixing angle.

</details>


### [93] [Prospects for precision Higgs boson measurements at a 10 TeV muon collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08392)
*Paolo Andreetto,Massimo Casarsa,Luca Castelli,Alessio Gianelle,Donatella Lucchesi,Leonardo Palombini,Imran Raghib,Lorenzo Sestini,Davide Zuliani*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了10 TeV缪子对撞机在希格斯物理精密测量中的潜力，重点评估单/双希格斯产生截面、希格斯质量及三线性自耦合的探测灵敏度，基于MUSIC探测器详细模拟与机器本底建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用10 TeV缪子对撞机中显著增强的矢量玻色子融合（VBF）产率，实现对希格斯扇区的高精度检验，尤其是对标准模型关键参数（如三线性自耦合）的直接约束。

Method: 基于MUSIC探测器的详细蒙特卡洛模拟，包含机器诱导本底；采用两个实验基准配置，各积累10 ab⁻¹积分亮度；对多个衰变道（bb̄, WW*, γγ, ZZ*, μμ）分别进行统计精度分析，并组合通道以提升希格斯质量测量精度。

Result: H→bb̄与H→WW*截面统计精度达0.18%与0.35%；H→γγ、H→ZZ*、H→μμ精度分别为2.6%、4.2%、6.9%；组合通道得希格斯质量精度~19 MeV；HH→bbbb与HH→bbWW*截面精度为4.2%与14%；由HH→bbbb可测三线性耦合λ_HHH，精度约5%。

Conclusion: 10 TeV缪子对撞机构成希格斯物理前沿的高精度平台，尤其在三线性自耦合测量方面具有不可替代优势，验证其作为下一代能量前沿设施的科学价值。

Abstract: A 10 TeV muon collider offers a powerful environment for precision studies of the Higgs sector through its large vector-boson-fusion production rates. This review presents projected sensitivities to single- and double-Higgs production cross sections, the Higgs boson mass, and the trilinear Higgs self-coupling. The projections are obtained from detailed simulations of the MUSIC detector, including machine-induced background, for a baseline configuration of two experiments, each collecting an integrated luminosity of 10 $\mathrm{ab}^{-1}$. The $H\to b\bar{b}$ and $H\to WW^\ast$ production cross sections can be measured with statistical precisions of 0.18% and 0.35%, respectively, while precisions of 2.6%, 4.2%, and 6.9% are expected for $H\toγγ$, $H\to ZZ^\ast$, and $H\toμ^+μ^-$. Combining the $H\to b\bar{b}$, $H\toγγ$, and $H\toμ^+μ^-$ channels yields an expected Higgs boson mass precision of about 19 MeV. Double-Higgs production can be measured with statistical precisions of 4.2% in the $HH\to b\bar{b}b\bar{b}$ channel and 14% in the $HH\to b\bar{b}WW^\ast$ channel. Using the $HH\to b\bar{b}b\bar{b}$ channel, the trilinear Higgs self-coupling can be determined with an expected precision of about 5%. These results demonstrate the potential of a high-energy muon collider for precision Higgs physics.

</details>


### [94] [Readout electronics for SUBMET](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08427)
*Claudio Campagnari,Sungwoong Cho,Suyong Choi,Seokju Chung,Matthew Citron,Albert De Roeck,Martin Gastal,Seungkyu Ha,Andy Haas,Christopher Scott Hill,Insung Hwang,Hoyong Jeong,Jaebak Kim,Jeonghwa Kim,Hyunki Moon,Ryan Schmitz,David Stuart,Eunil Won,Jae Hyeok Yoo,Jinseok Yoo,Ayman Youssef,Ahmad Zaraket,Haitham Zaraket*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SUBMET实验专用的DAQ系统，该系统针对探测极低电荷量（ε < 10⁻³）的millicharged particles（mCPs）而设计，核心目标是实现单光电子级灵敏度与亚纳秒级时间分辨；采用级联DRS4芯片实现820.5 MHz高速波形采样，8通道覆盖5 μs时间窗以捕获J-PARC 30 GeV质子束的8个连续bunch；通道间时间同步精度达1 ns（片内）、2 ns（板内）、8 ns（板间），远优于30 ns符合窗口要求；前端噪声RMS < 0.4 mV，通过正向基线偏置优化负脉冲的电压分辨率与动态范围；触发控制板支持多板数据聚合与实时传输，实测性能全面满足SUBMET对时间精度、本底噪声和数据吞吐率的要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测电荷量 Q = εe（ε < O(10⁻³)）的millicharged particles（mCPs），其信号仅含极少数散射光子，需极高灵敏度与时间分辨的DAQ系统以实现单光电子甄别与束流结构匹配。

Method: 采用级联Domino Ring Sampler 4（DRS4）芯片架构：8通道分两组级联，每组4通道构成一个读出输入，以820.5 MHz采样率连续采集4096点，覆盖5 μs有效时间窗；实施多层级时间校准（片内/板内/板间）；前端电路引入可控正向基线偏置以提升负脉冲的ADC电压分辨率与动态范围；设计专用触发控制板实现多读出板数据聚合与高速传输。

Result: 通道间时间同步精度达1 ns（同DRS4芯片）、2 ns（同PCB板）、8 ns（跨板），显著优于30 ns符合窗口；前端RMS噪声低于0.4 mV；基线偏置策略使负脉冲充分利用ADC量程；系统实测数据吞吐率满足J-PARC束流运行需求。

Conclusion: 所研制的DAQ系统在时间分辨、本底噪声、动态范围与数据吞吐率等关键指标上全面满足SUBMET实验对millicharged particle单光电子级探测的严苛要求，为后续物理分析提供了可靠的数据获取基础。

Abstract: A dedicated data acquisition (DAQ) system has been developed for the SUB-Millicharge ExperimenT (SUBMET) at the Japan Proton Accelerator Research Complex (J-PARC), a search for particles carrying a fractional electric charge $Q = εe$ with $ε$ below $\mathcal{O}(10^{-3})$, hereafter referred to as millicharged particles (mCPs). Because such particles are expected to produce at most a few scintillation photons, the system is optimized for single-photoelectron detection from the photomultiplier tubes (PMTs), combining high-speed waveform digitization with precise timing. To capture eight consecutive proton bunches of the 30 GeV J-PARC beam within a single trigger, the eight channels of the Domino Ring Sampler 4 (DRS4) chip are cascaded in groups of four to form two readout inputs, each sampling 4096 points continuously at 820.5 MHz over an effective time window of 5 us. After calibration, timing differences between channels are within 1 ns on the same DRS4 chip, 2 ns on the same board, and 8 ns across different boards, well within the 30 ns coincidence window of the experiment. The front-end electronics achieve an RMS noise below 0.4 mV. The baseline is deliberately offset upward such that the negative-going pulses span a larger fraction of the digitizer range, improving voltage resolution and dynamic range. A trigger control board aggregates data from multiple readout boards and sustains the data-transfer rate required for beam operation. The measured performance confirms that the DAQ system meets the timing, noise, and throughput requirements of the experiment.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 基于NOvA近远点探测器联合拟合，采用中微子与反中微子双模式运行数据（PoT分别为26.61×10²⁰和12.50×10²⁰），在sin²2θₘᵢₓ–Δm²₄₁平面进行卡方拟合以约束4ν振荡参数。

Result: 未观测到显著偏离3ν预期的振荡信号；在90% CL下排除了Δm²₄₁ ∈ [0.1, 10] eV²、sin²2θₘᵢₓ > 0.05的大片参数区域，尤其覆盖IceCube报告的大部分允许区。

Conclusion: NOvA当前数据不支持3+1模型下的惰性中微子存在，对轻惰性中微子振荡参数施加迄今最严格限制之一。

Abstract: We report a search for neutrino oscillations to sterile neutrinos in the NOvA detectors under a model with three active and one sterile neutrinos. This search simultaneously fits data in the two NOvA detectors and is the first from NOvA to use both neutrino- and antineutrino-mode beams, with exposures of $26.61\times10^{20}$ and $12.50\times10^{20}$ protons on target, respectively. There is no evidence for sterile neutrinos in the data and we are able to exclude regions of parameter space that were allowed by previous experiments, including most of the allowed region reported by IceCube.

</details>


### [96] [A virtual reality extension for the Geant4 toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08926)
*Benjamin Jobilal,Muhammad Ansar Iqbal,Jay Hauser*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了G4VR，一个为Geant4仿真工具包开发的虚拟现实（VR）可视化应用，通过新设计的可视化驱动G4XR实现与Geant4源码的直接集成，支持沉浸式粒子轨迹、相互作用与能量沉积的交互式三维探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升Geant4传统二维可视化在物理理解上的直观性与沉浸感，满足高能物理、核物理等领域对复杂事件结构进行三维空间-时间分析的需求。

Method: 开发基于OpenXR标准的新型可视化驱动G4XR，并构建跨平台VR应用G4VR，支持手柄交互、事件时序回放、动画电影模式及多类型内置事件显示。

Result: 实现了Geant4仿真实验在主流VR头显上的实时、交互式三维可视化；任意Geant4实验可无缝加载至VR环境；支持粒子轨迹选择、能量沉积定位、相互作用细节调阅与全事件动态回放。

Conclusion: G4VR显著拓展了Geant4的可视化能力边界，为粒子物理仿真提供了全新的沉浸式分析范式，兼具教学演示、实验调试与协作研究价值。

Abstract: Geant4 is a toolkit for simulating particle interactions in matter that is widely used in particle physics, nuclear physics, medical imaging, and astrophysics. At present, Geant4 visualizations are generally viewed on a conventional, two-dimensional computer display. To enhance immersion, we have developed G4VR, a virtual reality (VR) application that extends Geant4 with VR capabilities, providing a more intuitive and comprehensive way to understand these interactions. The user running G4VR in a headset can get detailed information about individual tracks, interactions, and energy depositions by selecting them with the hand controllers. G4VR also allows the user to examine the chronology of interactions, and includes animations of entire events as immersive movies. G4VR includes built-in event displays of several types that allow a first-time user to easily explore its various functionalities. To interface with Geant4, we have developed a new visualization driver called G4XR that allows direct integration with the Geant4 source code. This enables any Geant4 experiment to be loaded and experienced in VR seamlessly.

</details>
