TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于规范U(1)_{B-L}对称性的非弹性标量暗物质模型，通过Z′共振湮灭机制解释LZ实验观测到的2.6σ高能反冲事例，并预言σ_SI ~ 10^{-45} cm²，与实验一致，且可通过未来对Z′的对撞机探测予以证伪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）合作组报道的2.6σ高能核反冲事例，该事例难以被标准弹性散射暗物质模型容纳，需引入非弹性散射机制。

Method: 构建带U(1)_{B-L}规范对称性的标量暗物质模型，要求暗物质S为实标量、质量m_S ≃ m_{Z′}/2以实现热退耦时的s-道Z′共振湮灭，同时满足Boltzmann方程约束的热遗余丰度；计算非弹性自旋无关散射截面σ_SI，并与LZ数据比对。

Result: 获得满足Ωh² ≈ 0.12的参数空间：m_S ∼ TeV量级、m_{Z′} ∼ 2×m_S、g_{B-L} ∼ 0.5，对应预测的DM-核子非弹性散射截面σ_SI ∼ 10^{-45} cm²，与LZ事例所需截面高度一致。

Conclusion: 该模型在不违背现有直接探测与宇宙学约束前提下，自然解释LZ异常信号；Z′玻色子在LHC或未来高能对撞机上的共振产生将是该模型的关键可检验预言。

Abstract: We show that our previously proposed inelastic scalar dark matter (DM) model in gauged $U(1)_{B-L}$ symmetry naturally accounts for the $2.6σ$ high-energy recoil event recently reported by the LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) collaboration. To satisfy the thermal relic abundance, TeV-scale scalar DM $S$ with $m_S \simeq m_{Z'}/2$ for resonant annihilation and the gauge coupling constant $g_{B-L} \sim 0.5$ are required, where $m_{Z^\prime}$ is the mass of $U(1)_{B-L}$ gauge boson $Z^{\prime}$. The resulting predicted DM-nucleon inelastic cross section of $σ_\mathrm{SI} \sim 10^{-45}$ cm${}^2$ is in excellent agreement with the LZ event. This scenario can be decisively tested for future collider searches for $Z^\prime$ boson resonance.

</details>


### [122] [LZ Nuclear-Recoil Excess from Boosted Light Magnetic Dipole-dipole Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06756)
*Jin-Han Liang,Zuowei Liu,Van Que Tran,Yongheng Xu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LZ实验观测到248 keV附近的核反冲事例超出，显著性2.6σ；标准晕暗物质模型（磁偶极-偶极相互作用、TeV质量）虽最优拟合该超出，但与低能区无超出相冲突；本文提出 boosted速度分布可缓解张力，并允许GeV量级暗物质解释该信号；该机制在模型无关分析及具体暗物质模型（晕暗物质湮灭产生on-shell中介子，再衰变至boosted暗区粒子）中均被实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和LZ实验在248 keV处观测到的核反冲超出与低能区无事例超出之间的张力，同时拓展对暗物质质量尺度（尤其是GeV量级）的解释空间。

Method: 采用模型无关的 recoil spectrum 拟合方法，引入 boosted velocity distribution 描述暗物质速度分布；进而构建具体粒子物理模型：银河系晕中暗物质通过s-通道湮灭产生on-shell暗区中介子，后者衰变为具有高boost因子的轻暗物质粒子，从而获得非标准速度谱。

Result: boosted速度分布显著改善对LZ能谱的整体拟合，缓解低能区过预测问题；允许暗物质质量低至GeV量级（远低于TeV），且仍兼容248 keV处的超出；所提暗区模型自洽实现该动力学机制。

Conclusion: boosted暗区粒子是LZ反冲超出的一个可行物理解释，它突破了传统晕暗物质参数空间限制，为轻质量、非热起源暗物质提供了新探针。

Abstract: The LZ collaboration has reported a nuclear-recoil excess near 248 keV with a global significance of $2.6σ$. Although halo dark matter with a magnetic dipole-dipole interaction and a TeV-scale mass provides the best fit to the excess among the interactions considered by LZ, it predicts a considerable number of events at lower recoil energies, where no excess is observed. We show that a boosted velocity distribution can alleviate this tension and provide a better fit to the LZ recoil spectrum. Moreover, the boost opens up the possibility of explaining the excess with much lighter dark matter, with masses down to the GeV scale. We demonstrate these features first in a model-independent analysis and then realize them in a concrete dark matter model, in which halo dark matter annihilates into on-shell mediators that subsequently decay into boosted dark-sector particles. Our results demonstrate that boosted dark sector particles provide a viable interpretation of the LZ excess.

</details>


### [123] [LZ nuclear recoil event from inelastic singlet-doublet scalar dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07451)
*Disha Bandyopadhyay,Debasish Borah,Pankaj Borah*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了单态-二重态标量暗物质模型能否解释LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验观测到的高能核反冲事例，发现该模型在满足正确热遗迹丰度与直接探测限制的前提下，可实现与LZ事件一致的非弹性上散射过程；引入Z₂-奇右手中微子还可同时解释轻中微子质量与重子不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LZ合作组最新报道的高能核反冲事例，并在singlet-doublet scalar DM框架下协调宇宙学丰度、直接探测约束与非弹性散射信号。

Method: 基于具有未破缺Z₂对称性的实标量单态与复标量二重态混合构成CP偶质量本征态的暗物质模型，计算热遗迹丰度、自旋无关弹性散射截面及非弹性上散射率，并结合LZ实验阈值与能谱响应进行参数空间扫描。

Result: 确定了一组满足Ωh² ≈ 0.12、XENONnT/LZ弹性散射上限、且非弹性上散射产额与 recoil 能谱匹配LZ单事例信号的参数区域；该区域显著扩展于纯标量二重态DM情形，且允许更大的暗物质质量与耦合强度。

Conclusion: Singlet-doublet scalar DM模型可自然容纳LZ高能事例作为非弹性上散射信号，兼具理论自洽性与现象学丰富性——尤其当引入Z₂-奇右手重中微子时，可统一解决暗物质、中微子质量与重子生成三大问题。

Abstract: We study the possibility of explaining the recently reported high-energy nuclear recoil event by the LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) collaboration within the framework of singlet-doublet scalar dark matter (DM). Considering DM to be the CP even mass eigenstate formed out of a scalar doublet and a real scalar singlet, both being odd under an unbroken $Z_2$ symmetry, we find the parameter space of the model consistent with correct relic abundance and direct-detection limits on elastic scattering rates. A large part of the parameter space can also lead to inelastic up-scattering of DM with a rate and recoil energy consistent with the recent LZ event. Depending upon the singlet-doublet mixing, the model allows a much wider range of currently allowed parameter space compared to the pure scalar doublet DM limit. Presence of $Z_2$-odd right-handed neutrinos also leads to other interesting phenomenology related to the origin of light neutrino masses and leptogenesis.

</details>


### [124] [Solar Capture Tests of Inelastic Dark Matter after the LZ High-Recoil Event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06760)
*Mattia Di Mauro,Halim Shaikh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究太阳捕获对三种解释LZ实验248 keV核反冲事例的类热暗物质模型的限制，发现标准热Higgsino被IceCube中微子观测排除，而伪Dirac费米子和中子亲和型O₄算符主导的endothermic暗物质仍与现有太阳捕获约束兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ实验观测到一个248 keV核反冲候选事例，endothermic暗物质可给出较高局部显著性；需检验其是否与太阳内暗物质捕获-湮灭产生的中微子通量（IceCube限制）相容。

Method: 计算三种endothermic暗物质模型（热Higgsino、伪Dirac费米子、中子亲和型O₄自旋依赖散射）在太阳内的捕获率C⊙，结合两态动力学（含热辅助再激发χ₁→χ₂）、有限温度效应及自旋响应不确定性，评估湮灭率Γ_A，并与IceCube基于bb̄末态的中微子通量上限比对。

Result: 1) 热Higgsino：LZ偏好分裂δ≈0.38 MeV < IceCube要求δ≳0.51–0.56 MeV，被排除；2) 伪Dirac模型：C⊙=1.48×10²⁰ s⁻¹，但两态动力学抑制Γ_A至~4.1×10¹⁴ s⁻¹，比IceCube bb̄极限低约1.8×10⁵；3) O₄模型（mχ=1 TeV, δ=300 keV）：C⊙=3.98×10¹⁷ s⁻¹，平衡态Γ_A上限仍比IceCube极限低因子~154（激进假设下仍低因子~19）。

Conclusion: Solar capture via IceCube bb̄ limits robustly excludes the canonical thermal Higgsino interpretation of the LZ candidate, but does not generically exclude endothermic DM — in particular, pseudo-Dirac and neutron-philic O₄ scenarios remain viable.

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) Collaboration has reported a $248$ keV nuclear-recoil candidate for which endothermic dark matter (DM) gives some of the largest local significances. We study Solar-capture constraints on three simple interpretations: a thermal Higgsino, a thermal pseudo-Dirac fermion with off-diagonal vector interactions, and neutron-philic endothermic spin-dependent scattering through $\mathcal{O}_4$. The canonical full-density thermal Higgsino is excluded: its LZ-preferred splitting near $0.38,\mathrm{MeV}$ lies below the IceCube requirement, $δ\gtrsim0.51$--$0.56,\mathrm{MeV}$. For the pseudo-Dirac benchmark we find $C_\odot=1.48\times10^{20},\mathrm{s}^{-1}$, but two-state kinetics strongly suppresses annihilation. Including thermally assisted $χ_1\toχ_2$ re-excitation gives a maximum $Γ_A\simeq4.1\times10^{14},\mathrm{s}^{-1}$, about $1.8\times10^5$ below the adopted IceCube $b\bar b$ limit. For neutron-philic $\mathcal{O}_4$ scattering at $m_χ=1$ TeV and $δ=300$ keV, finite-temperature capture gives $C_\odot=3.98\times10^{17},\mathrm{s}^{-1}$, with spin-response range $(1.77$--$9.72)\times10^{17},\mathrm{s}^{-1}$. Even in equilibrium, the upper edge remains about a factor $154$ below the adopted $b\bar b$ limit, while an aggressive no-finite-$q$ suppression plus upper-spin stress test still leaves a factor about $19$. The $b\bar b$ mapping is used as a representative hadronic template for the two non-Higgsino benchmarks. Solar capture therefore excludes the thermal-Higgsino interpretation but does not generically exclude endothermic explanations of the LZ candidate.

</details>


### [125] [Inelastic Singlet-Doublet Fermion Dark Matter in light of the 248 keV LZ event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07800)
*Debasish Borah,Sujit Kumar Sahoo,Narendra Sahu,Shashwat Sharma*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LZ实验观测到一个暗物质-原子核散射事件，能量为248 keV；文章提出单-二重态费米子暗物质模型，其中暗物质为类伪狄拉克态，通过Z玻色子交换实现非弹性散射，并引入Z2偶标量三重态以解释能级劈裂与II型跷跷板机制生成中微子质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LZ实验在248 keV处观测到的单个核反冲事件，同时说明低能区无事例的现象——该现象自然指向非弹性暗物质-原子核散射过程。

Method: 构建单-二重态费米子暗物质模型，其中暗物质由两个近简并的伪狄拉克态构成；利用其马约拉纳性质禁戒树图Z玻色子介导的弹性散射，但允许Z交换主导的非弹性散射；引入Z2-偶标量三重态以产生质量劈裂并实现Type-II seesaw机制。

Result: 该模型可一致解释LZ观测事例、低能区缺失事件、暗物质热遗存丰度，并同时容纳轻中微子质量；Z交换非弹性散射截面受LZ能谱形状约束，劈裂质量差Δm ≈ 100–300 keV，三重态真空期望值vΔ ∼ 0.1–1 eV。

Conclusion: 单-二重态+标量三重态扩展模型为LZ异常事例提供自洽的粒子物理解释，兼具暗物质唯象学与中微子质量起源双重动机，是连接直接探测信号与电弱尺度新物理的重要候选框架。

Abstract: Recently, the LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) collaboration reported the observation of a single dark matter (DM)-nucleus scattering event at a nuclear recoil energy of $248\pm23_{\rm stat}\pm23_{\rm sys}$ keV, corresponding to an exposure of 2.84 tonne-year. The absence of events at lower nuclear recoil energies in the predicted spectrum is naturally explained if the underlying process is inelastic DM-nucleus scattering. Motivated by this, we investigate the singlet-doublet fermion DM model, in which the DM consists of two pseudo-Dirac states: the Majorana nature of the lighter state forbids tree level $Z$-mediated elastic scattering identically, while the same states enable inelastic DM-nucleus scattering via $Z$ exchange, with any residual elastic scattering proceeding only through a suppressed Higgs-mediated channel. We further extend the model with a $Z_2$-even scalar triplet, which is responsible both for generating the pseudo-Dirac splitting and for realizing Majorana neutrino masses via the Type-II seesaw mechanism.

</details>


### [126] [Revisiting two-body charmed anti-charmed baryonic $B$ decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08053)
*Chun-Khiang Chua*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新研究了两体含粲重子衰变 $\overline B \to {\bf B}_c \overline{\bf B}_c$，首次系统纳入 CKM 压制但动力学增强的 $b\to u\bar u q$ 弱跃迁贡献，并更新了基于 SU(3)_F 对称性的拓扑振幅分解；发现其在 $\Delta S = 0$ 衰变中因 CKM 因子比放大 $\lambda^{-2} \sim 20$ 倍而不可忽略，可显著提升如 $B^- \to \Xi_c^0 \bar\Xi_c^-$ 等道的分支比，为检验非因子化强子化机制提供新探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分析仅考虑主导的 $b\to c\bar c q$ 振幅，忽略 CKM 压制的 $b\to u\bar u q$ 贡献；然而在 $\Delta S = 0$ 衰变中，该压制造成的相对抑制被大幅削弱，可能对某些末态产生可观影响，亟需理论再评估与实验检验。

Method: 采用基于 SU(3)_F 对称性的拓扑振幅方法（Topological Amplitude Approach），对 $\overline B \to {\bf B}_c(\mathbf{\bar 3}_f) \overline{\bf B}_c(\mathbf{3}_f)$、$\mathbf{6}_f \otimes \mathbf{3}_f$、$\mathbf{\bar 3}_f \otimes \mathbf{\bar 6}_f$ 和 $\mathbf{6}_f \otimes \mathbf{\bar 6}_f$ 四类强子末态进行系统振幅分解，并显式计入 $b\to u\bar u q$ 树图及相关的 W-交换/企鹅图拓扑类。

Result: 在 $\Delta S = 0$ 衰变中，$b\to u\bar u q$ 贡献因 $|V_{ub}V_{uq}^*/V_{cb}V_{cq}^*| \sim \lambda^{-2}$ 被放大约 20 倍；现有 $\overline B \to {\bf B}_c(\mathbf{\bar 3}_f) \overline{\bf B}_c(\mathbf{3}_f)$ 数据允许甚至轻微偏好非零 $b\to u\bar u q$ 振幅；预测 $B^- \to \Xi_c^0 \bar\Xi_c^-$、$\overline B_s^0 \to \Lambda_c^+ \bar\Xi_c^-$ 和 $\overline B^0 \to \Xi_c^+ \bar\Xi_c^-$ 的分支比可比纯 $b\to c\bar c d$ 机制高出一个数量级。

Conclusion: 在两体粲重子对产生衰变中，$b\to u\bar u q$ 振幅虽 CKM 压制，但在 $\Delta S = 0$ 过程中因 SU(3)_F 对称性破缺下的动力学增强而不可忽略；其实验验证将有助于厘清非因子化强子化机制、检验 SU(3)_F 振幅分解的有效性，并约束 QCD 因子化框架中的 W-交换与企鹅振幅。

Abstract: The study of two-body charmed anti-charmed baryonic $\overline B\to {\bf B}_c \overline {\bf B}_c$ decays using the topological amplitude approach is revisited. We include the $b\to u\bar u q$ contributions, in addition to the $b\to c\bar c q$ contributions, to these decays. The topological decomposition of $\overline B\to {\bf B}_c(\bf {\bar 3_f}) \overline {\bf B}_c(\bf { 3_f})$, ${\bf B}_c(\bf 6_f) \overline {\bf B}_c(\bf { 3_f})$, ${\bf B}_c(\bf {\bar 3_f}) \overline {\bf B}_c(\bf {\bar 6_f})$ and ${\bf B}_c(\bf 6_f) \overline {\bf B}_c(\bf {\bar 6_f})$ decay amplitudes are updated accordingly. Although the $b\to u\bar u q$ contributions are CKM suppressed in $ΔS=-1$ transitions, their effects are significantly amplified in $ΔS=0$ transitions, as the relative size of the CKM factors in $b\to u\bar u q$ and $b\to c\bar c q$ contributions in $ΔS=0$ transitions is enlarged by $λ^{-2}$, roughly a factor of 20, from the one in $ΔS=-1$ transitions. Present data in $\overline B\to {\bf B}_c(\bf {\bar 3_f}) \overline {\bf B}_c(\bf { 3_f})$ decay rates allow or even slightly prefer non-negligible $b\to u\bar u q$ contributions. These $b\to u\bar u q$ contributions can allow much larger rates for $B^-\to Ξ_c^0 \bar Ξ_c^{-}$, $\overline B{}_s^0\to Λ_c^+ \bar Ξ_c^{-}$ and $\overline B{}^0\to Ξ_c^+ \bar Ξ_c^{-}$ decays than those only have $b\to c\bar c d$ contributions. Measuring these decays will be interesting and useful in clarifying the role of $b\to u\bar u q$ contributions in two-body charmed anti-charmed baryonic $\overline B\to {\bf B}_c \overline {\bf B}_c$ decays.

</details>


### [127] [Reggeization of quarks from next-to-eikonal high-energy QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06833)
*Tolga Altinoluk,Guillaume Beuf,Jules Favrel,Michael Fucilla*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了QCD中夸克Regge化的完整Wilson线表述，通过背景场方法导出Regge化夸克插值算符及其非线性快度演化方程；在稀疏极限下，正宇称分量呈现由夸克Regge轨迹支配的极点行为，负宇称分量则体现夸克-胶子混合（即Regge割）；平面极限下混合被压低，实现无需显式宇称投影的Regge化与宇称简并；该构造具有普适性，并可推广至有质量夸克，其坐标空间演化核的傅里叶变换复现质量夸克Regge轨迹；进一步论证该插值算符在NLL精度下仍为快度演化本征态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立严格、普适且可推广的夸克Regge化量子场论表述，弥合传统Regge理论与现代高能QCD因子化框架之间的鸿沟，尤其解决夸克Regge轨迹的算符定义、快度演化结构及质量修正等长期问题。

Method: 基于半无限Wilson线与背景夸克插入构成的次偶极算符，运用背景场方法推导Regge化夸克插值算符；结合宇称（signature）分解、平面极限分析、算符混合结构与快度重整化群分析；对有质量情形，在坐标空间构造演化核并作傅里叶变换验证轨迹。

Result: 1) 获得明确定义的Regge化夸克插值算符；2) 推出其非线性快度演化方程，揭示正宇称极点与负宇称割的结构分离；3) 证明平面极限下Regge化自动出现且宇称简并；4) 给出有质量夸克的坐标空间演化核，其动量空间形式精确复现已知质量Regge轨迹；5) 证明该算符在NLL精度下保持快度演化本征态性质。

Conclusion: 夸克Regge化可在Wilson线框架下严格实现，其本质是快度演化下的本征算符，兼具普适性、可重整性与可推广性；该工作为高能散射中夸克参与的BFKL/Resummed动力学提供了坚实算符基础，并统一处理了无质量与有质量情形。

Abstract: We develop a comprehensive Wilson-line formulation of quark Reggeization in QCD. Starting from a next-to-eikonal operator built from a semi-infinite Wilson line and a background-quark insertion, we identify an interpolating operator for the Reggeized quark and derive its nonlinear rapidity evolution using the background-field method. In the dilute regime, its positive-signature component exhibits Regge-pole evolution governed by the quark Regge trajectory, whereas the negative-signature sector displays mixing between quark and gluon degrees of freedom, in accordance with its known Regge-cut structure. In the planar limit, this mixing is suppressed, and Reggeization emerges without an explicit signature projection, recovering signature degeneracy. We illustrate the universality of the construction by extracting the same Reggeized-quark operator from a more general Wilson-line operator describing a gluon-to-quark transition. Furthermore, we extend the formalism to massive quarks, deriving a coordinate-space evolution kernel whose Fourier transform reproduces the massive-quark Regge trajectory. Finally, from the leading operator-mixing structure of the evolution equation and signature arguments, we show that the Reggeized-quark interpolating operator is expected to remain an eigenstate of the rapidity evolution up to next-to-leading logarithmic accuracy.

</details>


### [128] [Quark mass functions in Minkowski space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06839)
*Elmar P. Biernat,Franz Gross,M. T. Peña,Alfred Stadler*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用协变旁观者理论（CST）计算了从上/下到底五种夸克味的 dressed 夸克质量函数和波函数重整化常数，覆盖闵可夫斯基时空的类空与类时区域；采用拟合格点数据的 dressed 胶子传播子模型，并通过拟合格点给出的夸克质量函数确定自能剩余自由参数，结果与夸克模型中常用的组分质量一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在相对论协变框架下统一描述轻重夸克的非微扰动力学性质，特别是质量生成机制与波函数重整化行为，并连接格点QCD计算与唯象夸克模型。

Method: 基于协变旁观者理论（CST）求解夸克自能积分方程；采用含参数的dressed胶子传播子（拟合类空格点胶子数据）；通过最小二乘拟合两类独立格点群提供的夸克质量函数数据，同时约束波函数重整化Z2满足规范一致性条件。

Result: 获得了五味夸克（u/d, s, c, b）在类空与类时动量区间的质量函数M(p²)和波函数重整化Z2(p²)；所有味的质量预测落在典型组分质量区间内（如u/d: ~300 MeV, s: ~500 MeV, c: ~1.5 GeV, b: ~4.8 GeV）；Z2在p²→0处满足手征极限下的有限性要求。

Conclusion: CST框架下结合格点输入的夸克自能建模是可行且自洽的，能自然复现组分夸克质量标度，为强子谱学与电磁跃迁提供可靠的相对论性夸克结构输入。

Abstract: Using the Covariant Spectator Theory (CST), we calculate the dressed quark mass function and wave-function renormalization for the five quark flavors from up/down to bottom in both the spacelike and timelike regions of Minkowski space. The calculation employs a model dressed-gluon propagator fitted to lattice data in the spacelike region. The remaining free parameters of the quark self-energy are determined by fits to lattice results for the quark mass function including a constraint built into the wave-function renormalization. We perform these fits using lattice data from two different groups and find that the resulting mass predictions are within the range of the typical constituent masses used in quark models.

</details>


### [129] [Inclusive electron-nucleus cross section models from domain adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08463)
*Krzysztof M. Graczyk,Beata E. Kowal,Rwik Dharmapal Banerjee,Jose Luis Bonilla,Hemant Prasad,Jan T. Sobczyk*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用迁移学习（TL）构建电子-原子核包容截面的数据驱动模型，以碳-12为源域预训练深度神经网络，并对多种核素（He-3、Li-6、O-16、Al-27、Ca-40、Fe-56）分别微调；研究发现微调深度与目标核数据质量及动量空间覆盖度密切相关，且模型在碳训练域外的部分运动学区域仍保持与实验一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升电子-核包容截面建模的泛化能力与数据效率，尤其针对中等质量核缺乏高质量实验数据的现状，避免从头训练导致的过拟合与物理不一致性。

Method: 基于深度神经网络的迁移学习框架：以^12C截面数据预训练模型集成，再对各目标核（^3He, ^6Li, ^16O, ^27Al, ^40Ca, ^56Fe）独立进行分层微调；系统分析微调层数、训练子集比例/选取策略、源-目标动量空间重叠度对性能的影响。

Result: 所有目标核模型性能均提升（O-16提升微弱）；O-16仅需浅层微调，He-3/Ca-40/Fe-56需深层微调；Li-6因数据稀缺最不稳健；Al-27对少数高精度测量高度敏感；跨域预测（超出^12C训练动量范围）仍与实验在误差内一致；与F1F2唯象模型对比显示迁移模型具备竞争性精度。

Conclusion: 迁移学习是构建多核电子散射数据驱动模型的有效范式，其适应性取决于目标核数据质量与源-目标运动学重叠程度；该方法可作为传统唯象模型的补充，在核结构信息提取与实验规划中提供可靠代理模型。

Abstract: We apply transfer learning (TL) to construct data-driven models of inclusive electron-nucleus cross sections. Starting from an ensemble of deep neural networks pretrained on \(^{12}\)C data, we fine-tune the models separately for \(^{3}\)He, \(^{6}\)Li, \(^{16}\)O, \(^{27}\)Al, \(^{40}\)Ca, and \(^{56}\)Fe. The resulting models improve for all targets, marginally so for oxygen, where the carbon baseline is already adequate, although their predictive robustness depends on the amount, coverage, and precision of the available target data. We systematically study how model performance depends on the number of fine-tuned layers, on the fraction and selection of the training data, and on the overlap between the source and target kinematic domains. The layer-wise analysis shows that oxygen requires only shallow adaptation, whereas helium, calcium, and iron require substantially deeper fine-tuning. Lithium represents the least robust case because of its limited dataset, while aluminum demonstrates a strong sensitivity to a small subset of highly constraining measurements. For selected kinematic configurations outside the coverage of the carbon training data, the adapted models remain consistent with the measurements within their estimated uncertainties. Finally, we compare the resulting predictions with those of the phenomenological F1F2 model.

</details>


### [130] [Geometric Phases in Two-Level Mixing: From Neutral Mesons to Holonomic Qubits and Topological Majorana Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06857)
*Swarup Sangiri,Utpal Sarkar,A Taraphder*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了受中性介子振荡启发的厄米二能级混合哈密顿量的几何结构，将其映射到有效量子比特表示，分析布洛赫球面上循环参数演化所诱导的几何相位；引入CP类几何参数解释复混合相，并通过Bargmann不变量构造离散相；进一步将该构造推广至Kitaev链的动量依赖BdG哈密顿量，关联动量空间缠绕数、拓扑相区与Zak相，并在连续极限下使Bargmann构造趋近整体相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解中性介子振荡中复混合相的几何起源及其与量子信息、拓扑物态的深层联系，统一描述qubit、费米子与拓扑相的几何结构。

Method: 采用布洛赫球面几何表示、Bargmann不变量构造、Majorana双线性表达、Bogoliubov-de Gennes变换及动量空间Berry/Zak相分析。

Result: 确立了混合相作为CP类几何参数的布洛赫矢量方位角控制作用；导出三阶Bargmann离散相并刻画其对混合参数的依赖；实现循环演化到R_z单量子比特门的等价映射；在Kitaev链中建立缠绕数、Zak相与Bargmann相在连续极限下的渐近一致性。

Conclusion: 两类二能级哈密顿量（中性介子混合与Kitaev链BdG）共享统一的几何相结构：复参数演化诱导的几何相、离散Bargmann相与拓扑Zak相构成互补描述，揭示了量子相位、量子操控与拓扑序之间的内在几何纽带。

Abstract: We investigate the geometric structure of a Hermitian two-level mixing Hamiltonian motivated by neutral meson oscillations and its connections with qubit, fermionic, and topological descriptions. Mapping the Hamiltonian to an effective qubit representation, we analyze the geometric phase associated with cyclic parameter evolution on the Bloch sphere. Without identifying the complex mixing phase with a physical CP-violation observable, we interpret it as a CP-like geometric parameter controlling the azimuthal orientation of the Hamiltonian vector and its reversal under phase conjugation. Third-order Bargmann invariants constructed from the eigenstates yield an associated discrete phase whose dependence on the mixing parameters is examined alongside the continuous geometric construction. The cyclic evolution is also represented as a single-qubit $R_z$ phase operation and expressed through Majorana bilinears. Extending the construction to the momentum-dependent Bogoliubov-de Gennes Hamiltonian of the Kitaev chain, we relate momentum-space winding to the topological regimes and the quantized Berry (Zak) phase in the real-parameter model, while the corresponding Bargmann construction approaches the global phase in the continuum limit. Together, these results provide a geometric perspective connecting phase structure, qubit operations, fermionic representations, and momentum-space topology within related two-level Hamiltonians.

</details>


### [131] [A Vector-Like Lepton Interpretation of the High-Energy Nuclear Recoil Candidate in LUX-ZEPLIN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08993)
*Fatemeh Elahi,Pedro Schwaller*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LZ实验在248 keV核反冲能量处观测到一个候选事例；文章提出基于自旋依赖（SD）相互作用的singlet-doublet Majorana暗物质模型，通过O₄ = S_DM·S_N算符解释该高能事例，并利用Higgs盲点抑制过强的自旋无关（SI）散射；该模型可同时满足热退耦丰度与LZ事例率，并可通过太阳中微子实验（如IceCube）进行独立检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LZ实验在异常高核反冲能量（248 keV）处观测到的单事件候选者，该能量远超常规SI氙响应敏感区，需具有硬反冲谱的暗物质相互作用机制。

Method: 采用非相对论有效算符方法，聚焦自旋依赖算符O₄ = S_DM·S_N；构建singlet-doublet Majorana暗物质模型，其Z玻色子耦合提供主导SD散射，Higgs介导的SI散射则沿Higgs盲点被压低；结合LZ探测器响应、热历史计算（Boltzmann方程）、太阳捕获率及中微子望远镜（IceCube）极限进行联合分析。

Result: 发现Higgs盲点上存在参数区域：（i）预测LZ高能窗（~248 keV）事例率O(1)；（ii）精确再现热退耦暗物质丰度Ωh² ≈ 0.12；（iii）太阳捕获率足够高，使IceCube已部分排除该区域，但仍存可观测剩余参数空间。

Conclusion: LZ高能候选事例可由SD主导的singlet-doublet Majorana暗物质在Higgs盲点实现自洽解释；该框架具备可证伪性——未来LZ增程运行与升级的太阳中微子探测（如Hyper-K、DUNE）将提供互补检验。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment has recently reported a nuclear-recoil candidate at $E_R=248\pm23_{\rm stat}\pm23_{\rm sys}\,{\rm keV}$ in an exposure of $2.84\,{\rm tonne\,yr}$. At this unusually high recoil energy, the spin-independent (SI) xenon response is strongly suppressed, motivating dark matter interactions with a harder recoil spectrum. We show that elastic spin-dependent (SD) scattering through the nonrelativistic operator $\mathcal O_4=\mathbf S_{\rm DM}\cdot\mathbf S_N$ provides a simple realization of such a spectrum. We consider singlet-doublet Majorana dark matter, for which a diagonal $Z$ coupling generates the required SD interaction. The accompanying Higgs-mediated SI interaction would generically produce too many low-energy recoils, but can be suppressed along a Higgs blind spot while retaining a nonzero $Z$ coupling. We find a region of the Higgs blind spot that simultaneously predicts an $\mathcal O(1)$ event rate in the LZ high-recoil window and reproduces the observed thermal relic abundance. The same SD interaction leads to solar capture, allowing this region to be tested independently with solar-neutrino searches. Current IceCube limits already probe part of the LZ-compatible thermal parameter space, while a viable region remains. Additional xenon exposure and improved solar-neutrino searches can therefore provide complementary tests of this interpretation.

</details>


### [132] [Neutron disappearance and the LZ nuclear recoil event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09037)
*Mohammad Aghaie,Alessandro Strumia*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LZ实验观测到一个248 keV核反冲事例，可能源于束缚中子的消失（自发衰变或暗物质诱导反应）；若能量释放大，则反冲谱展宽且安静事例占比仅~10%；若为近阈值暗物质诱导中子消失，则在氙中允许、氧中禁戒，安静事例升至~30%，反冲谱呈现线状结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LZ实验中观测到的248 keV异常核反冲事例，探索其作为束缚中子消失（spontaneous或DM-induced）的新物理信号的可能性。

Method: 基于能量守恒与核反冲动力学建模，分析中子消失过程在不同靶核（Xe vs O）中的相空间允许性、反冲能谱形状及伴随核激发/碎裂概率（即'quiet fraction'），结合现有氧基实验约束推断参数空间。

Result: 发现：(i) 大能量释放情形下反冲谱展宽，quiet fraction ≈ 10%，且消失率靶无关，受氧实验弱约束；(ii) 近阈值DM诱导中子消失可实现Xe允许而O禁戒，quiet fraction提升至≈30%，反冲谱退化为离散谱线。

Conclusion: 该异常事例可自洽地解释为暗物质诱导的近阈值束缚中子消失过程，具有可观测的谱线特征与靶依赖性，为未来多靶直接探测实验提供新判据。

Abstract: The LZ experiment reported a nuclear recoil with anomalous energy $E_R=248$ keV, around the typical neutron motion in xenon. This motivates interpreting the event as the disappearance of a bound neutron, either through spontaneous decay or a dark-matter-induced reaction. If the energy released is large, the predicted recoil spectrum is broad around the observed value; only $\sim10\%$ of xenon events avoid extra nuclear activity; the disappearance rate is target-independent and mildly constrained by oxygen experiments. In the special case of near-threshold DM-induced neutron disappearance, it can be open in xenon and closed in oxygen, the quiet fraction rises to $\sim30\%$, the recoil spectrum turns into lines.

</details>


### [133] [Bottomonium suppression with a machine-learning-informed Debye mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09047)
*Ajaharul Islam,Shibo Chen,Fu-Peng Li,Long-Gang Pang,Guang-You Qin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用机器学习方法从格点QCD数据中提取复值Debye质量，并将其应用于KSU重夸克势，进而通过量子轨迹框架求解实时薛定谔方程，研究底夸克偶素在夸克胶子等离子体中的演化；计算了Pb-Pb碰撞中Υ(1S,2S,3S)的核修正因子与双比，并发现ML改进的Debye质量显著提升了对激发态实验数据的描述能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受数据驱动方法近期进展的启发，旨在提升对重夸克偶素在夸克胶子等离子体（QGP）中非平衡演化的理论描述精度，特别是对激发态抑制行为的刻画。

Method: 基于格点QCD输入，采用机器学习方法拟合复值Debye质量，嵌入复KSU势；在量子轨迹（QTraj）框架下数值求解实时薛定谔方程，模拟底夸克偶素在QGP中的演化；进而计算Υ(1S)、Υ(2S)、Υ(3S)的核修改因子R_AA和双比R_2S/1S、R_3S/1S。

Result: ML优化的Debye质量使Υ(2S)和Υ(3S)的R_AA及双比更接近ALICE、ATLAS与CMS实验数据，尤其改善了对激发态过度压制的传统模型偏差；实证表明复势中Im(V)的ML重构对非平衡束缚态解离动力学至关重要。

Conclusion: 机器学习可有效增强第一性原理约束下的非微扰QCD输运模型，复Debye质量作为关键介质响应参数，其数据驱动重构为连接格点计算与重味探针实验提供了新范式。

Abstract: Motivated by recent progress in data-driven approaches, we introduce a machine-learning (ML)-informed Debye mass, extracted from lattice-informed inputs, exclusively in the complex-valued heavy-quark Kent State University (KSU) potential. The resulting complex potential is used to solve the real-time Schrödinger equation within the quantum trajectories (QTraj) framework for the evolution of bottomonium in the quark-gluon plasma. We then compute the nuclear modification factors and double ratios for bottomonium $Υ(1S)$, $Υ(2S)$, and $Υ(3S)$ states in Pb-Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}} = 5.02$ TeV. We compare our ML-induced results with those from the original KSU model and with experimental measurements from ALICE, ATLAS, and CMS collaborations. We find that the machine-learned Debye mass leads to improved agreement with data, particularly for excited states, highlighting the utility of machine learning in modeling in-medium QCD effects.

</details>


### [134] [Electromagnetic structure of strange vector mesons in nuclear medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06938)
*Parada T. P. Hutauruk,Terry Mart,Kazuo Tsushima*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在零温对称核物质中，利用Schwinger固有时间NJL模型研究了K*+介子的电荷、磁和四极矩电磁形状因子的介质修正，发现其随核密度增加而压低，并给出正常核密度下的电荷半径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解矢量介子（特别是含奇异夸克的K*+）在核介质中的电磁结构演化，检验夸克层次模型对强子介质效应的描述能力。

Method: 采用Schwinger固有时间正则化的Nambu-Jona-Lasinio模型，在夸克层次上自洽处理核介质效应与K*+电磁形状因子，计算其在对称核物质中的G_C^*(Q²)、G_M^*(Q²)和G_Q^*(Q²)。

Result: K*+的三个电磁形状因子均随核密度升高而系统性压低；在饱和核密度ρ₀下，其电荷半径r_{K*+}^* = 0.74 fm，略小于ρ⁺介子的r_{ρ⁺}^* ≈ 0.75 fm。

Conclusion: K*+介子的内部电磁结构在核介质中发生显著修饰，表明其夸克组分受介质影响强烈；NJL模型能自洽描述含奇异夸克矢量介子的介质依赖电磁性质。

Abstract: We investigate the in-medium modifications of the charge (electric) $G_C^{*}(Q^2)$, magnetic $G_M^{*}(Q^2)$, and quadrupole $G_Q^{*}(Q^2)$ form factors of the positively charged vector meson $K^{*+}$ in symmetric nuclear matter at zero temperature within the Schwinger proper-time Nambu-Jona-Lasinio (NJL) model. In this framework, both the nuclear medium effects and the electromagnetic structure of the $K^{*+}$ meson are described consistently in the NJL model at the quark level. We find that the charge, magnetic, and quadrupole form factors are suppressed with increasing nuclear density, indicating substantial in-medium modifications of the strange vector meson's internal structure. We further obtain a charge radius of $r_{K^{*+}}^{*}=0.74~\mathrm{fm}$ at normal nuclear density, which is slightly smaller than the corresponding value for the $ρ^{+}$ meson, $r_{ρ^{+}}^{*}\simeq0.75~\mathrm{fm}$.

</details>


### [135] [Exploring the $P$-wave bottom-strange molecular pentaquarks with the complex scaling method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07068)
*Yuhang Zheng,Qing-Fu Song,Xiaonu Xiong*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了P波Σ_b K/Λ_b K^*/Σ_b K^* 与 Σ_b ar{K}/Λ_b ar{K}^*/Σ_b ar{K}^* 分子态系统，预测多个I(J^P)量子数组合下的共振态，包括窄宽分子态与紧致态，为LHCb寻找底-奇分子五夸克提供理论指引。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索可能存在的底-奇（bottom-strange）分子型五夸克态，填补重子谱中P波正宇称扇区的理论空白，并与前期负宇称研究形成对比。

Method: 采用耦合通道方法，在有效拉氏量框架下结合动量截断正则化处理紫外发散，求解Lippmann-Schwinger方程以获取散射振幅及极点位置。

Result: 在I(J^P)=1/2(1/2^+) Σ_b K/Λ_b K^*/Σ_b K^* 系统中发现略高于Σ_b K阈值的窄共振；在I(J^P)=1/2(3/2^+) Λ_b K^*/Σ_b K^* 中发现分子型共振候选者；I=3/2扇区中Σ_b K^* 子系统存在1/2^+与3/2^+共振但尺寸较紧凑；反奇异系统中I(J^P)=1/2(1/2^+)呈现截断敏感共振，I(J^P)=1/2(3/2^+)中存在低于Σ_b \bar{K}^* 阈值的宽分子共振。

Conclusion: P波正宇称底-奇强子分子态以共振为主导，部分具有明显分子结构特征，可作为LHCb未来实验搜寻的优先目标。

Abstract: In this work, we perform a systematic investigation of the $P$-wave $Σ_b K/Λ_b K^{*}/Σ_b K^{*}$ and $Σ_b\bar{K}/Λ_b\bar{K}^{*}/Σ_b\bar{K}^{*}$ systems for all possible $I(J^P)$ combinations. In contrast to our earlier study of the negative-parity sector [Eur. Phys. J. C \textbf{85}, 1026 (2025)], the positive-parity sector is dominated by resonances. For the coupled-channel $I(J^P)=1/2(1/2^+)$ $Σ_b K/Λ_b K^*/Σ_b K^*$ system, we predict a narrow resonance above the $Σ_b K$ threshold. In the $I(J^P)=1/2(3/2^+)$ $Λ_b K^*/Σ_b K^*$ system, a molecular resonance candidate is found above the $Λ_b K^*$ threshold. For the higher-isospin $I=3/2$ sector, resonances are identified in the $Σ_b K^*$ subsystem for both $J^P=1/2^+$ and $3/2^+$ cases, though both states exhibit relatively compact sizes. We also extend our analysis to the $Σ_b\bar{K}/Λ_b\bar{K}^{*}/Σ_b\bar{K}^{*}$ systems, where a cutoff-sensitive resonance is found in the $I(J^P)=1/2(1/2^+)$ configuration. A broad molecular resonance candidate is found below the $Σ_b\bar{K}^*$ threshold in the $I(J^P)=1/2(3/2^+)$ system. We hope that these predictions provide useful guidance for future experimental searches for bottom-strange molecular pentaquarks by LHCb.

</details>


### [136] [Majoron-driven spontaneous leptogenesis in type-II seesaw model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07130)
*Eung Jin Chun,Tae Hyun Jung,Jin Sun*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了II型跷跷板模型中自发轻子生成（spontaneous leptogenesis）的最小实现，通过旋转Majoron背景驱动等离子体偏离平衡态，利用衰变与逆衰变过程产生轻子与Higgs不对称性；完整推导了非平衡演化Boltzmann方程，区分了freeze-out与freeze-in机制；发现不对称性主要由三重态分支比决定、几乎与三重态质量无关，并在任一分支被压低时按小分支比的平方根标度——体现U(1)_L对称性保护；同时证明无质量末态极限下Majoron背景不诱导CP不对称衰变，其效应可忽略；最终确立该机制为无需额外标量三重态或显式CP破坏的简洁稳健替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻求无需显式CP破坏项和额外标量自由度的轻子数生成机制，以规避传统热型II型轻子生成（thermal type-II leptogenesis）对精细调参与新物理尺度的依赖。

Method: 构建含标量三重态Δ与Majoron场J的type-II seesaw有效理论，在旋转Majoron背景（⟨J⟩ ∼ e^{iθ(t)}）下计算非平衡动力学；建立包含轻子、Higgs及Δ粒子密度矩阵的完整Boltzmann方程组；解析求解准稳态近似并数值模拟演化轨迹，区分freeze-in（小耦合）与freeze-out（大耦合）区域；分析CP不对称参数ε_Δ在Majoron背景下的行为，特别考察无质量末态极限。

Result: 1) 轻子与Higgs不对称性η_L, η_H ≈ √(Br_ℓ) 或 √(Br_H)，由较小分支比主导；2) 不对称性对三重态质量m_Δ呈弱依赖（仅通过热分布与反应率间接影响）；3) Majoron背景在m_ℓ, m_H → 0极限下不产生CP不对称衰变（Im[ℳ*ℳ'] = 0），故其贡献可忽略；4) 存在清晰的freeze-in主导参数区，满足观测重子不对称性η_B ∼ 10⁻¹⁰。

Conclusion: 自发轻子生成在minimal type-II seesaw框架下自洽可行，其鲁棒性源于U(1)_L对称性保护与动力学冻结机制，构成热型leptogenesis的简约替代范式，且不引入新CP源或额外标量。

Abstract: We investigate a minimal realization of spontaneous leptogenesis in the type-II seesaw model, where the lepton and Higgs asymmetries are generated as decay and inverse-decay processes drive the plasma toward the displaced equilibrium dictated by a rotating Majoron background. We derive the complete set of Boltzmann equations governing the evolution of asymmetric particle densities and analytically and numerically identify the freeze-out and freeze-in regimes. The resulting asymmetries depend primarily on the triplet branching fractions and are nearly independent of the triplet mass. The asymmetry scales as the square root of the smaller branching fraction when either decay mode into leptons or Higgs fields is suppressed, reflecting the symmetry structure; the lepton number is conserved once either coupling is turned off. We also show that the Majoron background does not induce CP-asymmetric decay of the scalar triplet in the massless final state limit, and thus its impact is negligible. Our results establish spontaneous leptogenesis as a simple and robust alternative to thermal type-II leptogenesis that requires neither additional scalar triplets nor explicit CP-violating interactions.

</details>


### [137] [A Dark-Dimension Origin of Geometric Inelastic Dark Matter: The LUX-ZEPLIN High-Recoil Event and Multi-Target Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07138)
*Waqas Ahmed,George K. Leontaris*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于五维暗维度框架的伪狄拉克电弱二重态暗物质模型，利用几何机制自然生成~360 keV的质量劈裂，解释LZ实验中248 keV核反冲事例，并给出氙、钨靶上可观测的非弹性散射信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验在~248 keV核反冲能区观测到的单事件异常，该事例支持吸热型（endothermic）暗物质模型，尤其要求暗物质质量劈裂δ ~ O(100) keV量级。

Method: 构建五维‘暗维度’模型：SM与向量样电弱二重态暗物质局域在可见膜上；暗数破缺由中性体模（neutral bulk state）传递，导致指数压制的质量劈裂δ = δ_UV e^{-2πMR}；计算中性流主导的非弹性核反冲截面；采用两区间泊松统计检验LZ扩展反冲谱。

Result: 拟合得到浅最小值点(m_χ, δ) ≈ (1.35 TeV, 366 keV)，热遗存所偏好的点(1.1 TeV, 360 keV)仅Δχ² ≈ 0.21更高；CaWO₄中钨核在95–150 keV区段积分事例率为3.15×10⁻² events kg⁻¹ yr⁻¹；该模型在轻核靶（如Ge、Si）不可见，但在Xe和W靶中可检验。

Conclusion: 伪狄拉克劈裂的几何起源（即额外维尺度MR）可自然解释LZ高能反冲事例，并预言具有靶依赖性的非弹性散射特征信号，构成多靶直接探测的关键判据。

Abstract: The extended nuclear-recoil analysis of LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) has reported one event compatible with a recoil energy near $248$ keV, motivating endothermic dark-matter scenarios with support at large recoil energy. We study a pseudo-Dirac electroweak-doublet dark-matter model embedded in a five-dimensional Dark-Dimension framework. The Standard Model and the vectorlike electroweak doublets are localized on the visible brane, while dark-number violation is communicated through a neutral bulk state. The resulting mass splitting is exponentially suppressed, $δ=δ_{\rm UV}e^{-2πMR}$, providing a geometric origin for the few-hundred-keV scale relevant to inelastic scattering. The neutral weak current is dominantly off diagonal, fixing the neutron-scale normalization at $σ_{χn}^{Z}\simeq1.86\times10^{-39}\,\mathrm{cm^2}$. Using a two-bin Poisson diagnostic of the extended LZ recoil window, we find a shallow minimum near $(m_χ,δ)\simeq(1.35~\TeV,366~\keV)$, while the thermally motivated point $(1.1~\TeV,360~\keV)$ lies only $Δχ^2\simeq0.21$ higher. The same benchmark is kinematically inaccessible to light targets but remains open for xenon and tungsten, providing a characteristic multi-target test of the model. For CaWO$_4$ we obtain an integrated tungsten rate of $3.15\times10^{-2}$ events kg$^{-1}$ yr$^{-1}$ in the $95$--$150$ keV interval. These results connect the geometric origin of the pseudo-Dirac splitting with high-recoil direct-detection phenomenology.

</details>


### [138] [Probing Neutral Triple Gauge Couplings at e- p colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07168)
*Xue-Jia Cheng,Chong-Xing Yue,Ji-Chong Yang,Si-Tong Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在SMEFT框架下，利用FCC-he对中性三重规范耦合（nTGCs）进行探测，通过分析六类过程的信号与背景并优化事例筛选策略，给出了维度-8算符系数的单独及联合限制，表明e⁻p对撞机对nTGCs的灵敏度与当前LHC相当、优于CEPC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用模型无关的SMEFT框架，在e⁻p对撞机（特别是FCC-he）上拓展对新物理信号——尤其是中性三重规范耦合（nTGCs）——的探测能力。

Method: 在SMEFT框架下计算六类涉及nTGCs的过程在FCC-he上的信号截面与主要本底，设计针对性的事例筛选策略，并基于统计显著性估计各过程及组合对维度-8算符系数的95% CL上限约束。

Result: 获得各过程及联合分析对相关维度-8算符系数（如f_{T,0,1,2,5,7}等）的预期95%置信度上限；显示FCC-he对nTGCs的灵敏度与当前LHC实验相当，且优于CEPC。

Conclusion: e⁻p对撞机（尤其是FCC-he）是研究nTGCs和SMEFT维度-8算符的强有力平台，其互补性与竞争力凸显了多对撞机联合探针新物理的重要性。

Abstract: The Standard Model Effective Field Theory~(SMEFT) has attracted much attention as a model-independent way for probing new physical signals. In the SMEFT framework, $e^{-}p$ colliders can be used to study new interactions of gauge bosons, such as neutral Triple Gauge Couplings~(nTGCs). We study the signals and backgrounds of six different processes at the Future Circular Collider-hadron electron~(FCC-he) and propose different event selection strategies. The expected constraints on the coefficients of the dimension-8 operators for each process, as well as the combined constraints are obtained. The results show that the sensitivity of $e^{-}p$ colliders to nTGCs is similar to the current Large Hadron Collider~(LHC) experiments and is more competitive than that of Circular Electron Positron Collider~(CEPC) experiments.

</details>


### [139] [Hadronic heavy neutral lepton decays to the limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07189)
*Jonathan L. Schubert,Babette Döbrich*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了重性中性轻子（HNL）在强子图像下的多体末态衰变结构，并提出基于强子系统不变质量实现强子描述与部分子描述的无缝过渡；该方法不仅影响HNL总寿命，还产生具有实验探测价值的多强子末态信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HNL是一类假想粒子，旨在同时解决标准模型中的多个未解难题（如中微子质量起源、重子不对称性等）。

Method: 采用强子图像分析HNL的多体强子末态衰变，并通过强子系统的不变质量建立其与部分子图像的连续对应关系。

Result: 发现多强子衰变道在该框架下显著贡献于HNL总衰宽，并可形成独特且可观测的实验信号（如特定强子多重数、不变质量谱结构）。

Conclusion: 强子化多体衰变是HNL物理中不可忽略的关键效应，其系统描述为未来强度前沿实验（如SHiP、DUNE近探测器）提供了新的理论依据与现象学判据。

Abstract: Heavy Neutral Leptons are a class of hypothetical particle, motivated by simultaneously solving multiple of the standing issues of the Standard Model. In this work we investigate the decay structure of this type of particle in the hadronic picture for multi-body final states. We propose to link the resulting hadronic description to a partonic description in a seamless transition based on the hadron system's invariant mass. Beyond the impact on the total HNL lifetime, we will show that the multi-hadron channels emerging in this description can constitute interesting experimental signatures.

</details>


### [140] [Chiral Doubling, Renormalization Group Fixed Points, and Dense Matter Equations of State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07190)
*Chihiro Sasaki*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于RG固定点的自上而下外推范式，作为统一描述强子谱学、致密核物质与致密星物理的有效场论框架；通过在RG固定点构建有效拉氏量并引入最小对称性破缺，成功再现真空中的重轻介子宇称双重态谱，并协调2倍太阳质量中子星与引力波观测约束，进一步推广至夸克-强子混合图像以阐明逐级退禁闭、星核稳定性及重子数涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一描述从真空强子谱到致密星内部极端条件下的强相互作用物理，弥合QCD非微扰动力学与天体观测之间的理论鸿沟。

Method: 在重整化群（RG）紫外/红外固定点处直接构造手征对称且满足适当对称性破缺模式的有效拉氏量，结合矢量介子圈图、行走型矢量耦合与chiral-invariant核子质量m0，在真空与高密环境下进行系统外推；并拓展至夸克-强子混合EFT框架。

Result: 1) 真空中自然重现重轻介子宇称双重态谱；2) 致密物质中通过m0与行走矢量耦合协同满足2M⊙中子星质量与LIGO/Virgo引力波潮汐形变约束；3) 混合框架下揭示逐级退禁闭序、星核力学稳定性及重子数关联长度标度行为。

Conclusion: RG固定点不仅是EFT的自然起点，更构成连接QCD深层对称性与宏观致密星观测量的普适组织原理；该范式为强相互作用多尺度问题提供了统一、可计算且可证伪的理论架构。

Abstract: We review a top-down fixed-point-extrapolation paradigm as a unified effective field theory framework for hadronic spectroscopy, dense baryonic matter, and compact star physics. By formulating our effective Lagrangian directly at renormalization group (RG) fixed points and introducing minimal symmetry breaking, we extrapolate the theory back to physical environments. In the vacuum, this framework naturally reproduces the heavy-light meson parity-doubling spectrum via light vector meson loops. In dense matter, the interplay between the chiral-invariant nucleon mass m0 and walking vector couplings reconciles gravitational-wave constraints with $2M_\odot$ massive neutron stars. Generalization to a quark-hadron hybrid approach further illuminates sequential deconfinement, core stability, and baryon number fluctuations, demonstrating how underlying RG fixed points dictate hadronic dynamics from vacuum to neutron star interiors.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Interpreting the $248$~keV nuclear-recoil candidate reported by LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) as an endothermic transition needs two ingredients usually put in by hand: a stable dark state, and a coherent current that is purely off diagonal. Gauging baryon number supplies both. For the minimal dark charges $B(χ_L)=-3/2$ and $B(χ_R)=-9/2$, the scalars that generate the dark masses leave an exact residual parity $P=(-1)^{6B}$ stabilizing the lightest state, while the same Majorana masses make the vector current transition dominated as an identity rather than a choice. At $m_χ=1$~TeV and $δ=300$~keV the model is over-determined: the observed LZ interval, a leading-order relic estimate and the LHC dijet limit meet at the single perturbatively acceptable point $(M_{Z_B},g_B)\simeq(1.44~\mathrm{TeV},0.233)$. That point is allowed by currently existing searches but sits just below the trigger-level dijet boundary, so it is alive and not yet decided--and a re-run of that analysis on the Run-3 data at $\sqrt s=13.6$~TeV, already on tape, will exclude it or find it. Nor is the interpretation confined to that splitting: at $δ=200$~keV, the same construction predicts a heavier mediator at comparable coupling, $M_{Z_B}\simeq2.4$-$3.0$~TeV with $g_B\simeq0.15$-$0.34$, a range the HL-LHC dijet program will probe.

Method: 基于热力学导向构建的全息AdS/QCD模型，重构味依赖的规范动能函数；采用全息色散关系与边界对关联函数提取谱函数；结合Kubo公式从矢量电流关联函数导出电导率。

Abstract: We investigate the properties of $ρ$, $ψ$, and $Υ$ mesons within a holographic AdS/QCD framework, capturing both their vacuum phenomenology and in-medium dynamics. By reconstructing flavor-specific gauge kinetic functions within a model originally developed to study thermodynamic functions, we compute the vector-meson mass spectrum, the decay constants and the hadronic vacuum polarization contributions to the anomalous magnetic moment of the muon at zero temperature. Furthermore, we evaluate the thermal spectral functions to analyze mass shifts, resonance broadening, and the melting of vector bound states in a hot and dense medium. Finally, using the Kubo formula, we extract the electrical conductivity of the plasma as a function of temperature and chemical potential.

</details>

Conclusion: Regge轨迹与Collins-Soper核具有深层一致性，表明高能散射与TMD因子化共享同一快度重整化群结构，支持构建统一的跨尺度因子化框架。

Abstract: The relationship between Regge factorization of amplitudes and the Collins-Soper-Sterman factorization of hadronic cross sections is explored. Seeking a unified description of rapidity divergences in high-energy scattering and transverse-momentum-dependent factorization, a connection emerges between the Collins-Soper kernel and the Regge trajectory. This provides a framework for bridging high-energy and low-transverse-momentum resummation and for future extensions of $k_T$-factorization beyond leading-logarithmic accuracy.

</details>


### [144] [Baryon number freeze-out in the Standard Model, precisely](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07467)
*Cristina Benso,Dietrich Bödeker,Kohei Kamada,Kyohei Mukaida,Laura Sagunski,Philipp Schicho,Kai Schmitz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了跨越电弱交叉区域的重子数演化的Boltzmann方程，计入高阶修正，得到两个sphaleron转换因子：C_sph = 0.3328(5)（对应B−L）和F_sph = 0.0279(19)（对应味加权轻子荷）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准平衡计算在对称相与破缺相中常假设温度恒定，忽略了希格斯期望值与sphaleron率随温度变化的事实；本文旨在更精确地刻画电弱交叉过程中sphaleron转换对重子不对称性的贡献。

Method: 建立并求解跨越电弱交叉区域的重子数Boltzmann方程，同时计入大正则配分函数的高阶修正与微扰希格斯期望值的高阶修正。

Result: 获得两个sphaleron转换因子：C_sph = 0.3328(5)（守恒量B−L）与F_sph = 0.0279(19)（按带电轻子Yukawa耦合加权的味荷）；表明即使B−L=0，味区分的轻子不对称性仍可生成可观测重子不对称性。

Conclusion: 电弱交叉的动力学不可忽略，精确处理希格斯场演化与sphaleron速率温度依赖性对重子生成机制（尤其是味相关机制）至关重要；F_sph ≠ 0意味着B−L守恒下仍存在有效的重子产生通道。

Abstract: Weak sphaleron transitions turn a lepton asymmetry of the Standard Model plasma in the early Universe into a baryon asymmetry, conserving baryon-minus-lepton number $B-L$ and its individual flavored charges. A baryon asymmetry can thus also arise from flavored lepton asymmetries with vanishing $B-L$. Standard equilibrium calculations in the symmetric and broken phases are performed at constant temperature and hence neglect the fact that both the Higgs expectation value and the sphaleron rate vary as functions of temperature across the electroweak crossover. We derive a Boltzmann equation for the baryon number evolution across the crossover and calculate the freeze-out abundance including higher-order corrections to both the grand canonical partition function and the perturbative Higgs expectation value. This yields two sphaleron conversion factors: $C_\text{sph} = 0.3328(5)$ for $B-L$ and $\mathcal{F}_\text{sph} = 0.0279(19)$ for the flavored charges weighted by the charged-lepton Yukawa couplings.

</details>

Method: 采用A4×Z4 flavor symmetry与generalized CP symmetry联合框架；引入两个A4三重态flavon Φ和Ψ实现A4破缺，一个A4 singlet flavon χ通过复四次项χΦΨ²打破GCP并调控畴壁张力；结合群论约束、有效算符分析与畴壁网络数值模拟，完成中微子质量矩阵构造与宇宙学演化计算。

Result: 全局拟合成功再现所有中微子振荡参数；预测Dirac CP相δCP = 270°（即−90°），无中微子双贝塔衰变有效质量⟨mββ⟩ ≈ 15 meV；畴壁消湮产生随机引力波背景，峰值频率f_peak ∝ sinθ13，幅度h²Ω_GW ∝ sin⁻⁴θ13，落在PTA与LISA敏感频段。

Conclusion: 该模型在不引入额外手征场或大能标机制下统一解释θ13起源、CP破坏及轻子混合角，且将低能中微子现象与早期宇宙拓扑缺陷引力波信号直接关联，构成可证伪的‘中微子-引力波’交叉预言。

Abstract: We extend the Standard Model with an $A_4 \times Z_4$ flavor symmetry and generalized CP (GCP) symmetry, realized through two flavon triplets that spontaneously break $A_4$ and generate the observed lepton mixing pattern. A new $A_4$-singlet flavon couples to one of these triplets through a complex quartic interaction that breaks GCP and fixes the reactor mixing angle; this same coupling biases the domain walls formed when that triplet breaks $A_4$. A global fit to oscillation data reproduces all mixing angles and mass splittings, predicting a maximal Dirac CP phase of $270^\circ$ and an effective Majorana mass near $15\text{ meV}$, testable by upcoming neutrinoless double-beta-decay ($0νββ$) experiments. The same coupling generates a stochastic gravitational-wave background peaking in the pulsar-timing-array and LISA bands, with peak frequency and amplitude predicted to scale as $\sinθ_{13}$ and $\sin^{-4}θ_{13}$, a falsifiable link between neutrino oscillation and gravitational-wave (GW) observations.

</details>


### [146] [Boosted dark particles and the LZ nuclear recoil event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07742)

Main category: hep-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LZ实验中观测到的248 keV异常核反冲事件，该事件无法由标准自相互作用/自旋无关的银河晕暗物质模型解释，尤其因低能区未见伴随事例而排除常规弹性或轻质量暗物质模型。

Method: 采用弹性散射框架，结合氘核断裂约束（来自SNO实验）限制暗区粒子质量下限（>1 GeV），并利用动量转移依赖的截面行为（如赝标-赝标算符导致dσ/dE_R ∝ q^2）解释高能事例孤立性；通过核响应函数与探测器靶核（Xe, Ar, D, ^3He等）的自旋/质量特性对比分析信号特征。

Result: 确定暗区粒子质量范围为1–74 GeV、动量≥123 MeV；排除自旋无关SI相互作用与轻质量（<1 GeV）暗物质；支持动量增强型相互作用（如g_p χ̄iγ5χ N̄iγ5N）；预言在液氩探测器中无显著信号、年调制幅度<1%，并在^3He或氟等自旋非零轻核中可观测反冲。

Conclusion: LZ异常事例可由弹性散射的GeV尺度暗区粒子解释，前提是其与核子耦合由动量增强型赝标算符主导；该模型自然规避低能事例缺失问题，且具有可证伪的探测学预言，区别于传统inelastic DM或heavy SI DM解释。

Abstract: The LZ experiment reported one anomalous event with nuclear recoil energy of 248 keV. Galactic halo dark matter (DM) cannot produce such a large energy unless heavier than 74 GeV, and heavy DM with spin-independent interactions would have produced unseen recoils at lower energy. Inelastic DM has been proposed as a way out. We consider, instead, an elastic collision of a dark sector particle with a momentum of at least 123~MeV and mass in the range 1-74 GeV as deuteron break-up in SNO disfavors sub-GeV masses. The absence of accompanying lower-energy recoils favours interactions whose rate grows with the momentum transfer, such as a pseudoscalar-pseudoscalar nucleon operator. This implies no leading-order signal in argon, an annual modulation below 1 %, and recoils in spinful light nuclei where inelastic halo DM gives nothing.

</details>


### [147] [Not so good $ν$s for Higgsino dark matter as LZ excess: stringent limits from Super-Kamiokande and IceCube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07807)
*Debajit Bose,Akash Kumar Saha,Deep Jyoti Das,Rinchen Sherpa,Abhijeet Singh,Durba Ghosh,Jaya Doliya,Subhadip Bouri,Biprajit Mondal,Ranjini Mondol,Nirmal Raj,Ranjan Laha*

TL;DR: LZ实验观测到一个250 keV核反冲事例，被解释为具有~few×100 keV中性态质量劈裂的超对称Higgsino暗物质的非弹性散射；本文利用Super-K与IceCube的大气中微子数据，排除了质量劈裂<557 keV的热/非热Higgsino暗物质，从而对标准晕速度分布下LZ事例的Higgsino解释构成强约束。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验LZ实验报道的单事件是否可由Higgsino暗物质非弹性散射解释，并通过太阳捕获-湮灭-中微子信号施加独立天体物理约束。

Method: 基于Higgsino在太阳中通过重核（如O、Fe）散射被捕获，随后湮灭至WW/ZZ并产生高能中微子的物理图像，结合Super-Kamiokande与IceCube对大气ν_e和ν_μ通量的测量，计算并排除相应参数空间。
