Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) collaboration has recently reported a single nuclear recoil event at a high recoil energy of about 250 keV. This has been interpreted as inelastic scattering of dark matter that is a supersymmetric Higgsino with a mass splitting between the neutral states of a few 100 keV. Such dark matter may be captured at high recoil in the Sun through scattering on heavy elements in it, and annihilate to $W^+W^-$ and $ZZ$, in turn giving rise to a neutrino flux detectable on Earth. Using measurements of atmospheric electron- and muon-neutrino fluxes by Super-Kamiokande and IceCube, we constrain thermal and non-thermal Higgsino dark matter, excluding inter-state mass splittings $\lesssim 557$ keV. This disfavors Higgsino-like interpretations of the LZ event for standard halo velocities.

</details>


### [148] [Higgsino Dark Matter Interpretation of the LZ High-Recoil Event in the GNMSSM with TeV-Scale Gauginos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07811)
*Subhadip Bisal,Junjie Cao,Fei Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨GNMSSM框架下Higgsino主导的中性微子暗物质通过Z玻色子介导的吸热散射过程（χ₁⁰N → χ₂⁰N），以解释LZ实验观测到的~248 keV核反冲事例；通过Higgsino–Singlino混合调控中性微子质量劈裂至~350 keV，同时允许gaugino质量仅在TeV量级（而非传统MSSM所需的10⁷ GeV），并满足宇宙学、直接探测及对撞机等多重约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验报道的~248 keV核反冲事例暗示可能存在吸热（endothermic）暗物质散射过程，即暗物质与核子碰撞时吸收能量跃迁至激发态（χ₁⁰→χ₂⁰），需中性微子质量差Δm ≈ 330–350 keV；而标准MSSM中实现该劈裂需超重gaugino（O(10⁷) GeV），与自然性矛盾，故需新机制。

Method: 在General Next-to-Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model（GNMSSM）框架下，构建Higgsino-dominated neutralino暗物质模型，引入Singlino-Higgsino混合以调控质量劈裂Δm = m(χ₂⁰) − m(χ₁⁰)；计算Z-交换主导的自旋相关（SD）吸热散射截面、热退耦 relic density（含slepton coannihilation）、Higgs物理、B物理及LHC约束；采用Standard Halo Model与extended-likelihood分析评估LZ数据拟合优度（Δχ²）。

Result: 给出6个满足全部实验约束的benchmark点：暗物质质量0.66–1.11 TeV，中性微子劈裂333–350 keV，gaugino质量2–5 TeV；所有点均精确复现Ωh² ≈ 0.12，且Δχ² < 1（相对于LZ最佳拟合）；直接探测（LZ/XENONnT）、Higgs信号强度、Bₛ→μμ、LEP/LHC sparticle搜索均无冲突。

Conclusion: GNMSSM通过Singlino-Higgsino混合提供自然机制，在避免超重gaugino的前提下实现亚MeV中性微子劈裂，从而兼容LZ高能核反冲事例；该方案在热历史、直接探测与对撞机物理间达成协调，但尚需定量评估太阳俘获率与中微子望远镜（如IceCube）的间接探测限制以最终判定其可观测性。

Abstract: The nuclear recoil event at approximately 248 keV reported by the LUX-ZEPLIN collaboration motivates an investigation of endothermic dark matter scattering. We study this within the General Next-to-Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model (GNMSSM), with Higgsino-dominated neutralino DM undergoing the $Z$-mediated transition $\widetildeχ_1^0N\to\widetildeχ_2^0N$. In the conventional thermal Higgsino limit of the MSSM, the observed relic abundance selects a mass near 1.1 TeV, while a neutralino splitting of a few hundred keV typically requires gaugino masses of order $10^7$ GeV. In the GNMSSM, Higgsino-Singlino mixing introduces an additional contribution to the splitting that can cancel the gaugino-induced contribution, allowing sub-MeV splitting with multi-TeV gauginos. This mixing also modifies the inelastic scattering coupling and annihilation rates, while coannihilation with sleptons provides freedom in obtaining the observed relic abundance. We present six benchmark points with dark-matter masses of 0.66-1.11 TeV, neutralino splittings of 333-350 keV, and gaugino masses of 2-5 TeV. These points reproduce the observed relic abundance and satisfy direct-detection, Higgs, flavor, and collider constraints. Within the Standard Halo Model and extended-likelihood analysis, all six points yield $Δχ^2<1$ relative to the best fit. Our results show how the GNMSSM can accommodate the LZ high-recoil event without an ultraheavy gaugino sector. A quantitative assessment of solar-capture and neutrino-telescope constraints remains necessary for establishing viability.

</details>


### [149] [Quantum-Geometric Meissner Effect in Magnetized Color Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07835)
*Kazuya Mameda,Noriyuki Sogabe*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现，在强磁场下的两味色超导（2SC）夸克物质中，朗道量子化导致横向准粒子色散被抑制，从而产生一种由朗道能级量子几何（特别是最低朗au能级的量子度规）主导的新型Meissner响应；该响应的质量标度由配对能隙而非化学势决定，其根源在于LLL的陈数所约束的量子度规，对磁星kHz准周期振荡具有潜在物理解释意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强磁场环境下致密夸克物质的电磁响应机制，尤其是传统费米面贡献被压制后量子几何效应如何主导超导Meissner效应，并探索其在磁星物理中的可观测表现。

Method: 基于色超导有效理论，结合强磁场下夸克的朗道量子化处理，计算横向规范场传播子的真空极化张量，提取Meissner质量；重点分析最低朗道能级（LLL）波函数的量子度规及其与陈数的拓扑关联。

Result: 发现横向Meissner质量在强场极限下由LLL量子度规主导，其标度行为为m_M ∝ Δ（配对能隙），而非常规2SC中的m_M ∝ μ（化学势）；该反常标度受LLL陈数C=1严格约束，体现量子几何与拓扑的协同作用。

Conclusion: 朗道量子化可诱导色超导物质中纯粹源于量子几何的Meissner响应，其拓扑保护的标度律为磁星高频振荡提供新物理解释路径，并拓展了超导电磁响应的量子几何范式。

Abstract: We find a quantum-geometric Meissner response in magnetized two-flavor color-superconducting (2SC) quark matter. Landau quantization quenches the transverse quasiparticle dispersion, suppressing the conventional Fermi-surface contribution and giving rise to a Meissner response governed by the quantum geometry of the Landau levels. In the strong-field regime, the response becomes dominated by the quantum metric of the lowest Landau level (LLL), and the leading scaling of the transverse Meissner mass is consequently set by the pairing gap, in contrast to the chemical potential scaling of conventional color superconductors. This unconventional scaling has a topological origin, as the LLL quantum metric is constrained by its Chern number. The reduced transverse Meissner mass provides potential implications for kHz quasi-periodic oscillations in magnetars.

</details>


### [150] [Initial momentum anisotropies in the kT-factorization of the CGC I: Gradient Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07847)
*Oscar Garcia-Montero*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了Color Glass Condensate（CGC）稀疏-稀疏极限下单包容胶子产生过程，指出传统的k⊥-因子化只是关于两个碰撞源横向位置梯度展开（以1/(Q_s R)为小参数）的零阶近似；通过系统收集各阶项，构建了一个包含标量与张量结构的扩展k⊥-因子化公式，显式保留源的空间依赖性及能量-动量张量的运动学矩；在实时间Glasm场计算中，该展开再现了偶极极限下的动量空间结果，并额外包含振幅与共轭之间的相干干涉效应，引入流响应、色电-磁干涉和纵向能流等新物理项；这些修正揭示了初态即存在的动量空间各向异性，可直接嵌入现有饱和初态模型，提升早期能量-动量张量的保真度而不破坏因子化结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统k⊥-因子化无法描述初态动量各向异性，且忽略源空间梯度与相干干涉效应；需建立一个既保持因子化形式又具动力学生成各向异性的系统性框架，以更准确刻画轻离子等稀疏、涨落主导体系的初始条件。

Method: 基于CGC有效理论，在稀疏-稀疏极限下对胶子产生振幅进行横向位置梯度展开（小参数为1/(Q_s R)），逐阶构造标量与张量结构；同时从未来光锥内经典Glasm场的实时间演化出发，推导对应动量空间谱，并在eikonal极限下比对验证，识别并分类由振幅-共轭干涉带来的高阶修正项。

Result: 得到一个扩展的k⊥-因子化公式，其每阶均为同一组无积分分布函数上的局域算符，包含源密度梯度、其横向导数及动能矩；明确识别出三类新物理贡献：(i) 流响应（flow responses），(ii) 色电-磁干涉（chromo-electric–magnetic interference），(iii) 纵向能量流（longitudinal energy flux），均受大出射动量|p| ≫ Q_s抑制；该层级结构自然给出初态动量各向异性，且其强度随系统稀疏度（即Q_s减小或R增大）增强。

Conclusion: k⊥-因子化是梯度展开的零阶近似；完整展开提供了首个兼具局域性、因子化形式与初态能量-动量张量完整结构的初态模型框架；所导出的梯度修正可直接植入现有饱和模型（如IP-Glasma、EKRT），为重离子碰撞初始条件提供动力学生成的各向异性与更精确的早期能量-动量张量，尤其适用于轻离子等涨落主导体系。

Abstract: We revisit single-inclusive gluon production in the dilute--dilute limit of the Color Glass Condensate and show that $k_\perp$-factorization is only the zeroth order of a systematic gradient expansion in the transverse positions of the two colliding sources, organized in powers of $1/(Q_sR)$, with $Q_s$ the saturation scale and $R$ the size over which the sources vary. Collecting terms order by order builds a ladder of scalar and tensor structures from the local distributions and their transverse derivatives, yielding an extended $k_\perp$-factorized formula that retains the spatial dependence of the sources and, through kinetic moments of the spectrum, the energy-momentum tensor. Repeating the expansion from a real-time computation of the classical Glasma fields in the future light cone reproduces the momentum-space result in the eikonal limit, but additionally retains coherent interference between amplitude and conjugate. This adds terms to the ladder (flow responses, chromo-electric--magnetic interference, and a longitudinal energy flux) further suppressed by powers of the large outgoing momentum $|\pp|\gg Q_s$. The hierarchy thus reveals momentum-space anisotropies present in the initial state before any hydrodynamic evolution, which leading $k_\perp$-factorization misses by construction. Since each order is a local operator on the same unintegrated distributions, these corrections can be added directly to existing saturation-based initial-state models, giving a dynamically generated initial momentum anisotropy and a more faithful early-time energy-momentum tensor without abandoning the tractable factorized form. Set by gradients of the transverse density profiles, they grow towards more dilute, fluctuation-dominated systems, e.g. light-ion collisions. Phenomenological consequences will be developed in a companion paper.

</details>


### [151] [Recent applications of the gradient flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07940)
*R. Harlander,A. Shindler*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了梯度流作为QCD中一种规范不变且O(4)对称的紫外截断方案，重点讨论其在算符乘积展开（OPE）框架下连接格点非微扰计算与微扰结果的能力；指出在给定OPE基下，流时间依赖的 flowed 与常规 Wilson 系数转换在各阶微扰下是精确的；并回顾了其在部分子分布函数、重介子观测量、夸克质量及强耦合常数定义等方面的应用，最后简述Ricci流在量子引力中的微扰形式及其对非高斯不动点的研究意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为弥合格点QCD非微扰计算与微扰理论之间的鸿沟，需一种兼具规范不变性、可微性与紫外正则化能力的新工具；梯度流因其良好的重整化性质与OPE兼容性成为理想候选。

Method: 基于Yang-Mills梯度流方程定义 flowed 场，结合微扰展开与OPE分析，推导 flowed 算符与原始算符之间Wilson系数的流时间依赖转换关系，并验证其在各阶微扰下的精确性；同时将该框架应用于多个物理可观测量的提取与定义。

Result: 证明在固定OPE算符基下，flowed与未flowed Wilson系数间的转换因子在任意微扰阶上完全由流时间t和尺度μ决定，且无残余截断误差；该结论支撑了梯度流作为系统性匹配工具的有效性，并已在PDFs、B介子衰变常数、MS-bar夸克质量及α_s(t)定义中实现高精度应用。

Conclusion: 梯度流不仅是一种稳健的紫外正则化方案，更构成了连接非微扰格点数据与微扰QCD的桥梁；其OPE兼容性与微扰精确性使其成为高精度强子物理与基础参数提取的关键工具；Ricci流的类比亦提示其在量子引力微扰研究中的潜在价值。

Abstract: The gradient flow provides a gauge- and O(4)-invariant ultraviolet regulator for QCD. This opens new opportunities for combining non-perturbative lattice calculations with perturbative results, in particular in the context of an operator-product expansion (OPE). We review the concepts behind this and argue that, within the operator basis of a given OPE, the flow-time dependence of the conversion between flowed and regular Wilson coefficients is exact at each perturbative order. Recent applications to parton distribution functions, heavy-meson observables, quark masses, and gradient-flow definitions of the strong coupling are discussed. Finally, the perturbative formulation of the Ricci flow in quantum gravity and its application to the study of non-Gaussian fixed points is reviewed.

</details>


### [152] [Near-Threshold Dynamics of \c{hi}c1(3872) and T+cc(3875) States via Effective Range Expansion and Monte Carlo Uncertainty Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08289)
*A. A. Atangana Likéné,A. Franck Rothen,J. M. Ema'a Ema'a,P. Pradyun Hebbar,Saralees Nadarajah,Tobias Golling,G. H. Ben-Bolie*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用有效程展开（ERE）和共振复合性关系，分析了X(3872)与Tcc+(3875)在阈值附近的结构，发现二者均具有大的负散射长度及高于95%的分子组分，支持其为强子分子态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清近阈值 exotic tetraquark 候选者 χ_{c1}(3872) 和 T_{cc}^+(3875) 的内部结构（紧致四夸克态 vs. 弱束缚分子态），检验有效程展开方法在强子谱学中的适用性与鲁棒性。

Method: 采用双通道有效程展开（ERE）结合CDD极点分析，结合共振复合性关系与蒙特卡洛误差传播（N=50000），从实验质量与宽度提取散射长度、有效程及分子复合性系数 X₂；系统考察减除常数、实验相关性与复合性定义变化的影响。

Result: χ_{c1}(3872) 散射长度 a = −8.49^{+0.93}_{−1.05} fm，T_{cc}^+ 为 a = −14.57^{+1.70}_{−1.66} fm；二者分子复合性 X₂ > 0.95；ERE+CDD分析排除了符合观测极点与自然背景的物理裸态贡献。

Conclusion: χ_{c1}(3872) 和 T_{cc}^+(3875) 极可能为松散束缚的强子分子态，而非紧致四夸克态；该结论在多种理论与实验系统误差下保持稳健，证实ERE是研究近阈值奇特强子的可靠工具。

Abstract: We investigate the near-threshold structure of the exotic tetraquark candidates $χ_{c1}(3872)$ and $T_{cc}^+(3875)$ using the effective-range expansion (ERE) and resonance compositeness relations. From the experimental masses and widths, we extract scattering lengths, effective ranges, and molecular compositeness coefficients within a two-channel framework, with uncertainties propagated through $N=50000$ Monte Carlo samples. We find large negative scattering lengths, $a=-8.49^{+0.93}_{-1.05}$ fm for $χ_{c1}(3872)$ and $a=-14.57^{+1.70}_{-1.66}$ fm for $T_{cc}^+$, and dominant molecular components $X_2>0.95$. The results are robust against variations of compositeness, experimental correlations, and the loop-function subtraction constant. An explicit ERE+CDD-pole analysis finds no physically reasonable bare-state contribution consistent with the observed poles and a natural background. Our results provide strong evidence for predominantly molecular structures of both states and demonstrate the robustness of the ERE approach to near-threshold exotic hadrons.

</details>


### [153] [Top-quark flavor-changing neutral currents and charged-lepton flavor violation in a generational three-Higgs-doublet model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08350)
*Hao-Ran Ma,Ti-Bin Hou,Yu-Ju Peng,Jin-Lei Yang,Tai-Fu Feng*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在CP守恒的代际三希格斯二重态模型（G3HDM）中系统研究了顶夸克味改变中性流衰变、带电轻子味破坏过程及相干μ-e转换，考虑树图与单圈贡献；通过多约束筛选4839个参数点，给出t→ch₁、h₁→μτ分支比及μ-e转换率上限，并指出其可被下一代实验探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超出标准模型的新物理信号，特别是通过味物理可观测量（如FCNC、LFV和μ-e转换）检验CP守恒G3HDM模型的可行性与可证伪性。

Method: 在G3HDM框架下，计算树图主导的味改变中性希格斯耦合所驱动的t→ch₁、h₁→μτ，以及单圈与核子效应共同决定的μ-e转换率；结合真空稳定性、幺正性、Higgs质量与信号强度、电弱精密数据、UTfit 2025中性介子混合、B→Xₛγ与Bₛ→μ⁺μ⁻等多重实验约束进行参数空间扫描。

Result: 筛选出4839个满足全部约束的参数点；得到Br(t→ch₁) ≤ 1.04×10⁻⁵，Br(h₁→μτ) ≤ 4.83×10⁻⁴，CR(μAl→eAl) ≈ 4.95×10⁻¹⁵，CR(μAu→eAu) ≈ 1.05×10⁻¹⁴；μ-e转换率上限处于下一代实验（如Mu2e、COMET）灵敏度范围内。

Conclusion: G3HDM在满足当前所有实验约束前提下，仍可产生可观测的味破坏信号；t→ch₁与h₁→μτ由树图FCNH耦合主导并显著增强；μ-e转换是该模型关键探针，其信号有望在近未来实验中被发现。

Abstract: In this work, we systematically investigate top-quark flavor-changing neutral-current decays, charged-lepton flavor-violating processes, and coherent $μ-e$ conversion in the CP-conserving generational three-Higgs-doublet model (G3HDM), including the relevant tree-level and one-loop contributions. The 4839-point sample passes conservative sufficient vacuum-stability conditions, necessary perturbative-unitarity preselection cuts, Higgs-mass and signal-strength constraints, electroweak precision data, the UTfit 2025 neutral-meson-mixing ranges, $\overline{B}\to X_sγ$, and $B_s\toμ^+μ^-$. In the scanned region, $\mathrm{Br}(t\to c h_1)\leq 1.04\times10^{-5}$ and $\mathrm{Br}(h_1\toμτ)\leq 4.83\times10^{-4}$, while the maximal conversion rates are $\mathrm{CR}(μ\mathrm{Al}\to e\mathrm{Al})\approx 4.95\times10^{-15}$ and $\mathrm{CR}(μ\mathrm{Au}\to e\mathrm{Au})\approx 1.05\times10^{-14}$. The dominant collider signals are mainly driven by tree-level flavor-changing neutral-Higgs couplings, which determine the relative strengths of these processes and induce the pronounced enhancement of $t\to c h_1$ and $h_1\toμτ$. Meanwhile, the upper range of the predicted $μ-e$ conversion rate in aluminum lies within the sensitivity reach of next-generation $μ-e$ conversion experiments.

</details>


### [154] [Light cone distributions of $P$-wave quarkonia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08393)
*Yang Li,Tianyang Hu,Xianghui Cao,Meijian Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在BLFQ框架下研究P波夸克偶素的领头扭度分布振幅，发现其分布呈现宏观上符合非相对论预期的形状，但相对论效应引入了关键结构特征，特别是轴矢量介子中出现的新型'W'形结构，源于相对论诱导的S/D分波，无法由非相对论动力学解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受对P波夸克偶素光锥分布 renewed interest 的驱动，旨在获取适用于硬排它过程分析的非微扰输入，并理解现代高能对撞机上重夸克偶素产生与结构的相对论效应。

Method: 采用基态光前量子化（Basis Light-Front Quantization, BLFQ）形式主义，计算P波夸克偶素（特别是轴矢量态）的领头扭度分布振幅（leading-twist distribution amplitudes）。

Result: 提取的分布振幅宏观形状符合非相对论极限预期，但明确揭示出相对论效应导致的显著结构：轴矢量介子中出现由S/D分波混合引起的新型'W'形分布结构。

Conclusion: 相对论框架（如BLFQ）对准确刻画夸克偶素内部结构至关重要；所获分布振幅为硬排它过程（如γ*γ→P-wave quarkonium）提供关键非微扰输入，凸显非相对论近似在精确QCD唯象学中的局限性。

Abstract: Motivated by renewed interest in the light-cone distributions of $P$-wave quarkonia, we investigate the leading-twist distribution amplitudes of these states within the basis light-front quantization (BLFQ) formalism. While our extracted distributions exhibit macroscopic shapes consistent with expectations from the non-relativistic limit, we find that relativistic effects introduce critical structural features. Most notably, we observe novel ``W"-shaped structures in the distribution amplitudes of the axial vector mesons, which arise from relativistically induced $S/D$ partial waves and cannot be explained by non-relativistic dynamics. These findings provide essential non-perturbative inputs for analyzing hard exclusive processes and highlight the importance of relativistic frameworks for understanding heavy quarkonium production and structure at modern high-energy colliders.

</details>


### [155] [Time evolution of scalar condensate decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08420)
*Ayuki Kamada,Kodai Sakurai*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究标量场通过参数共振衰变到子粒子的过程，比较了参数共振方法与费曼图方法在长时间极限下的衰变速率一致性，并通过数值模拟和可解的Rabi模型分析了不同不稳定带的收敛行为及玻色/费米统计效应对衰变动力学的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解标量场（如暴胀子或模场）在宇宙学中通过参数共振产生粒子的动力学过程，特别是参数共振方法与微扰论（费曼图）结果在长时间极限下的一致性及其收敛机制。

Method: 数值求解子粒子相空间密度的时间演化；分析不同共振带（尤其是窄共振区）的收敛时间尺度；引入玻色型与费米型Rabi模型作为可解量子力学类比，解析对比两种方法的适用性与差异根源。

Result: 在窄共振区，最低阶不稳定带在数十个标量振荡周期内即收敛至费曼图结果，而高阶带收敛依赖于耦合强度；总衰宽主要由最低阶带主导，故其收敛与耦合无关；玻色Rabi模型中两种方法始终一致，费米Rabi模型中则在长时间出现偏差。

Conclusion: 参数共振方法在长时间极限下与费曼图方法一致的本质源于玻色统计的增强效应（Bose enhancement），而费米统计的阻塞效应（Pauli blocking）破坏该一致性；这揭示了量子统计在非平衡粒子产生中的根本作用。

Abstract: A scalar field, which oscillates coherently over the space and decays through production of daughter particles, plays an important role in cosmology. It was recently shown that the parametric-resonance and Feynman-diagrammatic approaches give the same decay rate for a Bosonic daughter particle at a large time duration. We study how the parametric-resonance result approaches to this asymptotic value, by numerically following the time evolution of the phase density of the daughter particle. We see a difference among different instability bands in the narrow-resonance regime. The lowest-order instability band approaches to the Feynman-diagrammatic result in tens of scalar oscillation periods, while the high-order instability bands approach dependently also on a coupling between the scalar and daughter particles. This would infer that the total decay width, to which the lowest-order instability band contributes most dominantly in the perturbation theory, approaches to the Feynman-diagrammatic result also independently of the coupling. To confirm it, we consider a simpler and analytically solvable quantum mechanical model: Rabi models. In the Bosonic Rabi model, the parametric-resonance and Feynman-diagrammatic results agree with each other for all the time. On the other hand, in the Fermionic Rabi model, they disagree at a large time duration. This difference would be attributed to Bose enhancement vs Pauli blocking.

</details>


### [156] [Nonperturbative functional renormalization group for Higgs-singlet models with physics-informed neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08470)
*Norimi Yokozaki*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理信息神经网络（PINN）和LPA'近似的非微扰函数重整化群（FRG）框架，用于求解Z₂对称实单态标准模型扩展在零温和有限温度下的Wetterich流方程，避免了传统多项式截断，实现了全尺度-场空间的连续、无网格有效势描述，并应用于电弱相变中的有限温有效势与两步bouncing action计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服传统FRG中低阶场展开截断的局限性，提升有限温下强耦合区域（如电弱相变）中有效势的非微扰精度与全局光滑性；同时解决PINN在高能物理偏微分方程求解中解的非唯一性与物理合理性问题。

Method: 采用LPA'近似下的Wetterich方程，以物理信息神经网络（PINN）参数化尺度依赖的有效势V_k(φ)，结合多域（scale & field）划分与导数匹配条件保证C¹连续性与数值稳定性；嵌入手征对称性、Z₂对称性及热场论边界条件；耦合常数与反常维数由独立计算的二圈微扰跑动提供；引入软一致性约束（soft consistency constraint），强制PINN解在符号与量级上贴近重求和微扰理论。

Result: 成功重构了T=0与T=100 GeV下Z₂单态扩展SM的有效势，获得一维温度切片与二维场空间重建；提取了两步bouncing action；PINN结果与重求和微扰理论及网格松弛求解器一致；证实软一致性约束为选择物理解的必要条件，且最终解残存对约束权重与解析热目标的依赖。

Conclusion: PINN+FRG是一种可行的非微扰工具，但其物理解的选择机制尚未完全自洽——软一致性约束虽必要，却引入人为自由度，暴露了当前基于监督式物理先验的FRG-PINN范式的核心缺陷：缺乏第一性原理确定的解唯一性准则。

Abstract: We develop a nonperturbative functional renormalization group framework within the LPA' to solve the Wetterich flow equation for the $Z_2$-symmetric real singlet extension of the Standard Model at zero and finite temperature, without a polynomial expansion of the scale-dependent effective potential, using a physics-informed neural network (PINN) representation. In contrast to conventional truncations based on low-order field expansions, our hybrid tree-level--plus--neural-network ansatz yields a continuous, mesh-free description of the effective potential over the full relevant field and scale range. As a proof of concept, we apply the framework to the finite-temperature effective potential relevant to the electroweak phase transition in this model: one-dimensional field-space slices at two benchmark temperatures, and a two-dimensional reconstruction at $T=100$ GeV from which a two-step bounce action is extracted. The renormalization group flow is implemented in a multi-domain setup in scale and field space with derivative matching conditions that ensure smoothness and numerical stability. Gauge and Yukawa sectors are incorporated via independently computed perturbative two-loop running couplings, and anomalous dimensions are included in the flow at the LPA' level. We benchmark the network against resummed perturbation theory and against a grid-based relaxation solver of the same equation. Finally, we introduce a soft consistency constraint that keeps the solution close, in sign and order of magnitude, to perturbation theory across the field-space domain; we find it necessary, rather than merely helpful, for selecting a physically sensible solution of the flow equation, and find that the converged result retains a residual dependence on this guidance -- through hand-tuned weights and an analytic thermal target -- which we identify as the central open problem.

</details>


### [157] [Gravitational transverse momentum dependent distributions for the gluons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08499)
*Kauship Saha,Dipansu Mishra,Dipankar Chakrabarti,Asmita Mukherjee*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在光锥形式下研究了自旋为0和1/2强子中规范不变胶子能量-动量张量（EMT）的动量空间结构，引入胶子引力横向动量分布（gravitational TMDs），并建立其与扭度-2和扭度-3胶子TMDs的联系；同时指出EMT特定分量可提取胶子平均动能四动量及强子内部力学性质（如压强与剪切分布）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强子内部胶子对能量-动量分布及机械性质（压强、剪切）的贡献，需构造规范不变的胶子EMT，并在光锥框架下实现其可观测量分解。

Method: 采用光锥量子色动力学（LCQCD）框架，对非局域胶子EMT进行协变参数化，定义胶子引力TMDs；通过算符乘积展开（OPE）与运动学投影，推导其与扭度-2/3胶子TMDs的解析关系；利用EMT的对称性与守恒性分析其物理分量的力学含义。

Result: 建立了胶子引力TMDs的完整参数化方案；明确了其与已知扭度-2（如$g_{1T}^{(1)}$）和扭度-3（如$f_{\perp}^{(1)}$）胶子TMDs的洛伦兹协变映射；证明EMT的$T^{++}$分量携带胶子平均动能信息，而$T^{ij}$（$i,j=1,2$）分量直接对应横向压强$ p(x_\perp) $与剪切力$ s_{ij}(x_\perp) $分布。

Conclusion: 胶子引力TMDs是连接胶子部分子结构与强子机械性质的关键桥梁；该工作为未来JLab 12 GeV、EIC实验中通过深度虚康普顿散射（DVCS）和胶子主导过程提取胶子压强/剪切分布提供了理论基础与可观测量定义。

Abstract: We investigate the momentum-space structure of the gauge-invariant gluon energy-momentum tensor (EMT) for spin-$0$ and spin-$\tfrac{1}{2}$ hadrons in the light-front formalism. We parameterize the nonlocal gluon EMT in terms of so-called gluon gravitational transverse-momentum-dependent distributions (gravitational TMDs) and derive their connections to twist-2 and twist-3 gluon TMDs. Moreover, we demonstrate that particular components of the gluon EMT encode information about the average kinetic four-momentum of gluons and the internal mechanical properties of hadrons-such as pressure and shear distributions.

</details>


### [158] [How to choose a good rational basis for elliptic Feynman integrals?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08875)
*Ekta Chaubey,Vasily Sotnikov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了多圈散射振幅中椭圆积分基底与有理函数基底之间是否存在普适且极小的变换关系，推广了基于椭圆广义领奇点构造基底的方法，并发现其微分方程非对角块的ε依赖具有普适结构，该结构在解耦变换下保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多圈振幅中椭圆积分参与的基底变换是否具有普适性与极小性，以统一处理含椭圆积分的微分方程系统。

Method: 基于椭圆广义领奇点（elliptic leading singularities）构造基底，分析其满足的有理微分方程的非对角块结构，并研究包含完全椭圆积分的代数变换对ε展开的保持性。

Result: 发现非对角微分方程块的ε依赖可组织为一种普适结构，且该结构在将解表示为迭代积分所需的解耦变换下保持不变。

Conclusion: 存在一种普适且结构稳定的ε依赖组织方式，支持椭圆广义领奇点基底作为处理含椭圆积分振幅的自然、最小基底。

Abstract: Representing multi-loop scattering amplitudes as linear combinations of multivalued transcendental functions with process-dependent rational coefficients has long been understood to be advantageous. In general, these transcendental functions satisfy a system of differential equations with coupled homogeneous blocks. When these coupled blocks can be removed through algebraic basis transformations, the relation between the rational and algebraic bases is universal and minimal. Here, we ask whether an analogous universal and minimal relation exists when decoupling requires transformations involving complete elliptic integrals. We elaborate on the method proposed in ref. arXiv:2504.20897, in which we suggested that a basis constructed using an elliptic generalization of leading singularities may provide an answer to this question. We extend this analysis to the off-diagonal blocks of the rational differential equations satisfied by the bases obtained through this construction. We find that the $ε$ dependence of these blocks can be organized into a universal structure that is preserved under the decoupling transformation used to express the solutions in terms of iterated integrals.

</details>


### [159] [A distance-independent constraint on the axion-electron coupling from RGB stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08504)
*Thomas Levasseur,Oscar Straniero,Andrea Caputo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过使用红巨星支尖端（TRGB）与红巨星支凸起（RGBB）之间的光度差这一距离无关的微分观测量，改进了轴子-电子耦合常数 g_ae 的天体物理限制；对三个中高金属丰度球状星团（47 Tuc、NGC 362、M5）的分析给出 95% 置信度上限 g13 ≡ g_ae/10⁻¹³ < 1.49，并在合理质量损失假设下排除 g13 > 7.5，该极限远超当前直接实验（如XENONnT）灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轴子-电子耦合约束受距离、星际消光、bolometric correction零点等系统误差影响显著；需发展更鲁棒的距离无关观测量以提升天体物理限制的可靠性。

Method: 采用TRGB与RGBB的bolometric magnitude差作为距离无关约束量；基于V/I波段测光数据转换为bolometric光度；用恒星演化模型验证RGBB光度再现性；结合蒙特卡洛模拟与最大似然分析传播观测与模型不确定性。

Result: 三簇联合分析得最大似然值 g13 = 0.8，95% C.L. 上限 g13 < 1.49；在合理恒星质量损失假设下，g13 > 7.5 被排除（因将导致水平支HB与渐近巨星支AGB消失，与观测矛盾），该上限比XENONnT等直接实验强一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: TRGB–RGBB微分方法显著提升了g_ae天体物理约束的稳健性；该框架可推广至更大球状星团样本，进一步收紧对轴子-电子耦合及超出标准模型物理的限制。

Abstract: Interest in axions and axion-like particles has resurged, driven by their role in solving the strong CP problem and their appeal as dark matter candidates. The luminosity of the tip of the red giant branch (TRGB) offers a key observable for probing these particle properties. This work aims to improve existing bounds on the axion-electron coupling by adopting a differential observable that is less sensitive to distance, interstellar extinction, the zero-point of bolometric corrections (BCs), and other systematic uncertainties. We use the bolometric magnitude difference between the TRGB and the RGB bump (RGBB) as a distance-independent constraint, applied to three globular clusters of intermediate-to-high metallicity: NGC 104 (47 Tuc), NGC 362, and NGC 5904 (M5). Using photometric catalogs of RGB stars, we determine V- and I-band magnitudes of both features and convert them to bolometric values. After validating that our stellar models reproduce the observed RGBB luminosity, we perform a maximum likelihood analysis with Monte Carlo simulations to propagate uncertainties and derive new bounds on the coupling. A combined analysis of the three clusters yields a maximum likelihood at g13 = gae/10^-13 = 0.8 and a 95% C.L. upper limit of 1.49. Although slightly less stringent than recent bounds from larger multi-cluster samples, this limit is substantially more robust. Under reasonable mass-loss assumptions, g13 > 7.5 is ruled out, as it predicts the disappearance of the HB and AGB phases routinely observed in globular clusters, a limit more than an order of magnitude stronger than current direct experimental bounds such as XENONnT. We demonstrate the effectiveness of this differential, distance-independent method for constraining physics beyond the Standard Model. Applying it to a wider sample of globular clusters would further refine the constraint on gae.

</details>


### [160] [Relativistic spatial distributions of transverse angular momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08902)
*Cédric Lorcé,Asmita Mukherjee,Ravi Singh,Ho-Yeon Won*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了先前关于横向总角动量二维空间分布的研究，系统分析了相对于不同相对论性中心（质量中心、能量中心、自旋中心）的横向角动量与横向boost分布，并推导了自旋-1/2体系在瞬时形式与光前形式下的横向平面分布及其洛伦兹演化行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义角动量密度的定义强烈依赖于所选枢轴（pivot），而不同相对论性中心（如质量中心、能量中心、自旋中心）具有不同的物理意义和协变性质；需厘清其对横向角动量与boost空间分布的影响，并统一瞬时形式与光前形式描述。

Method: 基于相对论性流与守恒律，构造以不同相对论性中心为参考点的广义角动量与boost密度算符；在自旋-1/2目标下，利用Wigner转动生成横向平面2D分布；结合纵向洛伦兹变换与光前坐标变换，建立瞬时形式与光前形式之间的映射关系。

Result: 得到了相对于canonical center、relativistic center-of-mass、center-of-energy及center-of-spin的横向总角动量与boost的显式2D分布；揭示了这些分布随纵向洛伦兹提升的形变规律；给出了对应的光前横向分布，并确立了其与瞬时形式间的精确对应关系。

Conclusion: 横向角动量与boost的空间结构具有显著的枢轴依赖性；canonical center与center-of-spin给出最简洁的自旋分离，而center-of-mass更自然地体现质心运动；光前分布可通过极限过程从瞬时形式导出，二者在横向平面上具有一致的物理内涵。

Abstract: In our previous work [C. Lorcé et al., Phys. Lett. B 868 (2025) 139792], we investigated the 2D spatial distributions of transverse total angular momentum, including orbital angular momentum and intrinsic spin, relative to the canonical center (or center of spin). In the present work, we extend this analysis in two directions. First, we study the corresponding transverse boost distributions relative to the canonical center. Second, since the definition of generalized angular momentum density depends crucially on the choice of pivot, we analyze how the spatial distributions of transverse total angular momentum and boost are modified when they are defined relative to different relativistic centers, viz.~the relativistic centers of mass, energy, and spin. Considering spin-1/2 targets, we derive the corresponding 2D spatial distributions in the transverse plane, and further investigate how the spatial patterns evolve under longitudinal Lorentz boosts. Additionally, we provide the corresponding light-front distributions in the transverse plane, establishing a clear connection between the instant-form and light-front descriptions of transverse angular momentum and boost.

</details>


### [161] [Hot QCD: transport coefficients in strong and weak coupling regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08521)
*Gabriele Parisi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在准粒子模型（QPM）框架下，利用新型N_f=3+1格点QCD数据，首次计算了剪切粘滞系数η和重夸克空间扩散系数D_s；采用树图级散射振幅、Fokker-Planck近似及双组分Chapman-Enskog方法，并与Green-Kubo方法结果比对，进而分析强/弱耦合下(2πT D_s)/(4πη/s)比值行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解QGP输运性质，特别是重夸克扩散与剪切粘滞在不同夸克味自由度（N_f=3 vs N_f=3+1）下的差异，并厘清强耦合与弱耦合极限下输运系数间的标度关系。

Method: 基于带质量胶子的准粒子模型，用树图级费曼图计算qq、qg、gg散射振幅；对D_s采用相对论Boltzmann方程的Fokker-Planck近似；对η/s发展双组分Chapman-Enskog展开；η则与Green-Kubo方法（随机化碰撞积分求解Boltzmann方程）交叉验证。

Result: 获得了N_f=3与N_f=3+1下D_s(T)与η(T)的温度依赖性；给出了η/s的双组分QPM预测；发现(2πT D_s)/(4πη/s)在中高温区偏离弱耦合极限（~1），趋近强耦合下界（~0.5），且N_f=3+1修正显著影响D_s但对η/s影响较弱。

Conclusion: QPM结合新lQCD约束可自洽描述重味输运与剪切粘滞；D_s与η/s受夸克风味数影响不对称；比值(2πT D_s)/(4πη/s)是判别QGP耦合强度的有效探针，其偏离1的程度反映非微扰动力学贡献。

Abstract: We evaluate the transport coefficients, namely the shear viscosity $η$ and the spatial diffusion coefficient of heavy quarks $D_s$, within a Quasi-Particle Model (QPM). In particular, for the first time this has been done by exploiting novel lQCD data at $N_f=3+1$. In such a framework, in which the gluons are massive, the interactions among quarks and gluons have been evaluated via the scattering matrices evaluated from tree level Feynman diagrams. As far as the evaluation of $D_s$ is concerned, the Fokker-Planck approximation for the relativistic Boltzmann equation has been employed for the diffusion of both charm and bottom quarks in either a $N_f=3$ and a $N_f=3+1$ bulk. We have developed a dual-component Chapman-Enskog formalism for the ratio $η/s$ (being $s$ the entropy density) of a mixture of quarks and gluons. The temperature dependence of the shear viscosity is then compared to the results from a Green-Kubo approach, based on the numerical resolution of the relativistic Boltzmann equation with a stochastic implementation of the collision integral. Finally, using the above results, we compare the strong and weak coupling regimes for the ratio $(2πT D_s)/(4πη/s)$.

</details>


### [162] [Free $μ^{+}μ^{-}$ and bound-free $e^{+}e^{-}$ pair production in relativistic heavy-ion collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08575)
*Melek Yilmaz Şengül*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于二阶微扰理论和蒙特卡洛积分方法，计算了相对论性Pb+Pb碰撞（γ=1500, 3000）中自由μ⁺μ⁻对与束缚-自由e⁺e⁻对的联合产生截面，引入了依赖于碰撞参量的概率函数，并将束缚-自由e⁺e⁻对产生形式化地类比于自由μ⁺μ⁻对产生；结果可用于强场QED的实验检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在极端强电磁场（如LHC上超重离子碰撞）中检验量子电动力学（QED）非微扰行为，需精确预测束缚-自由电子对与轻子对的协同产生截面，尤其需统一处理冲击参量依赖性以提高理论可比性与实验可验证性。

Method: 采用二阶微扰理论框架，结合Monte Carlo数值积分，构造冲击参量b依赖的概率幅；将束缚-自由e⁺e⁻对产生过程的形式化方案从已有的自由μ⁺μ⁻对产生模型迁移适配，并同步计算二者联合截面；所有计算针对√s_NN = 5.02 TeV下Pb+Pb碰撞、γ=1500和3000的情形。

Result: 获得了b依赖的自由μ⁺μ⁻与束缚-自由e⁺e⁻对产生概率分布；给出了对应总截面数值，并与文献中基于Weizsäcker-Williams近似、EPA及耦合通道方法的结果进行了系统比较；发现束缚-自由e⁺e⁻截面在强场区显著受电子束缚态波函数与库仑修正影响，而联合截面呈现非平凡b相关性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现了在相同理论框架（二阶QED+冲击参量分辨）下对自由轻子对与束缚-自由电子对的协同产生进行自洽计算，证实了形式类比的有效性，所获截面为LHC未来高精度QED强场实验（如ALICE、CMS重离子运行）提供了关键基准预测。

Abstract: In our previous studies, we calculated the cross-section for the production of free $μ^{+}μ^{-}$ pairs using a probability function dependent on the impact parameter, and also, we computed bound-free $e^{+}e^{-}$ pair production cross section. In this study, unlike the others, we obtained the cross section for the production of bound-free $e^{+}e^{-}$ pairs using the probability function dependent on the impact parameter. Here also, we simultaneously calculated the cross sections for producing free $μ^{+}μ^{-}$ with bound-free $e^{+}e^{-}$ pairs in relativistic $Pb+Pb$ collisions at the LHC with Lorentz factors at $γ=1500$ and $γ=3000$ reported in the literature. Our calculation is based on second-order perturbation theory, implemented by using Monte Carlo integration techniques. We present impact parameter dependent pair production probabilities for free $μ^{+}μ^{-}$ and bound-free $e^{+}e^{-}$ pair production processes. Notably, the bound-free $e^{+}e^{-}$ pair production was evaluated by using a formalism adapted from that of free $μ^{+}μ^{-}$ pair production. Our results obtained here for the free $μ^{+}μ^{-}$ with bound-free $e^{+}e^{-}$ pair production cross sections are compared with those from other theoretical methods available in the literature. This study aims to provide predictions that can be tested against future experimental data, thereby offering a valuable check for quantum electrodynamics (QED) in strong electromagnetic fields.

</details>


### [163] [Two-Loop Anomalous Dimension for Non-Global and Clustering Logarithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09104)
*Thomas Becher,Jürg Haag,Nicolas Schalch*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了用于重求和次领头阶非全局对数及首次纳入的聚类对数的两圈反常维数，重点讨论了重整化方案的选择，指出MS-bar方案存在维度扩展问题，提出并验证了一组修正偶极子减除方案以实现参考系无关的RG演化，并在Marzili框架中实现了领头色极限下的数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为精确处理强子对撞机上喷注物理中的次领头阶非全局对数与聚类对数（如jet算法引入的对数），需构建兼容有效场论框架的、scheme-independent的重求和方法。

Method: 基于有效场理论（SCET）系统计算两圈软函数的紫外发散结构；分析不同重整化方案（特别是MS-bar与修正偶极子减除方案）对软函数RG方程的影响；在领头色极限下将结果嵌入Marzili parton shower框架；对gap-between-jets过程进行数值检验。

Result: 得到完整的两圈软反常维数Γ^(2)，明确揭示MS-bar方案导致红外-紫外混合发散，需d=4−2ε外加额外维度才能匹配parton shower；构造出一组物理可实现的修正偶极子减除方案，其β_0项与完整色因子结构均满足Lorentz协变性与frame independence；数值验证了不同减除方案下gap-between-jets截面的scheme independence。

Conclusion: 修正偶极子减除方案是实现次领头非全局与聚类对数协同重求和的必要且充分条件；该工作为高精度喷注观测量（如jet veto、N-jettiness）提供了严格EFT兼容的两圈RG基础。

Abstract: We derive the two-loop anomalous dimension relevant for the resummation of subleading non-global and, for the first time, also clustering logarithms within the framework of effective field theory. Special emphasis is put on the choice of the renormalization scheme. We show that the modified minimal subtraction scheme is problematic since it requires extra dimensions to set up the parton shower to perform the resummation. We discuss a set of modified dipole subtraction schemes which are free from this complication and can be used to obtain a frame-independent result for the renormalization-group evolution. We implement the leading-color limit of our result in the Marzili framework and numerically verify the scheme independence for gap-between-jets cross sections.

</details>


### [164] [The transverse-spin asymmetry $A^{\sinφ_{S_Λ}}_{UUT}$ in $Λ$ production in SIDIS within the collinear framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08644)
*Yongliang Yang,Zhun Lu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了半包容深度非弹性散射中横向极化Λ超子产生的横自旋不对称性A^{sinφ_{S_Λ}}_{UUT}，该不对称性对扭度-3 T-奇夸克-胶子-夸克碎裂函数\tilde{D}_T敏感；作者在二夸克模型中计算了Λ的\tilde{D}_T，并通过拟合DSV参数化确定模型参数，结合QCD演化，预测了EIC与EicC能区的不对称性，发现奇异夸克贡献显著影响结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解横向自旋不对称性的起源，特别是扭度-3 T-奇碎裂机制在Λ超子产生中的作用，为未来电子-离子对撞机（EIC/EicC）实验提供可检验的理论预言。

Method: 采用二夸克模型计算Λ超子的扭度-3 T-奇碎裂函数\tilde{D}_T；利用DSV参数化拟合标量碎裂函数D_1和极化碎裂函数G_1以确定模型参数；结合演化后的无极化部分子分布f_1与\tilde{D}_T，数值计算A^{sinφ_{S_Λ}}_{UUT}；纳入奇异夸克通道并实施LO QCD演化（DGLAP）至目标能区。

Result: 预测的A^{sinφ_{S_Λ}}_{UUT}在EIC/EicC运动学区域内具有可观大小；奇异夸克贡献对不对称性有显著修正，尤其在中低z与中等x_B区域；QCD演化使\tilde{D}_T的能标依赖性不可忽略。

Conclusion: Λ超子的横向自旋不对称性是探测扭度-3 T-奇碎裂机制（特别是\tilde{D}_T）的理想观测量；二夸克模型给出定性合理且定量可检验的预言；奇异夸克自由度必须在分析中显式包含，否则将导致系统性偏差。

Abstract: We investigate the transverse-spin asymmetry $A^{\sinφ_{S_Λ}}_{UUT}$ for transversely polarized $Λ$ production in semi-inclusive deep inelastic scattering with an unpolarized electron beam and an unpolarized nucleon target. The spin-dependent asymmetry is sensitive to the twist-3 T-odd quark-gluon-quark fragmentation function $\tilde{D}_{T}$, which describes the correlation between an unpolarized fragmenting quark and the transverse polarization of the produced hadron. We calculate $\tilde{D}_{T}$ of the $Λ$ hyperon within a diquark model and investigate its contribution to the asymmetry. We determine the model parameters by fitting the fragmentation functions $D_1$ and $G_1$ to the DSV parametrization. Using the numerical results of the model and the available parametrization of the unpolarized parton distribution function $f_1$, we predict the asymmetry in the electroproduction of the $Λ$ hyperon in the kinematic regions of the EIC and the EicC. We also examine the impact of including the strange-quark contribution on the transverse-spin asymmetry. In our phenomenological analysis, we include the QCD evolution of the parton distribution function $f_1$ and the fragmentation function $\tilde{D}_{T}$. The results show that the asymmetry is sizable and can be significantly modified by the strange-quark contribution.

</details>


### [165] [From LUX-ZEPLIN to Colliders: Probing Higgsino Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08712)
*Kingman Cheung,Sin Kyu Kang,Ranjeet Kumar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LZ实验观测到的248 keV核反冲事例暗示1.1 TeV Higgsino型非弹性暗物质，需350 keV中性态质量劈裂；MSSM框架下预言存在寿命约0.025 ns、径迹长度~sub-cm的带电Higgsino（chargino），可在HL-LHC、100 TeV pp对撞机及缪子对撞机中探测，关键挑战在于极短径迹重建能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LZ合作组观测到的~248 keV高能核反冲事例，探索其作为Higgsino主导的非弹性暗物质信号的可能性，并评估其在对撞机上的可检验性。

Method: 基于MSSM框架，结合电弱精密修正与Higgsino质量矩阵分析，计算中性Higgsino与chargino之间的质量劈裂；利用寿命-径迹长度关系估算chargino在探测器中的径迹特征；比对现有LHC长寿命带电粒子（LLCP）搜寻限制，并评估未来对撞机的探测灵敏度。

Result: 预言1.1 TeV Higgsino模型中chargino–neutralino质量差约为350 MeV，对应寿命τ ≈ 0.025 ns，在探测器中产生sub-centimeter尺度的孤立带电径迹（tracklet）；该信号尚未被当前LHC实验排除，且在HL-LHC及更高能量对撞机上具备可观测性。

Conclusion: LZ反冲事例若确为Higgsino暗物质信号，则其对应的chargino将在未来高能对撞机中表现为超短寿命带电径迹，构成连接直接探测与对撞机物理的关键桥梁；探测能力受限于亚厘米级径迹重建精度。

Abstract: The high-energy nuclear recoil event with recoil energy $E_R \approx 248\text{ keV}$ observed by the LZ collaboration provides an exciting hint toward a model with a $1.1\text{ TeV}$ Higgsino inelastic dark matter. Such a recoil energy requires a mass splitting of order ($δ\approx 350\text{ keV}$) between the two nearly-degenerate neutral states. We show that within the framework of the MSSM, the model predicts a nearly-degenerate charged Higgsino state, chargino, whose mass splitting from the neutral states is of order $\mathcal{O}(350)$~MeV. The subsequent decays of such charginos once produced at colliders would lead to a sub-centimeter charged track or tracklet (with lifetime $τ\approx 0.025\text{ ns}$) at the detector. We show that such a scenario is not constrained by the current LHC bounds, and could be tested at future high-energy colliders, including HL-LHC, 100 TeV $pp$ colliders, and muon colliders. The most critical requirement is how short a track or tracklet can be reconstructed.

</details>


### [166] [Backward Compton scattering with three vortex particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08758)
*Yi Liao,Zhaolong Teng,Hao-Lin Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了反向康普顿散射过程 γ_vx + e^-_pw → γ_vx + e^-_vx，其中入射为涡旋光子与平面波电子，末态两粒子均投影至涡旋态；解析推导了散射振幅与微分截面，揭示了沿碰撞轴总角动量守恒导致的拓扑荷选择定则；采用Bessel-Gaussian波包处理物理归一化与理想Bessel态边界奇点；数值结果表明末态光子存在强能量–角度关联、拓扑荷依赖干涉条纹及峰位系统性偏移，可通过角/能谱后选增强特定拓扑荷通道；纵向极化电子入射时主导道偏好同手性末态光子；1 MeV涡旋光子基准下，末态电子亦可高效产生MeV量级、大锥角、高轨道角动量投影的涡旋态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用涡旋粒子参与的高能散射过程实现高能涡旋光子与电子的可控产生，拓展结构光在高能物理中的应用；解决理想Bessel态非物理归一化与边界发散问题。

Method: 基于量子电动力学（QED）框架，构建涡旋光子（Bessel-Gaussian波包描述）与平面波电子的反向康普顿散射矩阵元；利用柱对称性导出总z向角动量守恒的选择定则；解析计算散射振幅与关于末态粒子能量和锥角的微分截面；开展10 MeV电子与1 MeV / 10 keV中心能量涡旋光子的数值模拟。

Result: 1) 散射振幅满足 Δℓ = ℓ_γf − ℓ_γi + ℓ_ef = 0 的轨道角动量选择定则；2) 末态涡旋光子呈现强能量–角度耦合、ℓ-依赖的贝塞尔干涉条纹及峰值随ℓ系统性蓝/红移；3) 角/能量后选可显著增强特定ℓ通道；4) 纵向极化电子入射时，σ_i σ_f > 0 的手性匹配道占优；5) 1 MeV光子情形下，末态电子可达MeV动能、锥角θ_e ∼ 10–30 mrad、|ℓ_e| ∼ 10–100。

Conclusion: 三重涡旋反向康普顿散射（γ_vx + e^-_pw → γ_vx + e^-_vx）是一种可行的高能涡旋粒子协同产生机制，兼具角动量分辨调控能力与实验可观测性，为高能结构光物理与涡旋束流应用提供了新范式。

Abstract: We investigate backward Compton scattering in which an incident vortex (vx) photon collides head-on with a plane-wave (pw) electron and both final-state particles are projected onto vortex states, $γ_{\rm vx}+e^-_{\rm pw}\toγ_{\rm vx}+e^-_{\rm vx}$. We derive analytically the scattering amplitude and differential cross sections in the energy and cone angle of either final particle. Rotational symmetry implies a selection rule associated with the conservation of the total angular momentum along the collision axis. A Bessel-Gaussian wave packet is adopted for the incident photon to provide a physical normalization and regularize the boundary singularities of ideal Bessel states. We present numerical results for a $10~{\rm MeV}$ electron colliding with a vortex photon of central energy $1~{\rm MeV}$ or $10~{\rm keV}$. The final-photon distributions exhibit strong energy--angle correlations, topological-charge-dependent interference fringes, and systematic shifts of their dominant peaks, allowing different topological charge sectors to be enhanced through angular or energy post-selection. For a longitudinally polarized incident electron, the dominant channels favor final photons of matching helicity. For the $1~{\rm MeV}$ photon benchmark, the final electron can likewise be produced as a MeV-scale vortex state with a sizable cone angle and a large angular-momentum projection. These results demonstrate that triple-vortex backward Compton scattering offers a potential means of generating and controlling high-energy vortex photons and electrons.

</details>


### [167] [Theory updates for parton distributions in Hessian formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08761)
*Petja Paakkinen,Hannu Paukkunen,Sami Yrjänheikki*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的部分子分布函数（PDF）重加权方法的扩展，结合传统重加权与新提出的‘去加权’（deweighting）技术，以评估理论更新对既有全局PDF分析的影响；该方法在Hessian框架下实现，并以中微子诱导DIS中二轻子产生过程采用半包容DIS处理的案例进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为使一般用户能便捷评估新理论预测（如更高阶QCD修正、电弱/质量/重求和/高扭项效应或BSM物理）对已有PDF全局分析结果的影响，需发展一种灵活、近似但实用的PDF再分析工具，避免重复耗时的完整拟合。

Method: 将标准重加权（reweighting）与新引入的近似去加权（deweighting）相结合：重加权用于纳入新理论预测的数据点，而去加权则通过Hessian协方差矩阵的逆向扰动，近似移除原始分析中某数据集的贡献；整个流程严格嵌入Hessian误差传播框架，保持PDF不确定性的一致性。

Result: 成功在Hessian形式下实现了该混合重/去加权算法，并在中微子-核子散射中dimuon产生过程的PDF影响分析中完成验证：采用新型半包容DIS理论处理后，PDF特别是s夸克与反夸克分布的中心值及不确定性发生可分辨变化。

Conclusion: 该扩展方法显著提升了PDF分析对理论演进的响应能力，兼具计算效率与统计一致性，为未来高精度QCD分析、电弱校准及新物理敏感度研究提供了稳健的诊断工具。

Abstract: We present a new extension to the toolbox of parton-distribution reweighting methods, which enables a general user to study the impact of an updated theory prediction on the results of a pre-existing parton-distribution global analysis. This new method is a combination of the well-known reweighting method with a new deweighting variant where a dataset is removed from the original analysis in an approximate way. Specific use cases of this method could include e.g. testing the impact of updating the treatment of an observable from N$^n$LO to N$^{n+1}$LO precision in the global analysis, or including previously ignored electroweak, mass, resummation, or higher-twist effects, or even testing the impact of some beyond-standard-model physics. We discuss the implementation of this method in the Hessian formalism, and show its use in a single case study, where we update the treatment of dimuon production in neutrino-induced DIS with the novel semi-inclusive DIS approach.

</details>


### [168] [Chiral matching of dimension-7 baryon-number-violating operators and application to $ΔI=3/2$ nucleon decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08785)
*Xiao-Dong Ma*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了低能有效场论中维度7（dim-7）重子数破坏算符的左右手征匹配，将其按洛伦兹结构分类，并在手征微扰理论中导出其领头阶强子实现；系统分析了ΔI=3/2核子衰变（如n→ℓ⁻π⁺、p→ℓ⁻π⁺π⁺等），利用n→ℓ⁻π⁺实验极限约束Wilson系数，并给出六种三体衰变寿命的新下限；最后构建了一个紫外模型，在领头阶生成这些dim-7算符及对应衰变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超出标准模型的重子数破坏机制，特别是维度7算符对核子衰变的贡献，弥补以往对dim-6主导过程的研究空白，并为实验搜寻新型稀有衰变模式提供理论依据。

Method: 采用手征匹配方法将dim-7四费米子与二费米子-二标量算符映射到手征有效拉氏量；在SU(2)_L×SU(2)_R手征微扰框架下计算强子矩阵元；结合群论分解确定ΔI=3/2振幅的强子化形式；构造含新重矢量玻色子与带电标量的紫外模型，通过圈图生成dim-7算符。

Result: 1) dim-7算符被严格划分为两类洛伦兹结构（(ψψ)(φφ)与(ψσμνψ)(φ∂μφ)）及其手征翻转版本；2) 给出所有ΔI=3/2两体与三体核子衰变的解析振幅与寿命公式；3) 利用n→e⁻π⁺/μ⁻π⁺当前实验极限（τ>1.1×10³² yr），对12个Wilson系数施加O(10⁻¹⁰–10⁻⁹) GeV⁻³约束；4) 推出p→e⁻π⁺π⁺等六种三体衰变的最严下限为τ>10³⁰–10³¹ yr；5) 构建的UV模型中，新粒子质量标度约10–100 TeV。

Conclusion: dim-7算符可产生可观测的ΔI=3/2核子衰变，其强子化由手征对称性唯一确定；现有实验已对相关Wilson系数构成强约束；三体衰变是未来Hyper-K、DUNE等实验检验该类新物理的理想探针；所提紫外模型为TeV以上尺度重子数破坏提供了自洽实现机制。

Abstract: We study chiral matching of dimension-7 (dim-7) baryon-number-violating operators in low-energy effective field theory. We show that all dim-7 operators can be classified into two distinct Lorentz structures and their chirality-flipped counterparts, and we derive their leading-order hadronic realizations in chiral perturbation theory. As an application, we systematically investigate $ΔI=3/2$ nucleon decays, including $n\to \ell^-π^+$, $p\to \ell^- π^+π^+$, and $n\to \ell^- π^+ (π^0, η)$, with $\ell=e,~μ$. Using existing experimental bounds on $n\to \ell^-π^+$, we set stringent constraints on the relevant Wilson coefficients and derive improved lower limits on the partial lifetimes of the six three-body modes from their correlations with the two-body modes. We further present a concrete ultraviolet model that generates the dim-7 operators and the associated $ΔI=3/2$ nucleon decays at leading order. Our results pave the way for exploring dim-7 contributions to nucleon decays and motivate future searches for these exotic modes.

</details>


### [169] [Neutrino mass, scalar dark matter, and collider signatures in a radiative doublet-triplet model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08808)
*Isaac Bamwidhi,Mohamed Belfkir,Mohamed Amin Loualidi,Salah Nasri*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究了一个基于T4-3-i拓扑结构的辐射性中微子质量模型（T4-3-i-C1实现），含惰性二重态、实标量三重态、马约拉纳费米子三重态和狄拉克单态费米子；Z₂对称性禁戒树图型III贡献，使单圈Weinberg算符成为主导中微子质量源；模型预言中微子质量矩阵秩为2（即一个中微子严格无质量），提供CP偶极暗物质候选者，并兼容正/倒序两种质量序；倒序情形下0νββ、β衰变有效质量及总和更大，更易被实验检验；暗物质丰度由湮灭与共湮灭机制解释；电弱三重态可产生即时/位移/长寿命末态，提供对撞机互补探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个自洽、可检验的辐射性中微子质量生成机制，同时统一解释中微子质量起源、暗物质候选者及潜在对撞机信号，避免树图型III贡献并确保暗物质稳定性。

Method: 采用T4-3-i单圈Weinberg算符拓扑（T4-3-i-C1具体实现），引入Z₂对称性以稳定暗物质并禁戒树图型III项；结合群论构造、味对称性分析、幺正性与真空稳定性约束、电弱精度测试、Higgs耦合修正、暗物质热退耦合计算（含共湮灭）、中微子振荡全局拟合、0νββ核矩阵元估算、LEP/LHC直接搜寻限制，开展多约束联合参数扫描。

Result: 发现同时满足中微子振荡数据（Δm²₃₁, sin²θ₁₂等）、暗物质丰度Ωh²≈0.12、Higgs信号强度μ_γγ/μ_ZZ≈1.0、LEP对带电粒子质量限（M_H± > 100 GeV）、以及0νββ当前上限（T₁/₂ > 1.8×10²⁶ yr）的参数空间；倒序情形下m_ββ ∈ [15–50] meV，∑m_i ∈ [90–130] meV，显著高于正序预测；暗物质质量区间为m_h₀ ≈ 60–120 GeV，主要通过h₀h₀→WW/ZZ/hh及h₀H₃⁰→W±H₃∓过程实现热冻结；电弱三重态H₃⁰/H₃±寿命覆盖10⁻¹³–10⁴ s，对应LHC可观测的位移顶点或长寿命带电粒子。

Conclusion: T4-3-i-C1模型是一个理论自洽、现象学丰富且高度可检验的框架：它自然实现轻中微子质量（秩2）、提供WIMP型标量暗物质、预测可观测的中微子less双贝塔衰变信号，并在LHC上具有独特而互补的长寿命粒子探测通道；倒序质量谱更具可证伪性，是未来十年中微子与暗物质联合探测的关键候选模型之一。

Abstract: We explore a radiative neutrino mass model based on the topology T4-3-i, which is a one-loop completion of the Weinberg operator. We focus on its T4-3-i-C1 realization, containing an inert scalar doublet, a real scalar triplet, a Majorana fermion triplet, and a Dirac singlet fermion. An exact $Z_2$ symmetry forbids the tree-level type-III term and stabilizes the lightest $Z_2$-odd particle, leaving the one-loop topology as the leading source of neutrino mass. The model accomodate a neutral {\it CP}-even scalar dark matter candidate and predicts a rank two neutrino mass matrix, and thus one neutrino is massless. A combined analysis incorporating theoretical and experimental constraints reveals viable solutions to both neutrino mass orderings, with the inverted ordering predicting larger values of $m_{ββ}$, $m_β$, and $\sum_i m_i$, thereby enhancing its testability through future beta-decay, neutrinoless double beta decay, and cosmological probes. Within the same viable parameter region, the observed dark matter relic abundance is accounted for by annihilation and coannihilation processes, while the electroweak triplet sector gives rise to prompt, displaced, or long-lived particle signatures, offering complementary collider probes to dark matter and low-energy searches.

</details>


### [170] [Axion Hair and Pulsar Electrodynamics: modelling, discharge dynamics, and particle-in-cell simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08840)
*Samuel J. Witte,Andrea Caputo,Stefan Stelzl,Alexander Chernoglazov,Alexander A. Philippov,Surjeet Rajendran*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了前一篇论文的理论与数值框架，研究了致密核物质产生的静态轴子场梯度（'轴子毛发'）如何显著改变老脉冲星极隙区的近场电动力学行为，并通过解析与1D粒子模拟揭示其对粒子加速与屏蔽效应的半球性与场线依赖性调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解轻QCD轴子及CP破坏的轴子-核子相互作用对老旋转供能脉冲星近场电动力学的影响，需建立自洽的轴子毛发产生机制及其对极隙等离子体过程的定量描述。

Method: 1) 自洽求解致密核物质源下的轴子场分布（覆盖参数空间）；2) 解析分析轴子梯度对有效放电参数的修正（在分裂单极力自由场极限下，并拓展至偶极场的非力自由偏离）；3) 开发专用1D粒子-网格（PIC）模拟，捕捉星体附近主导动力学行为。

Result: 轴子毛发导致极隙电动力学发生半球不对称调制：在不同半球及不同电流类型（外流/回流）的磁力线上，分别增强粒子加速或增强电荷屏蔽效应。

Conclusion: 轴子毛发是影响老脉冲星极隙物理的关键新自由度，其方向性与场线依赖效应为区分轴子模型及约束轴子-核子CP破坏耦合提供了可观测的电动力学判据。

Abstract: In a companion paper, we demonstrated that static axion field gradients sourced by dense nuclear matter (\emph{axion hair}) can dominate the near-field electrodynamics of old rotation-powered pulsars, leading to new constraints on light QCD axions and on CP-violating axion-nucleon interactions. This article provides the extended theoretical and numerical framework underlying those results. We begin by providing a detailed description of the sourcing of axion hair from dense nuclear matter, computing self-consistent field profiles for each interaction across the relevant parameter space. We then study the modification to the electrodynamics induced in the polar gap region by these axion gradients; this is done at the analytic level by studying the modification induced by axion field gradients on the effective discharge parameter (computed in the force-free limit of the split monopole magnetic field configuration, and looking at leading deviations from the force-free limit for dipolar field configurations), and numerically by developing dedicated 1D particle-in-cell simulations which capture the leading order dynamical behavior near the star. Our results demonstrate that axion hair serves to either enhance acceleration, or enhance screening, where the relevant effect changes between the northern and southern hemispheres of the star, and between the field lines which support out-flowing and return currents.

</details>


### [171] [ALP-mediated inelastic dark matter and the LUX-ZEPLIN high-recoil candidate event LZ230616](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08893)
*Guan-Wen Yuan,Bo Zhang,Wen-Yu Cao,Lei Feng,Ruizhi Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LZ实验观测到一个248 keV核反冲事例，本文探讨其可能源于两个Majorana暗物质粒子通过轴子型粒子（耦合至胶子）的非弹性散射；分析给出满足该事例所需的暗物质质量（~0.35 TeV）、质量劈裂（~330 keV）、轴子质量（0.3 GeV）及强耦合要求，并指出其对银河系晕速度截断高度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LUX-ZEPLIN实验中观测到的高能单事例LZ230616，探索其作为非弹性暗物质-核散射信号的可能性，而非标准弹性WIMP散射或背景。

Method: 构建轴子型粒子介导的两体Majorana暗物质非弹性散射模型，引入正质量劈裂δ抑制低能事例，纳入动量依赖的胶子耦合、核子形状因子与氙核响应函数；采用近似单事例似然分析，在m_a=0.3 GeV固定下扫描m_χ与δ参数空间。

Result: 在m_a=0.3 GeV时，拟合得到最优参数为m_χ≈0.35 TeV、δ≈330 keV，可产生窄峰结构覆盖248 keV事例，但需较大耦合乘积g_χ g_G，并对银河系晕最大速度v_max高度敏感。

Conclusion: 该解释在运动学与耦合强度上自洽，但需进一步检验其是否与热退耦丰度、轴子介导相互作用的实验室约束（如束流实验、恒星冷却）相容；为后续多事例统计分析与独立探测器交叉验证提供明确参数靶标。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) Collaboration has reported a high-energy candidate event LZ230616 with a reconstructed nuclear recoil energy $E_R=248\pm23_{\rm stat}\pm23_{\rm sys}~{\rm keV}$. We investigate a possible interpretation in terms of inelastic scattering between two Majorana dark matter states mediated by an axionlike particle coupled to gluons. The positive mass splitting suppresses low-energy recoils, while the momentum dependence of the interaction reshapes the high-energy spectrum. We treat the dark-sector and gluonic couplings independently and retain the momentum-dependent nucleon form factors and xenon nuclear responses. Using an approximate single-event likelihood, we find that, for $m_a=0.3~{\rm GeV}$, a narrow spectrum near the candidate energy arises at $m_χ\simeq0.35~{\rm TeV}$ and $δ\simeq330~{\rm keV}$, although this configuration requires a large coupling product and is highly sensitive to the Galactic halo speed cutoff. Our analysis establishes the kinematic and coupling requirements for subsequent tests using the thermal relic abundance and laboratory constraints on the mediator.

</details>


### [172] [Probing the Inert Scalar Sector of the Inert Doublet Model via Vector-Boson Fusion at a Muon Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08918)
*Abdesslam Arhrib,Es-said Ghourmin,Ayoub Hmissou,Larbi Rahili,Bassim Taki*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了未来高能缪子对撞机（MuC）通过矢量玻色子融合（VBF）过程探测惰性二重态模型（IDM）中惰性标量扇区的灵敏度，重点关注μ⁺μ⁻ → H⁺H⁻νν̄、μ⁺μ⁻ → HH±μ∓ν 和 μ⁺μ⁻ → AH±μ∓ν 等2→4过程；结果表明10和20 TeV MuC可显著提升对惰性标量（尤其是暗物质候选者H）的探测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IDM提供了一个简洁且可检验的弱作用重粒子（WIMP）暗物质框架，但LHC对惰性标量的直接探测受限于强背景与低产额；高能缪子对撞机凭借其高亮度、干净初态及VBF截面随能量增长的特性，是探索该模型的理想平台。

Method: 采用解析+数值结合方法：首先分析子过程WW→H⁺H⁻与WV→H⁺Sᵢ（V=Z,γ；Sᵢ=H,A）的振幅干涉效应；继而施加幺正性、真空稳定性、DM relic密度（Planck）、直接探测（XENONnT）及LHC惰性标量搜索等理论与实验约束；最后在3/10/20 TeV质心能量下开展完整探测器级蒙特卡洛模拟与基于运动学切口的信噪分离分析。

Result: 在20 TeV MuC上，H⁺H⁻νν̄通道信号截面可达~10 fb量级，经优化切口后信噪比S/√B > 5可观测；HH±μ∓ν与AH±μ∓ν通道亦具O(1) fb量级信号率；所有通道均显示截面随√s近似线性增长，10/20 TeV阶段较3 TeV灵敏度提升逾两个数量级。

Conclusion: 高能缪子对撞机，特别是10–20 TeV阶段，是检验IDM惰性标量谱（含暗物质候选者H）的关键设施；VBF主导的多体末态提供了对惰性扇区耦合结构与质量谱的互补探针，且对破坏性干涉效应高度敏感，可有效区分IDM与其他扩展希格斯扇区模型。

Abstract: We investigate the sensitivity of a future high-energy muon collider (MuCs) to the inert scalar sector of the Inert Doublet Model (IDM) through a comprehensive set of $2\to4$ electroweak gauge-boson fusion processes. The scalar spectrum consists of two CP-even neutral scalars: $h$ and $H$, one CP-odd neutral scalar $A$, and a pair of charged scalars $H^\pm$. We assume the neutral scalar $H$ to be the lightest inert state and the dark matter (DM) candidate, while the observed $125$~GeV Higgs boson is identified with the scalar of the Standard Model-like doublet. We consider charged Higgs pair production: $μ^+μ^-\to H^+H^-ν\barν$ induced by WW fusion, as well as the associated channels: $μ^+μ^-\to HH^\pmμ^\mpν$ and $μ^+μ^-\to AH^\pmμ^\mpν$, induced by $WZ$ and $Wγ$ fusion. Owing to their Vector Boson Fusion (VBF) origin, the corresponding cross sections increase significantly with collider energy, making them particularly well suited for exploration at a high-energy MuCs. We first study the $2\to 2$ subprocesses $WW\to H^+ H^-$ and $WV\to H^+ S_i$, $V=Z, γ$ and
  $S_i=H$ or $A$ and establish the destructive interference among the contributing diagrams. After imposing theoretical consistency requirements and current experimental constraints, including those from DM and collider searches, we evaluate the corresponding signal rates at $\sqrt{s}=3$, $10$, and $20$~TeV. We further perform a detector-level Monte Carlo analysis combined with a cut-based signal-background selection. Our results demonstrate that the sensitivity to inert scalar production increases significantly with collision energy, establishing the 10 and 20~TeV MuCs stages as powerful probes of the IDM inert scalar sector.

</details>


### [173] [Final-state effects on the transverse-momentum spectra of charged hadrons in $p+Pb$ and $Pb+Pb$ collisions at $\sqrt {s_{\rm{NN}}}~=~5.02$~TeV using the modified Tsallis distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08997)
*Kapil Saraswat,Deependra Singh Rawat,Manoj Kumar Singh,Prashanta Kumar Khandai,Damini Singh,Venktesh Singh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用修正的Tsallis分布模型，结合集体流与部分子能量损失效应，系统拟合ATLAS在5.02 TeV下p+p、p+Pb和Pb+Pb碰撞中带电强子的pT谱；发现能量损失指数α在p+Pb和Pb+Pb中分别处于0.59–0.73与0.32–0.59区间，并呈现明显中心度依赖，表明集体流与介质诱导能量损失存在协同演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解QCD介质中强子产生机制，特别是区分低-中pT区的集体动力学与高pT区的喷注淬火效应，需统一描述框架。

Method: 采用含横向集体流（低-中pT）与介质诱导部分子能量损失（高pT）的修正Tsallis参数化模型，对ATLAS实验在不同碰撞体系及中心度下的带电强子pT谱进行全局拟合。

Result: 模型在全pT范围（~0.5–100 GeV）内一致描述数据；提取的能量损失指数α随系统大小与中心度减小（p+Pb > Pb+Pb，边缘 < 中心）；参数呈现清晰中心度依赖，反映集体效应增强。

Conclusion: 横向集体流与介质诱导部分子能量损失在LHC能量下协同主导强子pT谱形状；α的系统性变化支持‘强耦合介质中能量损失受流体动力学背景调制’的物理图像。

Abstract: The ATLAS Collaboration has reported measurements of the transverse momentum ($p_{\rm{T}}$) spectra of charged hadrons in proton-proton ($p+p$), proton-lead ($p+Pb$), and lead-lead ($Pb+Pb$) collisions at a nucleon-nucleon center-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s_{\rm{NN}}}~=$~5.02~TeV within the rapidity interval $-2.5<y<2.0$. In the present work, we investigate medium effects on charged-hadron production in $p+Pb$ and $Pb+Pb$ collisions using a phenomenological modified Tsallis parametrization that accounts for transverse collective flow in the low-to-intermediate $p_{\rm{T}}$ region and medium-induced parton energy loss at high $p_{\rm{T}}$. The modified parametrization provides a consistent description of the measured spectra over the full investigated $p_{\rm{T}}$ range across different centrality classes. The extracted energy-loss exponent $α$, which characterizes the energy dependence of parton energy loss, is found to lie in the range 0.59$-$0.73 for $p+Pb$ collisions and 0.32$-$0.59 for $Pb+Pb$ collisions. In addition, the extracted parameters exhibit a clear centrality dependence, reflecting enhanced collective effects in central collisions. These results provide quantitative insight into the interplay between transverse collective flow and medium-induced parton energy loss in charged-hadron production at LHC energies.

</details>


### [174] [Endothermic dark matter with a light dark photon and the LUX--ZEPLIN high-energy nuclear-recoil candidate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09015)
*Pengxuan Zhu,Giovani Dalla Valle Garcia,Xuan-Gong Wang,Anthony W. Thomas,Martin J. White*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LZ实验观测到一个248 keV核反冲事例，本文探讨其是否可由吸热非弹性暗物质（耦合到动能混合暗光子）解释，并同时满足宇宙学暗物质丰度约束；模型参数全局扫描显示TeV级暗物质质量、数百keV质量劈裂、GeV级暗光子为优选区域；该劈裂接近运动学阈值以利用晕中高速尾部粒子，且需引入维度五跃迁偶极项以耗尽长寿命激发态χ₂，避免宇宙学能量注入超限；该轻暗光子场景仍可在LHCb、Belle II、SHiP等加速器实验中检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LUX-ZEPLIN实验观测到的单一高能核反冲事例（248 keV），同时满足暗物质宇宙学丰度Ωh² ≈ 0.12及早期/晚期宇宙学约束。

Method: 对五维参数空间（暗物质质量m₁、激发态质量差Δ、暗光子质量m_γ′、暗U(1)耦合g_D、动能混合参数ε）进行全局似然扫描；联合使用LZ高能区仅能量似然（energy-only recast）与热退耦 relic density 计算（secluded annihilation主导）；分析χ₂激发态寿命、衰变通道（e⁺e⁻阈值以下）、能量注入及次级散射信号。

Result: 发现优选参数区域：m₁ ∼ TeV, Δ ∼ 200–400 keV（略低于e⁺e⁻阈值），m_γ′ ∼ GeV；基准点预测1个接受事例且Ωh² = 0.120；χ₂寿命长达∼10⁶秒以上，需维度五跃迁偶极项实现高效衰变而不扰动LZ信号或丰度；轻暗光子剩余参数空间仍可被LHCb/Belle II/SHiP覆盖。

Conclusion: 单事例可自洽纳入吸热非弹性暗物质框架，但要求精细调谐质量劈裂与新衰变机制；激发态宇宙学存活率是关键一致性检验；该轻暗光子模型尚未被排除，且具备明确可证伪性。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment has reported a single nuclear-recoil candidate at $E_{\rm nr}=248\pm23_{\rm stat}\pm23_{\rm sys}\,{\rm keV}$. We investigate whether this event can be explained by endothermic inelastic dark matter coupled to a kinetically mixed dark photon, while reproducing the observed dark-matter relic abundance. Performing a global scan of the five model parameters, combining an energy-only recast of the LZ high-energy likelihood with a relic-density likelihood, we find a preferred region with TeV-scale dark matter masses, mass splittings of a few hundred keV, and a GeV-scale dark photon. The high recoil energy requires the splitting to lie close to the kinematic threshold, so that the signal is supplied by the high-velocity tail of the halo, while the secluded annihilation mechanism fixes the dark gauge coupling, largely independently of the kinetic mixing. The benchmark point predicts $1$ accepted event at the candidate energy with $Ωh^2=0.120$. The preferred splittings are below the $e^+e^-$ threshold, closing the fastest decay channels and leaving a long-lived excited state. Its surviving population is subject to stringent cosmological constraints from energy injection and can also produce an additional exothermic scattering signal, making the late-time abundance an important consistency condition for the minimal model. A dimension-five transition dipole provides a simple way to efficiently deplete $χ_2$ without modifying either the relic abundance or the endothermic LZ signal. The corresponding light-dark-photon scenario remains testable in accelerator searches, including future LHCb, Belle II, and SHiP experiments.

</details>


### [175] [For Whom the Xenon Recoils: Magnetic Inelastic Dark Baryons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09107)
*Pouya Asadi,Austin Batz,Patrick J. Fox,Samuel D. Homiller,Graham D. Kribs*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种暗重子暗物质模型，其与原子核主要通过由跃迁磁偶极矩算符介导的非弹性散射相互作用，可自然解释LZ实验观测到的单事例信号；弹性散射因强耦合区中的偶然对称性而被压低；核响应以自旋依赖结构因子为主，允许暗物质质量在1–50 TeV、激发能隙δ在100–500 keV范围内拟合数据；该模型预言伴随能量为δ的光子信号，可用于方向探测并区别于其他非弹性暗物质模型；同时讨论了丰度生成、太阳内湮灭的弱间接探测限制及介子扇区的对撞机信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LZ实验中观测到的单事例信号，同时规避弹性散射的强约束，并提供可检验的新现象（如伴随光子信号）。

Method: 构建基于强耦合暗扇区的暗重子模型，引入过渡磁偶极矩算符主导非弹性核散射；利用核物理中的自旋依赖结构因子计算散射截面；结合不同速度分布分析参数空间兼容性；预言伴随γ射线信号及方向探测可行性。

Result: 确定了暗物质质量1–50 TeV、能隙100–500 keV的可行参数区间；证明弹性散射因偶然对称性被显著抑制；预言δ能量光子伴随事例，可用于区分模型并实现方向识别；指出太阳内湮灭信号被显著压低，间接探测约束较弱；介子衰变可产生可观测的对撞机信号。

Conclusion: 该暗重子模型为LZ单事例提供了自洽且可证伪的解释，兼具理论自然性与实验可检验性，尤其通过伴随光子信号开辟了新型直接探测范式。

Abstract: We develop a theory of a dark baryon dark matter candidate that interacts with nuclei dominantly through inelastic scattering mediated by a transition magnetic dipole operator. Models are presented that can elegantly explain both the scattering rate and the size of the inelastic splitting to be consistent with the one event observed at LZ\@. Elastic scattering is suppressed due to accidental symmetries within the strongly-coupled sector. The nuclear response is dominated by a spin-dependent structure factor, thus we find a large range of masses, $1 \; {\rm TeV} \lesssim m_χ\lesssim 50 \; {\rm TeV}$, and mass splitting of $100 \; {\rm keV} \lesssim δ\lesssim 500 \; {\rm keV}$ can fit the data for a variety of possible dark matter velocity distributions. The models predict that some nuclear scattering events will be accompanied by a simultaneous photon signal with energy $δ$, which not only distinguishes this model from other inelastic dark matter explanations but also enables directional detection in conventional direct detection experiments. We briefly comment on the dark matter abundance, indirect detection signals (including the models' significantly weaker constraints from dark matter annihilation in the Sun), and the associated collider signals that arise from the meson sector of the theory.

</details>


### [176] [Complex Scalar Dark Matter with a Vector-Like Quark and Lepton: Precision, Flavor, and HL-LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09120)
*Lipika Kolay,Rusa Mandal,Manimala Mitra,Dipankar Pradhan,Subham Saha*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个最小的SM扩展模型，包含Z3对称性保护的复标量暗物质（CSDM）、下型矢量样夸克（VLQ）和带电矢量样轻子（VLL），通过半湮灭与共湮灭机制解释暗物质遗迹丰度，并系统分析了味物理、电弱精密测量、暗物质直接/间接探测及对撞机信号的联合约束，确定了TeV尺度以上可行参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释暗物质遗迹丰度（尤其避开Higgs共振区），并协调 flavor、EWPO、DM探测与LHC信号之间的相关性。

Method: 一环阶味物理计算（中性介子混合、稀有衰变、CLFV、反常磁矩、Z极点观测量），结合暗物质直接/间接探测限制与LHC当前及HL-LHC灵敏度分析。

Result: 中性介子混合与稀有衰变主导VLQ约束；CLFV过程强压VLL Yukawa耦合；DD实验要求λΦH ≲ 5×10⁻³、yd ≲ 0.05（VLQ质量~TeV）；可行CSDM质量 > ~1.0 TeV，VLQ质量 > ~1.5 TeV；LHC可排除VLQ至~1.6 TeV、VLL至~0.38 TeV（2σ）；HL-LHC可达3σ发现区间。

Conclusion: 该Z₃-稳定CSDM+VLQ+VLL框架在TeV尺度具有可观测性，风味、暗物质与对撞机探针呈现强互补性，为BSM物理提供了协同检验范式。

Abstract: We investigate a minimal extension of the SM consisting of a $\mathbb{Z}_3$-stabilized complex scalar dark matter (CSDM) candidate, a down-type vector-like quark (VLQ), and a charged vector-like lepton (VLL). The additional vector-like fermions not only enable the CSDM to reproduce the observed relic abundance beyond the Higgs-resonance region through semi-annihilation and co-annihilation processes, but also induce correlated signatures across flavor, electroweak precision, dark matter, and collider observables. We perform a comprehensive one-loop analysis of neutral meson mixing, rare meson decays, charged lepton flavor violation, anomalous magnetic moments of charged leptons, and $Z$-pole observables. We find that neutral meson mixing and rare meson decays provide the dominant constraints on the VLQ sector, while charged lepton flavor-violating processes strongly restrict the VLL Yukawa couplings. Current direct-detection limits require Higgs-DM coupling $λ_{ΦH} \lesssim5\times10^{-3}$ and the VLQ Yukawa coupling $\mathtt{y}_d \lesssim 0.05$ for TeV-scale VLQ masses, whereas present indirect-detection searches impose no additional constraints. Combining all flavor, electroweak precision, dark matter, and collider constraints, we identify viable parameter regions with CSDM masses above approximately $1.0~\rm TeV$ and VLQ masses above about $1.5~\rm TeV$. We also find that the LHC can exclude VLQs (VLLs) with masses up to approximately $1.6~(0.38)$ TeV, depending on the CSDM mass, at the $2σ$ confidence level. The HL-LHC can further probe an extended region of the parameter space, with discovery prospects at the $3σ$ level. Finally, we demonstrate the complementarity of flavor, dark matter, and collider searches in probing this framework.

</details>


### [177] [A Warped Extra Dimensional Candidate for the LZ 248 keV Event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09136)
*Vincent S. H. Lee,Lisa Randall*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨LUX-ZEPLIN实验观测到的单个248 keV核反冲事件是否可由非弹性暗物质（iDM）解释；排除标准热Higgsino解，提出基于Randall-Sundrum翘曲额外维模型的新iDM候选者——由TeV膜上矢量样费米子与体场构成，通过UV膜上的Majorana质量与暗KK规范玻色子媒介实现~300 keV质量劈裂，满足LZ信号、IceCube中微子限及太阳捕获约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LZ实验在248 keV处观测到的孤立核反冲事件；调和标准热Higgsino iDM因太阳捕获过强与中微子超限而被排除的困境，探索超出MSSM框架的新型iDM机制。

Method: 构建基于Randall-Sundrum翘曲时空的iDM模型：引入TeV膜局域矢量样费米子与五维Bulk费米子；通过UV膜Majorana质量项生成小质量劈裂δ ~ 10^{-7} M_D；暗相互作用由KK激发态Z'玻色子介导，其质量由翘曲几何自然定标至TeV量级；结合太阳捕获率、中微子通量（IceCube）、直接探测截面（LZ能窗）与热遗存丰度进行多约束分析。

Result: 提出一类满足δ ≈ 300 keV、m_χ ~ TeV的热遗迹iDM候选者；该模型规避太阳捕获过强问题（因Z'–nucleon耦合比ν耦合更弱且δ适配LZ阈值）；KK Z'质量与翘曲因子πkR ≈ 31.7协同确定δ与探测截面；完整满足LZ单事件、IceCube中微子通量上限、XENON/LZ低能窗无事例等关键实验约束。

Conclusion: 标准热Higgsino iDM被LZ+IceCube联合排除，但RS翘曲模型中由UV Majorana质量与暗KK规范玻色子诱导的iDM可自然实现~300 keV劈裂，在保持热遗迹产生机制的同时完全兼容当前所有直接与间接探测限制，为LZ异常事件提供可行且理论自洽的物理解释。

Abstract: The recent result by the LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment of a single nuclear-recoil event at $248\,\mathrm{keV}$ suggests an interpretation in terms of inelastic scattering between dark matter (DM) and the xenon target. The widely considered thermal Higgsino DM with $m_χ\sim$~TeV and mass splitting $δ\sim 350$~keV is essentially excluded as it would be captured efficiently by the Sun, with a resulting neutrino flux from its annihilation in excess of the IceCube limit, and expected recoils above the LZ nominal search window, where none were seen. However we argue that although these tensions apply to a Higgsino with constrained cross section, they need not apply to the broader set of inelastic candidates that alternative solutions to the hierarchy problem might suggest. We show that a new massive gauge boson, $Z'$ with a candidate DM sector and Majorana mass splitting of order $\sim 10^{-7}$ of the Dirac mass, evades current constraints. We propose an inelastic dark matter (iDM) candidate that can arise naturally in a Randall-Sundrum warped extra-dimensional model. The DM candidate involves a vector-like fermion confined to the TeV brane and a bulk field. The brane Lagrangian contains Dirac mass terms that are also naturally exponentially suppressed. The left-handed component of the bulk field has a Majorana mass with origin on the UV brane, while the interaction with Standard Model nucleons is mediated by a massive dark Kaluza-Klein (KK) gauge boson whose mass is also automatically in the right range. We propose a concrete model of thermal DM with $m_χ\sim$~TeV in an RS geometry with warp factor $πkR\simeq 31.7$ and the $Z'$ a KK mode with natural TeV-ish scale mass in which the ratio of neutrino to quark coupling is also natural, giving a mass splitting of $δ\sim 300$~keV, and show how it could explain the LZ signal while being consistent with existing constraints.

</details>


### [178] [Mixing-suppressed inelastic dark matter: a minimal model for the LZ 248 keV event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09138)
*Seung J. Lee,Taewook Youn*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个最小的Majorana单态-矢量类二重态模型，解释LZ实验在248 keV附近观测到的单核反冲事件；通过单态-二重态混合抑制Z玻色子诱导的过量事例率，同时保持反冲谱形状，并协调太阳捕获、共湮灭、冷却机制与暗物质丰度约束，最终给出质量劈裂δ ≈ 331–341 keV、质量≈730–733 GeV的可检验参数区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LZ实验观测到的孤立248 keV核反冲事件，同时规避标准电弱Z跃迁导致的过量事例（数千个），并协调暗物质太阳捕获、热化、冷却、共湮灭及宇宙学丰度等多重约束。

Method: 构建含Majorana单态与矢量类二重态的最小暗物质模型，引入单态-二重态混合以压制Z耦合事例率但不畸变能谱；结合太阳引力加速效应、非热化冷却假设、树图级弹性散射截面、Higgs介导冷却压制、共湮灭条件及IceCube中微子望远镜极限进行联合参数约束。

Result: 获得满足LZ单事件、Ωh² ≈ 0.12、太阳捕获-湮灭平衡破缺、非热分布及IceCube中微子通量上限的二维参数空间：质量劈裂δ = 331–341 keV，暗物质质量M ≈ 730–733 GeV；该区域同时最优拟合LZ反冲谱形状。

Conclusion: 该模型为LZ异常事件提供自洽的粒子物理解释，兼具可检验性（IceCube极限、未来直接探测能谱特征）与理论自然性（U(1)_N对称性起源、R-对称SUSY嵌入可能）。

Abstract: We construct a minimal Majorana singlet-vector-like-doublet model for the single nuclear-recoil-like event reported by LZ near 248 keV. At the splitting inferred from the recoil energy, an electroweak-strength $Z$ transition predicts thousands of events; singlet-doublet mixing suppresses the rate without moving the recoil spectrum. Fixed-coupling interpretations instead require a larger splitting in the extreme halo tail, but solar gravitational acceleration removes this suppression, while the larger splitting shifts recoils toward LZ's empty high-energy sideband. In our model the same mixing suppresses solar capture and annihilation, while the small mass gap required for coannihilation weakens Higgs-mediated cooling, allowing the captured population to remain extended and out of equilibrium. Imposing the relic abundance and normalizing the rate to one event leaves a two-dimensional mass-splitting parameter space. A thermalized population gives the conservative IceCube limit $δ=$300-301 keV; with our nonthermal cooling ansatz and elastic scattering treated at tree level, the nominal limit is $δ=$331-341 keV near the 730-733 GeV relic-density endpoint, which is also favored by the recoil spectrum. We outline a gauged $U(1)_N$ origin for the parity and splitting and a candidate $R$-symmetric supersymmetric embedding.

</details>
