### [179] [Cosmology, Cluster Algebras, and $\boldsymbol{u}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05606)
*Daniel Glazer,Austin Joyce*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究共形耦合标量场在德西特时空中的波函数与关联子所具有的簇代数结构，通过u-变量建立宇宙学运动学与簇变量之间的映射；链状费曼图对应A_{2n−2}型簇代数，环状图对应B_{2n−1}型；利用簇相容性约束符号（symbol）结构，发现链情形下波函数与关联子共享同一簇结构，而环情形下波函数被簇结构唯一确定，关联子则不满足相同相容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将宇宙学可观测量（如波函数、关联子）的解析结构与数学上高度受控的簇代数联系起来，以揭示其深层对称性与约束，并为高阶微扰计算提供bootstrap框架。

Method: 基于共形耦合标量场在dS空间中的树图波函数与动量空间关联子，引入u-变量作为运动学与簇代数的共同参数；通过识别费曼图拓扑（链/环）对应的簇代数类型（A_{2n−2}/B_{2n−1}），导出运动学变量到簇坐标（如y-或x-变量）的非线性映射；进而定义并应用簇相容性（cluster compatibility）条件限制符号字母（symbol letters）集合，实现符号层级的bootstrap。

Result: 1) 链状n点费曼图波函数与关联子均满足A_{2n−2}簇相容性，且后者为前者的特定组合；2) 环状n点波函数唯一满足B_{2n−1}簇相容性，而对应关联子不满足该相容性；3) u-变量提供了运动学空间与簇代数刚性空间之间的自然同构。

Conclusion: 簇代数不仅是描述dS标量波函数组合结构的合适数学语言，更在环状拓扑中展现出对物理解的选择性——仅波函数（而非其乘积形式的关联子）能嵌入B型簇结构，暗示波函数比关联子更基本，且簇相容性可作为有效原理用于构造高阶宇宙学振幅。

Abstract: We explore the cluster algebra structures present in the wavefunction and correlators of conformally coupled scalar fields in de Sitter space. To connect cosmological kinematics to cluster variables, we utilize the so-called $u$-variables that appear on both sides. Since $u$-variables parameterize a rigid space, it is natural to identify the ones that appear from cosmological graphs and those that arise from cluster algebras. This provides a mapping between kinematic variables and cluster variables. For $n$-site chain Feynman graphs, this gives a coordinate-invariant relation to $A_{2n-2}$-type cluster algebras, while in the case of $n$-site cycle graph momentum integrands, the relevant cluster algebra is $B_{2n-1}$, as found previously. Since kinematic variables are mapped to nonlinear combinations of cluster variables, cluster compatibility imposes strong conditions on possible symbol entries. We use this notion of compatibility to bootstrap the symbol. In the chain case, both the cosmological wavefunction and products of lower-point functions satisfy cluster compatibility, with the correlator a particular combination, so that the wavefunction and correlator share the same cluster structure. In the cycle case, however, the wavefunction appears to be uniquely selected by the cluster structure, and the correlator does not display the same compatibility as the wavefunction.

</details>


### [180] [Matrix Holography on an Optical Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05613)
*Robin Löwenberg,Julian Sonner*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于原子系综晶格与时间平均Floquet动力学的模拟协议，用于实现BFSS型玻色子矩阵量子力学中的四体力，具有多项式标度（O(N³)）和强耦合可调性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实验实现大N极限下的全息对偶相关物理（如强耦合规范理论），需克服数字量子计算中电路深度随N增长的瓶颈。

Method: 采用原子系综构成空间晶格，结合时间平均Floquet工程，在绝热/周期驱动下有效生成非局域四体相互作用项；控制参数仅需在每个Floquet周期内施加固定数目的操控阶段。

Result: 实现了目标BFSS类哈密顿量的有效模拟；关键资源——驱动频率带宽——至多按O(N³)标度增长；耦合常数g可超越O(1)，支持强耦合区域模拟。

Conclusion: 该模拟方案提供了一条实验可行的、多项式资源开销的大N矩阵模型量子模拟路径，特别适用于探索全息对偶中的非微扰相。

Abstract: We propose an analog quantum-simulation protocol based on a lattice of atomic ensembles and time-averaged Floquet dynamics to construct the four-body potentials that typically appear in bosonic BFSS-like matrix quantum mechanics. In contrast to gate-based digital implementations requiring deep circuits, our approach generates the target model through a fixed set of control stages per Floquet cycle, keeping the number of consecutive unitaries constant with respect to the matrix size $N$. The principal scaling cost in our construction is given by the frequency range required to control the growing lattice. We show that this cost grows at most as $\mathcal{O}(N^3)$, giving a polynomial scaling route to large$-N$, attractive to experimental implementation. Additionally, we demonstrate that the coupling parameter can be tuned beyond $\mathcal{O}(1)$, enabling the simulation of strong-coupling physics necessary for holographic phases.

</details>


### [181] [Equation of State of a Strongly Coupled Perfect Fluid with Spin from Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05630)
*Andres Anabalon,Horatiu Nastase*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用全息对偶，通过AdS₄中旋转黑洞的引力描述，推导了2+1维强耦合、有限自旋相对论完美流体的状态方程；发现存在临界角速度，超过后流体热力学失稳，并给出QGP的旋转约束4πT/ω > 2.77239；同时指出同一引力解对应双边界，另一边界对应非旋转黑洞但其对偶流体却具有旋转与‘奇异’状态方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立强耦合、有限自旋 relativistic fluid 的热力学描述，尤其在重离子碰撞中产生的旋转夸克胶子等离子体（QGP）背景下，需超越传统无自旋流体力学框架。

Method: 采用AdS/CFT全息对偶，将2+1维边界CFT流体映射至4维渐近AdS时空中的Kerr-AdS黑洞；通过Hawking温度、角速度与Bekenstein-Hawking熵提取流体压强、能量密度与角动量密度；进而导出状态方程并分析Hessian判据下的局部热力学稳定性。

Result: 得到含自旋贡献的状态方程；发现热力学失稳临界条件4πT/ω = 2.77239；揭示同一bulk解支持两个共形边界，其中第二边界对应非旋转黑洞但其对偶流体呈现整体旋转与非标准（'exotic'）状态方程。

Conclusion: 全息方法成功给出了有限自旋强耦合流体的微观可溯状态方程；黑洞角动量直接调控边界流体的内禀自旋与热力学稳定性；双边界结构暗示自旋–几何对偶存在更深层的全息结构。

Abstract: We derive the equation of state of a strongly coupled relativistic perfect fluid with finite spin in $2+1$ dimensions using holography. The dual gravitational description is provided by a spinning black hole in $AdS_4$, whose Hawking temperature, angular velocity, and Bekenstein-Hawking entropy determine the thermodynamic properties of the boundary fluid. We obtain the equation of state and analyze its local thermodynamic stability, finding a critical rotation above which the fluid becomes thermodynamically unstable providing a bound for the rotation of a strongly coupled quark gluon plasma at temperature $T$ and angular velocity $ω$ given by $\frac{4πT}ω> 2.77239$. Remarkably, the same gravitational solution contains a second black hole associated with a second boundary. Although this black hole is non-spinning, its dual fluid rotates and possesses a distinct, ``exotic'' equation of state. Our results provide a holographic equation of state for strongly coupled matter with finite spin and establish a direct connection between black-hole rotation, intrinsic angular momentum, and the thermodynamic stability of relativistic fluids.

</details>


### [182] [AMSB in Truly Confining Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06093)
*Riku Ishikawa,Hitoshi Murayama,Shota Saito*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在小反常介导超对称破缺下的超对称t-禁闭规范理论的形变，并确定了其中全局对称性的破缺模式，这些结果原则上可与非超对称理论的格点模拟进行比较（除一种伪实且手征的情形外）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将超对称禁闭理论的对称性破缺模式与非超对称理论的格点模拟结果进行对照，以探索超对称破缺机制与强耦合动力学之间的联系。

Method: 分析超对称t-禁闭理论在小反常介导超对称破缺下的有效低能描述，运用对称性分析和手征拉氏量方法确定全局对称性破缺模式。

Result: 明确了各类t-禁闭理论中R-对称性、味对称性及手征对称性的破缺模式；特别指出伪实且手征情形因拓扑障碍无法直接对应格点模拟。

Conclusion: 小反常介导破缺下的t-禁闭理论提供了可控的强耦合超对称模型，其对称性破缺结构为理解非超对称禁闭理论的真空结构提供了重要参照。

Abstract: We study deformation of supersymmetric $t$-confining (truly confininig) gauge theories with small anomaly mediation of supersymmetry breaking. We identify breaking pattern of global symmetries in the theories. These results can be compared in priciple to lattice simulation of non-supersymmetric theories (except for one of the cases where the theory is pseudoreal and chiral).

</details>


### [183] [Birth and Death in Two-color ABJM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05653)
*Marco S. Bianchi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文精确求解了有限秩U(2)×U(2) ABJM理论中半BPS算符两点度规的主导量子修正，在Schur基下该两圈修正构成作用于两行Young图链上的Jacobi算子，并通过基态变换关联到以Plancherel测度（限制于至多两行）为稳态分布的可逆生灭过程；其本征模为Racah多项式，大n极限对应径向Ornstein-Uhlenbeck动力学，且等价于受限SU(2) Casimir算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解ABJM理论中半BPS算符的量子修正结构，特别是在有限群秩下保持可解性的非微扰框架，揭示规范/引力对偶中度规量子涨落的代数与概率结构。

Method: 在耦合无关的Schur正交基下计算U(2)×U(2) ABJM中半BPS两点函数的两圈量子修正；应用基态变换将Jacobi算子映射为可逆生灭过程；利用正交多项式理论识别本征函数为Racah多项式；通过渐近分析建立与径向Ornstein-Uhlenbeck算子的联系；构造等价双自旋系统并识别其与受限SU(2) Casimir的对应。

Result: 两圈度规修正严格表示为作用于两行Young图链上的Jacobi算子；该算子经ground-state transform后成为以条件Plancherel测度为平稳分布的生灭过程；本征函数为Racah多项式；大n极限下动力学收敛于径向Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程；存在双自旋实现，且该Jacobi算子等于SU(2)⊗SU(2)中受SU(2)_diag约束的二次Casimir算子。

Conclusion: ABJM理论中半BPS两点函数的量子修正承载深层代数-概率结构：其Jacobi性质、Racah本征谱、Plancherel统计性及SU(2) Casimir实现共同表明，有限秩规范理论的量子几何可被编码为可积随机过程与紧致群表示论的精确对应。

Abstract: We solve exactly the leading quantum deformation of the two-point metric of half-BPS operators in $U(2)\times U(2)$ ABJM theory at finite rank. In the natural coupling-independent Schur frame, its tree-normalized two-loop correction is a Jacobi operator on the chain of two-row Young diagrams. A ground-state transform maps it to a reversible birth--death process whose stationary distribution is the Plancherel measure conditioned on diagrams with at most two rows. Its eigenmodes are Racah polynomials, its large-$n$ limit approaches radial Ornstein--Uhlenbeck dynamics, and an equivalent two-spin description identifies the Jacobi operator with a restricted $SU(2)$ Casimir.

</details>


### [184] [Searching for Bell-CHSH-Violating Bose Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05685)
*T. Newton,S. P. Sorella*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了相对论性自由玻色场中贝尔不等式的显式有界楔形局域可观测量，采用精确谱纤维方法，严格证明了楔形局域性、自伴性、幺正性和精确反对易性；通过平移胞和反射分支构造的可观测量分别实现了可控误差下的贝尔违背，证实了简单有界局域算符可在相对论量子场论中构造性揭示贝尔非定域性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在相对论量子场论（RQFT）框架下，如何构造显式、有界、局域的可观测量以严格验证贝尔非定域性，是基础量子理论与局域性原理交叉的关键问题；此前多数构造依赖无界算符或理想化假设，缺乏严格的算子代数实现。

Method: 采用精确谱纤维（spectral-fibre）方法，将楔形区域对应的阿基米德代数（wedge algebra）中的规范对（canonical pair）映射为可测纤维对合（fibre involutions）；利用Haag-Kastler公理框架，结合调制算子与反射对称性，构造平移胞（translation-cell）及含反射分支（reflection branches）的有界自伴可观测量。

Result: （1）首次给出自由玻色场中楔形局域有界可观测量的显式构造；（2）严格证明其楔形局域性、自伴性、幺正性与精确反对易性；（3）平移胞构造给出带可控无穷尾误差的贝尔违背；（4）引入反射分支后贝尔违背显著增强；（5）确立该违背是严格有效的下界，而非数学人工产物。

Conclusion: 有界、局域、构造性可观测量足以在相对论量子场论中实质性地揭示贝尔非定域性；该结果弥合了代数QFT与量子信息中贝尔检验之间的技术鸿沟，并为未来在共形场论或相互作用模型中推广提供了谱纤维范式。

Abstract: We construct explicit bounded wedge-local observables for Bell inequalities in a relativistic free Bose field using an exact spectral-fibre approach. The construction turns the relevant canonical pair into measurable fibre involutions and provides an operator-algebraic proof of wedge locality, self-adjointness, unitarity, and exact anticommutation. Translation-cell observables yield an explicit Bell violation with a controlled infinite-tail error, while inclusion of reflection branches produces a substantially larger violation. These results demonstrate that simple bounded local observables can expose Bell nonlocality constructively in relativistic quantum field theory and that the translation-cell result is not artifact but a strictly valid lower bound.

</details>


### [185] [Landau and cluster structures of one-loop amplitudes in ${\mathcal N}=4$ SYM in dimensional regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05716)
*Athanasia-Konstantina Angelopoulou,Ruth Britto,Matteo Parisi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了一圈平面N=4超对称杨-米尔斯理论中散射振幅的符号结构，揭示了广义幺正性、Landau分析与簇邻接性之间的深层联系；通过最大切割领头奇点确定权重二符号的LS部分，利用两质量三角关系简化盒图贡献，并证明全有理符号（含红外发散部分）满足旗簇代数Fl_{2,4;n}中的簇邻接性；还提出并验证了Gr(4,n)中更强的簇性质猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解散射振幅中广义幺正性、Landau奇点结构与簇邻接性这三类核心约束如何协同作用，尤其在存在红外发散和代数字母的通用一圈振幅中。

Method: 基于维度正规化下任意圈数、手征性和粒子数的一圈平面N=4 SYM振幅，运用最大切割领头奇点构造权重二符号的LS部分，结合全局留数定理推导盒系数的两质量三角关系，并通过树级amplituhedron投影几何解释其本质；利用旗簇代数Fl_{2,4;n}和Gr(4,n)的结构分析符号字母的邻接性。

Result: 1) 权重二符号可分解为LS部分、代数四质量部分及剩余项；2) 盒图贡献的字母抵消由两质量三角关系解释，该关系被严格证明并赋予amplituhedron几何意义；3) 全有理符号（含IR发散）严格满足Fl_{2,4;n}簇邻接性；4) 在四括号依赖扇区，提出并证得NMHV情形下Gr(4,n)更强的簇邻接性猜想；5) 四质量代数字母呈现Sklyanin括号模式。

Conclusion: 广义幺正性、Landau分析与簇邻接性并非孤立约束，而是在振幅符号结构中统一实现：LS部分编码Cut-Landau对应，三角关系体现环路几何到树图几何的投影一致性，而簇邻接性则作为普适代数约束贯穿有理与代数字母——这为构建更普适的振幅几何与代数框架提供了关键线索。

Abstract: Generalized unitarity, Landau analysis, and cluster adjacency encode complementary aspects of scattering amplitudes. We use one-loop planar $\mathcal N=4$ SYM amplitudes in dimensional regularization, at arbitrary multiplicity and helicity, to make their interface explicit. The weight-two symbol decomposes into an LS part, in which maximal-cut leading singularities furnish the coefficients and Landau loci associated with nested cuts organize the ordered symbol entries, an algebraic four-mass sector, and residual terms. Cancellations of certain letters contributed by individual box integrals, as well as further simplifications, are explained by the two-mass triangle relations among box coefficients. We prove these relations using a BCFW-like application of the global residue theorem and show that they can be understood geometrically as different dissections of the same region in the tree amplituhedron obtained by projecting the loop geometry of a triple cut. We then prove that the full rational symbol, including its infrared-divergent part, obeys cluster adjacency in the flag cluster algebra $\mathrm{Fl}_{2,4;n}$ for all multiplicities and helicities. Within the sector depending only on momentum-twistor four-brackets, we conjecture a stronger cluster property in $\operatorname{Gr}(4,n)$ for all helicities and prove it for NMHV amplitudes: the amplitude admits a representation in which every pole of each coefficient is compatible with both symbol entries. Finally, we observe that the algebraic four-mass letters, although non-rational, exhibit a suggestive Sklyanin-bracket pattern.

</details>


### [186] [Non-critical M-Theory and the Resolved Conifold: Refinement and Nonperturbative Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06075)
*Fengjun Xu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将Hořava-Keeler对应推广至精炼（refined）情形，建立了有限温非临界M理论与精炼A模型解析化簇（resolved conifold）之间的精确对偶；其大势（grand potential）复现了精炼Gopakumar–Vafa展开，角化学势对应Ω-背景偏离自对偶点ε₁ = −ε₂；在有限热半径下，与Hattab–Palti（未精炼）和Chuang（精炼）提出的非微扰conifold完备化完全匹配；一粒子Schwinger被积函数及其积分围道均由非临界M理论的谱与预解式给出，实现了这些完备化的微观谱学实现；该对应唯一确定了非常数BPS扇区与三次局域贡献，而多项式不确定性和模无关常映射扇区需额外归一化；并解释了为何解析化conifold的单一原始无自旋多重态使该识别成为可能，同时指出推广至更一般的局部Calabi–Yau几何所需补充的荷与自旋依赖的微观数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立非临界M理论与精炼拓扑弦A模型之间的微观对偶，特别是将已知的Hořava–Keeler对应推广至refined setting，并阐明非微扰完备化（如Hattab–Palti与Chuang方案）的谱学起源。

Method: 利用非临界M理论的谱结构与预解式构造一粒子Schwinger被积函数及积分围道；通过大势展开与refined Gopakumar–Vafa不变量比对；在有限热半径下验证与已知非微扰conifold完备化的精确匹配；分析BPS谱、Ω-背景变形、局域贡献与多项式模糊性的分离。

Result: 1) 精炼Hořava–Keeler对应成立：旋转非临界M理论真空的大势精确复现refined GV展开；2) 角化学势等价于Ω-背景参数ε₁, ε₂的非自对偶形变；3) 一粒子Schwinger被积函数及其围道具有明确的非临界M理论谱学起源；4) 完全重现Hattab–Palti（unrefined）与Chuang（refined）的非微扰conifold完备化；5) 唯一确定了非恒定BPS扇区与三次局域贡献，剩余多项式模糊性与常映射扇区需独立归一化。

Conclusion: 解析化conifold的单一原始无自旋BPS多重态是实现该精炼对应的关键结构约束；该工作为局部Calabi–Yau上refined拓扑弦提供了首个具微观M理论基础的非微扰完成方案；向更一般几何（如含多支链、高秩规范群或自旋激发）的推广需引入荷与自旋分辨的谱数据及相应预解式结构。

Abstract: We extend the Hořava--Keeler correspondence between finite-temperature non-critical M-theory and the resolved-conifold A-model to the refined theory. The grand potential of the stationary rotating non-critical M-theory vacuum reproduces the refined Gopakumar--Vafa expansion, with the angular chemical potential deforming the $Ω$-background away from the self-dual locus $\eps_1=-\eps_2$. At finite thermal radius, we establish an exact match with the nonperturbative conifold completions proposed by Hattab--Palti and by Chuang in the unrefined and refined cases, respectively. Both the one-particle Schwinger integrand and its integration cycle arise from the non-critical M-theory spectrum and resolvent, providing a microscopic spectral realization of these completions. The correspondence fixes the nonconstant BPS sector and the cubic local contribution, while the remaining polynomial ambiguity and the modulus-independent constant-map sector require separate normalization. We explain why the single primitive spinless multiplet of the resolved conifold makes this identification possible, and discuss what additional charge- and spin-dependent microscopic data would be needed for an extension to more general local Calabi--Yau geometries.

</details>


### [187] [The Wilson-line-dressed charged sector of scalar QED: superselection and the infraparticle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06224)
*Gordon W. Semenoff,Conor Waterfield*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种显式保持规范与洛伦兹不变性的微扰方法，用于研究标量QED中Wilson线修饰标量场算符所产生带电态的有限时空演化；通过Osterwalder-Schrader反射与欧氏拼接，将两个修饰态的重叠表示为欧氏两点函数，并在Stueckelberg光子质量作为红外截断（保持BRST、规范与洛伦兹不变性）下进行重整化微扰计算；结果揭示了‘云正交性’（cloud orthogonality）——非平行Wilson线修饰态的重叠以红外调节器的幂律消失，其指数由夹角决定，是一圈精确的红外类cusp反常维数；由此导致按修饰方向超选择；在每个超选择扇区内，两点函数红外有限但呈现infraparticle结构：质量壳极点被支割线端点取代，导致动量空间渐近分布呈幂律衰减，其指数一圈精确可算；该幂律标度律在宽泛的固有时窗口内鲁棒成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决带电态在规范理论中缺乏良好定义的红外问题，统一微扰可计算性与代数/非微扰揭示的结构性质（如超选择、infraparticle），并建立显式、规范不变的态构造与演化框架。

Method: Osterwalder-Schrader反射+欧氏拼接将希尔伯特空间内积转化为欧氏两点函数；采用Stueckelberg光子质量作为BRST/规范/洛伦兹不变的红外截断；在重整化微扰论中严格计算Wilson线修饰算符的欧氏关联函数。

Result: 1) 非平行Wilson线修饰态满足‘云正交性’，重叠~m_γ^{2Γ_cusp(θ)}，Γ_cusp(θ)为一圈精确红外cusp反常维数；2) 带电态按Wilson线方向超选择；3) 每个超选择扇区内两点函数红外有限，但谱密度在质量壳处无极点，而具支割线，体现infraparticle；4) 渐近传播呈固有时τ^{-α}幂律，α一圈精确且依赖于修饰与运动方向；5) 该幂律在宽固有时窗内稳定。

Conclusion: Wilson线修饰提供了一族规范不变的带电渐近态，其红外结构（云正交性、超选择、infraparticle幂律传播）可在显式微扰框架下完全解析刻画，且核心指数具一圈精确性，表明这些现象根植于规范对称性与红外动力学的基本张力。

Abstract: We develop a manifestly gauge and Lorentz invariant perturbative calculus to study the finite time and distance evolution of the electrically charged states created by Wilson-line-dressed scalar field operators in scalar quantum electrodynamics. By an Osterwalder-Schrader reflection and Euclidean gluing, the overlap of two dressed states is expressed as a Euclidean 2 point function of dressed operators, which is then evaluated in renormalized perturbation theory with a Stueckelberg photon mass as a gauge, BRST and Lorentz invariant infrared cutoff. This renders explicit and computable in closed form several structural features of the charged sector that have so far been accessible mainly through non-perturbative or algebraic arguments. We find that states dressed by non-parallel Wilson lines are ``cloud orthogonal". Their overlap vanishes as a power of the infrared regulator, with an exponent that depends only on the angle between the two dressings and is an infrared analogue of the cusp anomalous dimension with the difference that it is one-loop exact whenever the charged matter is mass-gapped. Charged states are thereby superselected by the orientation of their dressing. Within a superselection sector the 2 point function is infrared finite but exhibits the infraparticle. The mass shell pole is replaced by the edge of a branch cut. As a result, the asymptotic spread of a dressed charge is a power law in proper time with a one-loop-exact computable exponent depending on the dressing and on the direction of motion. We show that although cloud orthogonality and the infraparticle are each extremely sensitive to fundamental infrared cutoffs, the infraparticle scaling law persists across a wide window of proper time.

</details>


### [188] [Finite stress-tensor moment tomography of boundary-graviton coherences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06363)
*Dyuman Bhattacharya,Robert B. Mann*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了AdS3引力中边界引力子的量子态层析，发现利用应力张量矩进行态重构时，阶数为N的测量对水平N态不完全，而N+1阶测量则信息完备；该阈值在左右手理论及重态半经典极限下均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AdS3/CFT2中边界引力子（Virasoro descendant态）的可观测性与量子态层析的最小测量复杂度，尤其针对有限支撑、含层级相干性的未知纯态或密度矩阵块。

Method: 基于Virasoro代数最高权模与非退化Shapovalov形式，构建应力张量矩（stress moment）作为观测量；通过分析n点相关函数中应力张量在共形边界上的插入阶数与descendant水平间的匹配关系，严格证明信息完备性阈值。

Result: 对单个最高权模中水平N≥2的descendant态，应力矩n体关联测量在n=N时信息不完备（存在正交态给出相同记录），而n=N+1时完备；对支撑至水平L≥2的态，L阶测量不完备，L+1阶可完全重构所有密度矩阵块；该结论推广至全左-右Virasoro代数及重态半经典区域。

Conclusion: 应力矩层析的信息完备性存在普适阶数阈值：测量阶数必须严格超过被探测量子态的最大descendant水平，该结果揭示了AdS3中边界引力子可分辨性的基本量子限制，并为全息量子计量提供严格基准。

Abstract: Einstein gravity in 2+1 dimensions is a topological theory that does not admit the existence of local bulk gravitons. However, $\mathrm{AdS}_3$ gravity with Brown-Henneaux boundary conditions does admit the existence of boundary gravitons, which are described by Virasoro descendants. An observer living on the asymptotic boundary can use finite order moments of the boundary stress tensor as a probe of these states. We fix one highest-weight module and assume that the Shapovalov forms are nondegenerate. We also assume that the unknown state the observer is probing has a known finite descendant support and that interlevel coherence is part of the unknown data. We find that, given a descendant at level $N\geq2$, stress moment tomography at order $N$ (meaning $N$ stress moment insertions) is informationally incomplete, while tomography with stress moments of order $N+1$ is informationally complete. This is to say that, on the unitary regular locus, orthogonal pure states can have the same complete order-$N$ record. Likewise, for exact finite-window result for levels through $L\geq2$, tomography of order $L$ is incomplete while tomography of order $L+1$ recovers every density-matrix block. Corresponding exact thresholds hold in the full left-right theory. In heavy sectors for which a semiclassical bulk interpretation exists, these thresholds say when finite-support boundary-graviton states first become distinguishable.

</details>


### [189] [Fermionic hidden zeros](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06552)
*Yong Zhang,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发现并证明了无质量费米子树图振幅中一类普遍的'隐藏零点'，这些零点出现在特定动量构型（矩形运动学流形）及物种依赖的'桥条件'下，其普适性源于世界面分析中的局域融合通道，并可推广至高维规范与引力振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示无质量费米子参与的散射振幅中系统性消失行为的深层结构与普适机制，统一理解规范理论与引力理论中费米子相关的隐藏零点现象。

Method: 采用世界面（worldsheet）分析方法，结合色序振幅、矩形运动学流形约束、物种依赖的桥条件（fermion–fermion bridge由矢量流χ_iγ^μχ_j决定，混合/纯胶子桥由协变收缩确定），并利用D=4,6,10维超对称性与伽马迹无条件极化构造。

Result: 证明任意偶数个无质量实伴随费米子与胶子的色序规范振幅在D=4,6,10维于矩形动量构型加桥条件下严格为零；该机制可推广至D=10含任意偶数个无迹极化gravitinos的引力振幅，以及Einstein-Yang-Mills单迹振幅中含gravitons与adjoint gluinos的情形。

Conclusion: 费米子隐藏零点具有有限局域结构，其起源是基本融合通道ff→g、fg/gf→f、gg→g的世界面局域性，不随费米子数增加而引入新桥结构，表明该现象是规范与引力振幅中共有的普适代数-几何特征。

Abstract: We uncover and prove a general class of hidden zeros in tree-level amplitudes with massless fermions. For arbitrary even numbers and arbitrary arrangements of massless real adjoint fermions and gluons, color-ordered gauge-theory amplitudes in $D=4,6,10$ vanish on general rectangular kinematic loci supplemented by species-dependent bridge conditions. The fermion--fermion bridge is governed simply by the vector current $χ_iγ^μχ_j$, while mixed and gluonic bridges are fixed by the corresponding gauge-covariant contractions. A worldsheet analysis proves the result at arbitrary multiplicity and traces its universality to the local fusion channels $ff\to g$, $fg/gf\to f$, and $gg\to g$, with no new primitive bridge structures appearing at higher fermion multiplicity. The same mechanism extends to ten-dimensional amplitudes with gravitons and arbitrary even numbers of gravitinos in factorized gamma-traceless polarizations, and to single-trace Einstein-Yang-Mills amplitudes with adjoint gluinos and gravitons. These results expose a finite local structure underlying fermionic hidden zeros across gauge and gravitational amplitudes.

</details>


### [190] [Constructing the Monopole Formula for $A$-Type Good Quivers via Quiver Yangians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06711)
*Tiantai Chen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于quiver Yangian与Coulomb分支代数的猜想对应关系，为3D N=4良构A型quiver理论（即T_ρ(SU(N))理论）的monopole公式提供了quiver Yangian诠释；通过其在1/2-BPS涡旋上的作用，构造了适配边界条件的生成元，并导出Slodowy切片S^gl_N_ρ上以U(N) Casimir为变量的经典关系，最终将monopole公式的Hall-Littlewood表达式重构为截断平移quiver Yangian的Hilbert级数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立quiver Yangian与3D N=4超对称规范理论中monopole公式之间的代数结构联系，特别是T_ρ(SU(N))类理论的Coulomb分支几何刻画。

Method: 利用quiver Yangian在1/2-BPS涡旋模空间上的作用，定义边界适配生成元；将其映射至Slodowy切片S^{𝔤𝔩_N}_ρ，并用U(N) Casimir算子写出经典关系；比对Hall-Littlewood型monopole公式的fugacity展开以验证代数重构。

Result: 证明截断平移quiver Yangian的Hilbert级数精确复现T_ρ(SU(N))理论的monopole公式；边界生成元与U(N) Casimir间满足Slodowy切片上的经典关系；fugacity匹配确认该代数结构正确编码了Coulomb分支的几何与计数信息。

Conclusion: quiver Yangian可作为T_ρ(SU(N))理论Coulomb分支的有效代数模型，其截断平移版本的Hilbert级数完全捕获monopole公式所描述的物理与几何数据，从而强化了quiver Yangian/Coulomb代数对应猜想。

Abstract: Based on the conjectured (and checked for tree-type quivers) quiver Yangian/Coulomb branch algebra correspondence, we give a quiver Yangian interpretation of the monopole formulas for 3D $\mathcal N=4$ good $A$-type quiver gauge theories, also known as the $T_ρ(SU(N))$ theories. Using the algebra action on the $\frac12$-BPS vortices, we reorganize the generators of the truncated shifted quiver Yangian into boundary-adapted generators, and obtain the classical relations in terms of $U(N)$ Casimirs on the Slodowy slice $\mathcal S^{\mathfrak{gl}_N}_ρ$, whose coordinates are given by these boundary-adapted generators. We also show that the boundary-adapted generators and the Casimir relations have the correct fugacities as predicted by the Hall-Littlewood expression of the monopole formula for $T_ρ(SU(N))$, hence reconstructing it as the Hilbert series of the truncated shifted quiver Yangian.

</details>


### [191] [Dual N=1 Lagrangians for Argyres-Douglas theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06995)
*Minseok Cho,Jaewon Song*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发现了新的一类四维N=1拉格朗日规范理论，其红外不动点为N=2 Argyres-Douglas理论（(A₁,A₂ₙ)等型），其对偶描述为带flip场的SU(2)ⁿ四叉规范理论，与已有的Sp(n)/SU(n+1)描述构成对偶，实现了quiver中秩与节点数的交换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造N=2 Argyres-Douglas理论的N=1拉格朗日对偶描述，以深化对S-duality、IR/UV对应及超对称规范动力学的理解。

Method: 通过分析超共形指数、a-函数单调性、中心电荷匹配及quiver结构变换，构造并验证SU(2)^n带flip场的N=1理论与(A₁,Aₖ)/(A₁,Dₖ)型AD理论的红外等价性。

Result: 给出了(A₁,A₂ₙ)、(A₁,A₂ₙ₊₁)、(A₁,D₂ₙ₊₁)、(A₁,D₂ₙ₊₂)四类AD理论的新型N=1 SU(2)^n quiver对偶，含规范单态flip场，并确立其与Sp(n)/SU(n+1)描述之间的quiver rank-node对偶性。

Conclusion: SU(2)^n带flip场的N=1理论构成一类普适且可计算的Lagrangian框架，统一刻画多类AD奇点的红外物理，揭示了quiver几何与AD分类间的深层对应。

Abstract: We find a new set of four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=1$ Lagrangian gauge theories that flow in the infrared to $\mathcal{N}=2$ Argyres-Douglas theories of type $(A_1, A_{2n})$, $(A_1, A_{2n+1})$, $(A_1, D_{2n+1})$, and $(A_1, D_{2n+2})$. The new dual theories are given by $SU(2)^n$ quiver gauge theories with a number of gauge-singlet flip fields. This description provides dual theories for the previous $\mathcal{N}=1$ Lagrangian realizations given by $Sp(n)$ or $SU(n+1)$ gauge theories, exchanging the rank and the number of nodes in a quiver diagram.

</details>


### [192] [Tachyon-massless couplings at order $α'$ in bosonic string theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07221)
*Mohammad R. Garousi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过T-对偶性推导了玻色弦理论中tachyon与无质量场在α'阶（四导数阶）的有效作用量耦合，发现仅存在6个非零耦合项，由两个参数刻画；其中一个参数由零tachyon极限确定，另一个由四tachyon散射振幅固定，并指出tachyon势能可写为(2/α')(-1+cos T)，其真空期望值T=π导致AdS背景，宇宙学常数Λ=-4/α'。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决玻色弦理论经典有效作用量中tachyon耦合的结构问题，特别是验证‘仅含偶数个tachyon场’的猜想，并构建满足T-对偶性的最低阶有效拉氏量。

Method: 运用T-对偶性约束，结合高阶场重定义消除冗余项，构造四导数阶下协变导数形式的最小耦合基（14项），再施加T-对偶不变性将其约化；最后通过匹配零tachyon极限和球面四tachyonS矩阵元定标系数。

Result: 得到6个独立T-对偶允许的四导数耦合项，含两个自由参数；确定tachyon四次项T⁴系数为正；tachyon质量项与四次项共同支持势能形式V(T)=(2/α')(-1+cos T)；该势能在T=π处取极小，诱导AdS₄背景，Λ=-4/α'。

Conclusion: 玻色闭弦tachyon的有效动力学由T-对偶严格约束，其势能具有余弦形式，tachyon凝聚导致非平凡真空——负常曲率AdS时空，为弦论中tachyon凝聚提供自洽低能实现。

Abstract: Recently, it has been proposed that the classical effective action of bosonic string theory should contain only couplings with an even number of tachyon fields. We employ the T-duality procedure to derive such couplings at order \(α'\) (four-derivative order) for the tachyon and massless fields. We first observe that, through higher-derivative field redefinitions of the tachyon potential term, one can choose a scheme in which, apart from the tachyon potential itself, all remaining couplings involve only covariant derivatives of the tachyon and the massless fields. We then construct a minimal basis of such couplings at four-derivative order, consisting of 14 independent terms. Imposing T-duality reduces this set to 6 nonzero couplings, expressed in terms of two unfixed parameters. One of these parameters is determined by matching to the known effective action in the zero-tachyon limit, while the other is fixed by comparison with the sphere-level S-matrix element of four tachyon vertex operators. This latter comparison also establishes that the coefficient of the \(T^4\) term in the tachyon potential is positive. Remarkably, the tachyon mass term and this quartic term are consistent with a potential of the form \((2/α')(-1+\cos T)\), which implies that the closed string tachyon condenses to the minimum of the potential at \(T=π\), thereby generating an Anti-de Sitter spacetime with cosmological constant \(Λ=-4/α'\).

</details>


### [193] [Can one hear the shape of a black hole singularity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07514)
*Sean A. Hartnoll,Alexander Zhiboedov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过WKB分析方法，从渐近AdS黑洞的大overtone准正规模频率中提取内部Kasner奇点附近的指数，并揭示Kasner跃迁与准正规模之间的对应关系，建立了overtone数n与到奇点的固有时之间的映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞内部奇点附近的动力学结构，特别是BKL混沌行为在边界可观察量（如准正规模）中的体现。

Method: 采用WKB近似方法分离由近奇点时空决定的非解析指数与非普适数据（如边界到奇点的光学距离），并分析大overtone极限下∂ωₙ/∂k²在k=0处的色散行为。

Result: 发现Kasner指数可从大n极限下∂ωₙ/∂k²|_{k=0}的渐进行为中干净提取；Kasner跃迁在准正规模中可观测，并给出n与固有时τ之间的标度关系。

Conclusion: 准正规模的高频谱编码了黑洞内部奇点的Kasner几何结构，为通过边界谱探测强引力场内部动力学提供了新途径。

Abstract: We compute the large overtone quasinormal frequencies $ω_n$ of asymptotically AdS black holes with interior near-singularity Kasner epochs. We show that the Kasner exponents can be extracted cleanly from the large $n$ behaviour of the dispersion $\partial ω_n/\partial k^2$ at $k=0$, where $k$ is a boundary momentum. We show that Kasner transitions, as occur in BKL near-singularity chaos, are audible in the quasinormal frequencies and reveal a map between overtone number $n$ and proper time to the singularity. Our results are obtained using a WKB analysis that isolates non-analytic exponents, determined by the near-singularity spacetime, from non-universal data such as the optical distance from the boundary to the singularity. We check our formulae in several numerical models.

</details>


### [194] [Exact spin form factors and correlations at a massive Kramers-Wannier interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07612)
*Zoltan Bajnok,Yizhuang Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文精确计算了标度Ising模型中分离有序与无序相的Kramer-Wannier界面在有限体积下的自旋形因子和两点关联函数；通过两种互补的格点实现（自对偶非均匀Ising链与交换时空后的混合热平均），导出NS-R自旋矩阵元及可因式分解的Fredholm行列式形式的界面关联函数，并由缺陷TCSA与费米子自旋链计算独立验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解临界与非临界相变界面的精确量子结构，特别是Kramer-Wannier对偶性所定义的拓扑缺陷在标度极限下的可观测量。

Method: 结合自对偶非均匀Ising链的连续极限（含符号变化的Majorana质量剖面）与Euclidean时空交换后的Toeplitz/Fredholm方法；辅以缺陷Truncated Conformal Space Approach (TCSA) 和严格可解费米子自旋链数值验证。

Result: 获得有限体积下精确的NS-R分段自旋矩阵元、界面两点关联函数的可因式分解Fredholm行列式表达式，以及界面激发谱的完整解析刻画。

Conclusion: Kramer-Wannier界面在标度Ising模型中构成一个严格可解的缺陷CFT对象，其形因子与关联函数具有明确的解析结构，证实了Majorana质量畴壁与NS-R超对称结构之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We determine exact finite-volume spin form factors and two-point correlations for a massive Kramer--Wannier interface in the scaling Ising model, separating ordered and disordered phases. The interface admits two complementary lattice realizations: a self-dual inhomogeneous Ising chain whose continuum limit has a sign-changing Majorana mass profile, and, after exchanging Euclidean space and time, a mixed thermal average amenable to Toeplitz/Fredholm methods. Combining these descriptions, we obtain the exact Neveu-Schwarz-Ramond spin matrix elements and a factorized Fredholm determinant for the interface correlator. Defect TCSA and independent fermionic spin-chain calculations confirm the predicted spectrum and matrix elements.

</details>


### [195] [(Anti-)De Sitter null strings and Carroll-Weyl symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07765)
*Ulf Lindström,Bo Sundborg*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖构造d维德西特（dS_d）零张力弦的方法：通过将标准无张力弦提升至R^{1,d}，并引入世界面尺度对称性规范场，从而在保持SO(1,d)对称性的同时破缺平移不变性；选取dS对称的规范后，得到一个具有明确d维靶空间解释的Carroll-Weyl对称弦作用量，澄清了其并非(d+1)维闵氏空间中的弦模型；该方法可推广至反德西特（AdS）背景，并暗示代数构造弯曲时空几何的普适潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Carroll-Weyl弦模型靶空间解释模糊的问题，澄清其是否对应(d+1)维平直时空中的弦，并为在(d)A)dS背景下构建张力为零的弦理论提供严格、对称性保持的构造方案。

Method: 将标准张力为零的弦提升至嵌入空间R^{1,d}，通过对世界面尺度对称性进行规范固定（gauging），引入破坏平移但保留SO(1,d)对称性的项；选择De Sitter对称的规范条件，导出d维有效作用量，并识别其为Carroll-Weyl对称弦作用量。

Result: 获得具有明确d维dS_d靶空间解释的张力为零弦作用量；证明Carroll-Weyl弦不描述(d+1)维闵氏空间中的弦，从而消除了近期文献中提出的概念混淆；该构造可自然推广至AdS_d背景；首次实现仅通过代数操作（无预设几何）生成dS_d曲率。

Conclusion: 张力为零的弦可在dS_d与AdS_d中严格实现，其作用量由嵌入空间R^{1,d}上的Carroll-Weyl对称性与尺度对称性规范共同决定；该框架不仅统一理解零张力弦的几何起源，也为代数构造弯曲时空提供了新范式。

Abstract: We construct a $dS_d$ null string in a novel way by lifting the standard tensionless string to $R^{1,d}$. Gauging worldsheet scale symmetry introduces terms in the action breaking translation invariance, but preserving the $dS_d$ symmetry $SO(1,d)$. Choosing a De Sitter symmetric gauge fixing of the scale symmetry yields a $d$ dimensional theory which we show has all the expected properties of $dS_d$ tensionless strings. The action happens to be the intriguing Carrol-Weyl symmetric string action in $R^{1,d}$, now with a clear target space interpretation in $d$ dimensions. That it does not, as recently claimed, represent a ($d+1$)-dimensional string in flat ($d+1$)-dimensional Minkowski space settles an apparent issue with the Carrol-Weyl string model. Furthermore, while there are clear obstacles for similar formulations of tensile strings in (A)dS backgrounds, our new insights are easily modified to the formulation of Anti-De Sitter tensionless strings. That our construction generates a curved geometry purely algebraically may have even wider applications.

</details>


### [196] [Eikonal Scattering of Two D0-branes with Graviton Emission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07788)
*Paolo Di Vecchia,Dawid Maskalaniec*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用边界态形式论研究了两个D0-膜散射过程中无质量闭弦态（引力子与胀子）的辐射，计算了玻色弦与II型超弦中的五点振幅，并分析其场论极限；结果在非零碰撞参数下可由低能有效理论的世界线方法独立重现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解D-膜动力学及其低能有效描述之间的对应关系，特别是通过弦微扰论精确计算辐射过程以检验AdS/CFT类对偶及开/闭弦对偶的自洽性。

Method: 采用边界态（boundary state）形式论构造D0-膜散射初末态，结合共形场论计算含一个闭弦外线（引力子或胀子）的五点树图振幅；随后取α′→0场论极限，并与世界线量子化下的低能有效作用量（如N=2超引力耦合点粒子）计算比对。

Result: 成功导出玻色弦与II型超弦中引力子、胀子辐射的解析五点振幅；其场论极限精确匹配由D0-膜有效作用量（含相对论性动能、Chern-Simons项及标量势）推得的世界线辐射率，验证了开/闭弦对偶在辐射过程中的实现。

Conclusion: D0-膜散射中闭弦辐射的弦论计算与低能世界线描述完全一致，表明边界态方法是连接高能弦动力学与低能有效理论的可靠工具；该结果为D-膜作为黑洞微观态的辐射机制提供了微观基础。

Abstract: We study the emission of a massless closed-string state in the scattering of two D0-branes using the boundary-state formalism. We compute the corresponding five-point amplitudes for graviton and dilaton emission in bosonic string theory and in type II superstring theory, and analyze their field-theory limit. For nonzero impact parameter, the resulting amplitudes are reproduced by an independent calculation in the worldline formulation of the corresponding low-energy theory.

</details>


### [197] [Logarithmic correction to the entropy of near-extremal higher-curvature black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07801)
*Pablo Bueno,Pablo A. Cano,Robie A. Hennigar,Javier Moreno,Ángel J. Murcia,Guido van der Velde*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文建立了高维球对称引力理论与二维Horndeski理论之间的对应，并由此导出近极值黑洞在低温下的普适Jackiw–Teitelboim（JT）引力有效描述；证明了JT熵的对数量子修正S_JT = (3/2) log(T/T_breakdown) 在所有具备二维Horndeski约化能力的理论中具有普适性，并给出了T_breakdown由高维理论数据决定的一般表达式，最后以Lovelock和拟拓扑引力中的具体黑洞解为例进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解近极值黑洞低能动力学的普适结构，特别是量子修正熵的模型无关性；揭示高维引力理论在球对称扇区的共性低能有效描述。

Method: 利用球对称约化将D维引力映射为二维Horndeski理论；对近视界几何进行AdS₂×S^{D−2}渐近分析；提取JT重参数化不变作用量；计算一循环精确JT配分函数并导出熵修正；将T_breakdown通过高维背景场、耦合常数及几何数据显式表达。

Result: 1) 所有 admitting 2D Horndeski约化的D维引力理论，其近极值黑洞低温熵均具相同JT对数修正形式；2) T_breakdown被确定为高维理论中Horndeski系数、标量毛发强度、视界曲率半径及有效cosmological常数的函数；3) Lovelock与Quasitopological引力中电荷/正则黑洞性质均满足该普适性。

Conclusion: 近极值黑洞的JT熵对数修正具有强普适性，源于球对称扇区的共形对称性破缺与AdS₂红外固定点的鲁棒性；该结果支持JT引力作为全息红外普适低能理论的地位，并为高维量子引力的有效场论刻画提供了新范式。

Abstract: The spherically symmetric sector of broad classes of $D$-dimensional gravitational theories can be effectively described by general two-dimensional Horndeski theories. Exploiting this correspondence, we study the low-temperature dynamics of near-extremal black hole solutions of the parent theories by deriving a universal near-horizon effective description in terms of Jackiw--Teitelboim (JT) gravity. We explicitly show that the logarithmic quantum correction to the entropy, $S_{\rm JT}=\frac{3}{2}\log \left(T/T_{\rm breakdown} \right)$, previously derived for near-extremal black holes in Einstein gravity coupled to matter from the one-loop exact JT partition function, is universal across all models admitting an effective two-dimensional Horndeski description. We determine the general form of the scale at which this correction becomes dominant, $T_{\rm breakdown}$, in terms of the data of the $D$-dimensional theories. We illustrate our general results with charged black holes in Lovelock gravities and regular black holes in Quasitopological gravities.

</details>


### [198] [SolutionsX: supergravity theories/solutions as code, with an agentic workflow for machine-verifiable physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07826)
*Vasil Dimitrov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SolutionsX——一个基于Mathematica与xAct的可验证符号计算框架，用于系统化、机器可验地编码和复现广义相对论、超引力等理论中的解析解；它支持张量、微分形式与任意维旋量，并已在多个超引力解及AI代理协同编码任务中验证其可靠性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超引力等高能物理解析解常因约定不清、笔误或代数繁琐而难以独立复现与验证，亟需一种机器可验、自包含、可重用的符号计算记录范式。

Method: 构建基于xAct生态的Mathematica软件包SolutionsX，将物理推导过程封装为可重放内核代码，内置自动验证机制（如Bianchi恒等式、场方程检验）；结合AI编码代理进行盲测实验，并对核心引擎性能与xAct原生工具对比基准测试。

Result: 成功将多个文献中超引力解（含含旋量与通量的D=10/11解）转化为SolutionsX记录；组件引擎性能媲美xAct；三轮盲测中AI代理在≤3小时内完整编码最新论文解并自动通过全部验证。

Conclusion: SolutionsX实现了物理解的‘可计算文献化’（computable documentation），既提升人类研究者复现可靠性，也为AI物理代理提供结构化训练数据与实时内核级验证能力，是迈向可信AI-增强理论物理研究的关键基础设施。

Abstract: A supergravity solution in the literature is an artifact that cannot be verified without re-deriving it: conventions might not be clearly stated, papers have typos, algebra is tedious. SolutionsX is a Mathematica package that turns an xAct environment, whatever physics it may describe, into a stored, machine-verifiable record: code that replays in a fresh kernel, carries its own verification, and can later be fetched for re-use or extension. The shipped examples are supergravity theories and their solutions, but the scope is anything xAct reaches: abstract tensors, differential forms, spinors in any dimension. We follow one published solution through the entire workflow, benchmark the package's component engine against xAct's, and report on three blind runs of an agentic workflow in which an AI coding agent, given the package and a small toolkit, correctly codified a recent paper in under three hours. A database of such records is useful to researchers today and gives agents worked examples to learn from. The package acts as an internal verifier during the thought process of an agent: a claim is checked in the kernel before it is made.

</details>


### [199] [Unitary quantum conformal Bondi-Metzner-Sachs field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07828)
*Daniel Grumiller,Iva Lovrekovic*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有量子共形BMS代数对称性的二维场论的幺正性约束，通过正定性条件限制中心荷c的取值，最终筛选出四个可能的幺正理论候选者（c = 1, 3/2, 13/7, 2），并验证其原初算符谱满足BPS型与全息不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建与渐近平直时空渐近对称性（BMS）兼容的幺正二维共形场论，为平坦空间全息提供微观基础。

Method: 利用量子共形BMS代数的表示论与顶点代数的正定性条件（即幺正性），推导中心荷c的允许取值范围，并结合原初算符谱分析检验BPS与全息不等式。

Result: 得到中心荷的紧致上界c < 2.053；进一步由顶点代数幺正性得出仅c = 1, 3/2, 13/7, 2 四个离散值可行；对应理论的原初谱均满足BPS-like及全息能量-自旋不等式。

Conclusion: 在量子共形BMS对称性下，仅有四个离散中心荷值支持幺正、有良定义的二维场论，暗示该对称类下存在高度受限的、可能具有全息意义的微观理论结构。

Abstract: We impose unitarity constraints on two-dimensional field theories whose symmetries form the quantum conformal Bondi-Metzner-Sachs algebra, which combines conformal symmetries with asymptotic symmetries of asymptotically flat spacetimes. Positivity constraints put bounds on the central charge, 0<c<2.053. Positivity of the associated vertex algebra allows only four central charges, c = 1, 3/2, 13/7, 2. Thus, only four candidates for unitary field theories remain. We calculate their spectra of primaries and show that they are compatible with BPS-like and holographic inequalities.

</details>


### [200] [Diffeomorphism-invariant Approach to Asymptotically Safe Quantum Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07829)
*Friederike Ihssen,Benjamin Knorr,Silas Mezger,Jan M. Pawlowski,Paul P. Sprenger*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的微分同胚不变的渐近安全度规量子引力方法，基于物理启发的重整化群流，确保每一步都保持微分同胚不变性，并能控制算符的相关性计数；所得有效作用量是量子微分同胚不变且背景无关的；作为首次非平凡应用，作者以显式微分同胚不变方式计算了Reuter不动点，并系统讨论了截断依赖性与近似误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统背景依赖重整化方法破坏微分同胚不变性、难以维持算符相关性计数的问题，实现真正背景无关且对称性保持的渐近安全量子引力构造。

Method: 构建微分同胚不变的重整化群核，定义物理启发的RG流，采用完全协变的函数积分框架，在无背景前提下导出量子有效作用量，并在谱正则化下进行截断近似求解Reuter不动点。

Result: 获得量子微分同胚不变且背景无关的有效作用量；首次以显式微分同胚不变方式复现Reuter不动点；量化了正则化方案依赖性及多项式截断引入的系统误差。

Conclusion: 微分同胚不变RG流为渐近安全引力提供了严格协变的框架，证实Reuter不动点具有内在几何本质而非截断人工产物，增强了ASQG作为紫外完备引力理论的可信度。

Abstract: We provide a novel diffeomorphism-invariant approach to asymptotically safe metric quantum gravity. It is based on physics-informed renormalisation group flows with a renormalisation group kernel that guarantees diffeomorphism invariance at each renormalisation group step. Importantly, it also allows us to control and maintain the relevance counting of operators in metric quantum gravity. The resulting effective action is quantum diffeomorphism-invariant and background-independent. As a first non-trivial application, we compute the Reuter fixed point in a manifestly diffeomorphism-invariant way. This computation is augmented with a detailed discussion of regularisation dependence and a systematic estimate of the errors arising from approximations.

</details>


### [201] [Truncated Polyakov bootstrap for BCFTs: Neumann-Dirichlet flow and Ising special transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07841)
*Kaushik Kangsabanik,Apratim Kaviraj,Astha Tiwari*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将截断Polyakov bootstrap方法推广至边界共形场论（BCFT），利用解析泛函框架求解BCFT交叉方程；通过追踪从广义自由场（GFF）Neumann边界到Dirichlet边界的算符谱流，识别非微扰解；并将该方法应用于三维Ising特殊相变，以Wilson-Fisher微扰描述为变形路径，所得BCFT数据与格点结果高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展适用于BCFT的非微扰bootstrap方法，弥补现有Polyakov bootstrap在含边界情形下的缺失，并为强耦合BCFT（如Ising特殊转变）提供定量预言工具。

Method: 基于解析泛函方法（analytic functional framework）构建截断Polyakov bootstrap；引入GFF作为起点，通过连续边界条件变形（Neumann→Dirichlet）实现BCFT数据流追踪；对3d Ising特殊转变，以ε-展开下的Wilson-Fisher理论作为微扰变形基准，结合bootstrap约束进行数值求解。

Result: 建立了BCFT的truncated Polyakov bootstrap形式体系；成功复现并外推了GFF边界变形路径上的算符维数与OPE系数；对3d Ising特殊转变，给出了边界标量算符Δσ_b、Δε_b及边界OPE系数λσσb等关键BCFT数据，与格点QMC结果误差<1%。

Conclusion: 截断Polyakov bootstrap可自然推广至BCFT，且GFF变形流提供了识别物理解的有效机制；该方法兼具解析结构性与数值精度，在临界BCFT问题中具备普适应用前景。

Abstract: We extend the recently proposed truncated Polyakov bootstrap to boundary conformal field theories (BCFTs). The formalism uses the analytic functional framework to find approximate solutions of BCFT crossing. We show that a generic nonperturbative solution can be identified by relating it to a family of deformations around generalized free fields (GFF) with a fixed boundary condition. We show this by tracking the flow of BCFT data from GFF with Neumann boundary condition to that with Dirichlet. The same method is then used for the 3d Ising special transition, using the perturbative Wilson-Fisher description as the deformation to identify it. The BCFT data obtained are in remarkable agreement with existing lattice results where available, and are otherwise new.

</details>


### [202] [Scattering of Null strings - Flipped Vacuum & CHY](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07845)
*Arjun Bagchi,Sachin Grover,Sharang Rajesh Iyer,Amartya Saha,Stephan Stieberger*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究零张力弦的散射，基于ILST作用量导出的二维共形Carroll代数（CCA）的不同真空态与表示，聚焦于实现CCA最高权表示的'翻转'真空，并通过新型顶点算符构建散射振幅，最终在零张力'翻转'弦的质量less态散射中自然导出CHY公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解零张力弦的量子化及其与散射振幅结构（如CHY公式）的深层联系，特别是探索Carroll对称性在弦理论中的作用。

Method: 基于ILST作用量构造经典零弦，分析其残余规范对称性——二维共形Carroll代数（CCA），选取'翻转'真空实现CCA最高权表示；引入与紧化零弦密切关联的新类顶点算符，构建量子散射振幅。

Result: 在零张力'翻转'弦框架下，质量less态的散射振幅精确复现Cachazo-He-Yuan（CHY）公式。

Conclusion: 零张力弦的'翻转'量子化提供了一个自然实现CHY公式的新物理背景，揭示了Carroll对称性与散射振幅普适结构之间的深刻关联。

Abstract: We investigate the scattering of null tensionless strings. Classical null strings, given by the ILST action, give rise to three distinct quantum theories built on different vacua and representations of the underlying 2d Conformal Carroll algebra (CCA) which form the residual gauge symmetries on the null worldsheet. In this paper, we are interested in the quantum theory built out of the so-called ``flipped'' vacuum which realises the highest weight representation of the 2d CCA. By considering a novel class of vertex operators, intimately connected to the compactified null string, we build scattering amplitudes of the theory. We show that one naturally obtains the Cachazo-He-Yuan (CHY) formulae when one considers scattering of massless states in the null ``flipped'' string.

</details>


### [203] [Double Copy from the Flipped Null String](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07846)
*Arjun Bagchi,Sachin Grover,Sharang Rajesh Iyer,Amartya Saha,Stephan Stieberger*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文揭示了玻色零弦在翻转真空中的树图振幅具有双拷贝结构，通过Carrollian世界面实现：其一级矢量关联器给出$(DF)^2$规范理论的CHY运动学半积分核，特定动量-缠绕扇区给出Parke-Taylor因子；二级可分解态则生成两个半积分核，实现Weyl立方引力的对称双拷贝；格点扇区复现BCJ序关系与KLT表示，为双拷贝提供了直接的Carrollian世界面起源，并暗示与$O(d,d)$格点结构兼容的流代数实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立双拷贝结构的世界面起源，特别是从Carrollian对称性出发理解高导数规范/引力理论的CHY和KLT表示。

Method: 分析玻色零弦在翻转真空下的树图振幅，提取不同能级（level-one, level-two）及格点扇区（compact momentum-winding sectors, common external momenta）的关联器结构，比照CHY公式、Parke-Taylor因子、BCJ关系与KLT矩阵。

Result: level-one矢量关联器 = $(DF)^2$理论的CHY kinematic half-integrand；特定紧致扇区 = Parke-Taylor因子；level-two factorized态 → 两个kinematic half-integrands → Weyl-cubed引力的对称双拷贝；共动量格点扇区 → BCJ序与KLT表示；整体结构具备$O(d,d)$对称性。

Conclusion: 双拷贝结构可直接源于Carrollian世界面，且其颜色扇区可由流代数实现，与弦紧致化的$O(d,d)$结构自洽。

Abstract: We study the double-copy structure of tree amplitudes of the bosonic null string in the flipped vacuum. We show that its level-one vector correlator supplies the Cachazo-He-Yuan (CHY) kinematic half-integrand of the higher-derivative $(DF)^2$ gauge theory, while distinguished compact momentum-winding sectors generate the complementary Parke-Taylor factor. For factorized level-two states, the same Carrollian world-sheet produces two kinematic half-integrands and hence the symmetric double copy to the six-derivative Weyl-cubed graviton sector. Lattice sectors with common external momenta reproduce the Bern-Carrasco-Johansson (BCJ) ordering relation and the corresponding field-theory Kawai-Lewellen-Tye (KLT) representation. These results provide a direct Carrollian world-sheet origin of the double copy and point toward a current-algebra realization of the emergent color sector compatible with the $O(d,d)$ structure of the compact lattice.

</details>


### [204] [Entanglement Wedge Reconstruction Beyond the Large N Limit via the Twirled Petz Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07866)
*Arash Alipour Shahmiri,Maryam Sharifian,Niloofar Vardian*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用量子纠错框架，系统地将纠缠楔重构推广到大N展开的次领头阶，通过twirled Petz映射导出显式的双局域修正项，从而为全量子引力背景下的体空间重构提供了可控路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠楔重构在AdS/CFT中是体空间局域性的最锐利表述，但其在半经典以外（即超越大N领头阶）的适用性尚不明确。

Method: 采用量子纠错理论框架，结合twirled Petz映射，推导出包含1/N次领头修正的显式重构公式，并识别出由次领头Petz核控制的双局域修正项。

Result: 成功构造了大N展开下首阶量子修正的显式体重构算符，确立了量子纠错在全息对偶中对超越半经典 regime 的 bulk 重建的核心作用。

Conclusion: 量子纠错不仅是AdS/CFT中bulk重建的自然语言，而且为处理全量子引力效应（如非微扰量子涨落）提供了系统、可控的数学框架。

Abstract: Entanglement wedge reconstruction provides the sharpest formulation of bulk locality in AdS/CFT, identifying the entanglement wedge as the largest bulk region reconstructible from a boundary subregion. While this statement is well understood at leading order in the large N expansion through both modular flow and Petz map reconstruction, its extension beyond the semiclassical regime has remained unclear. In this work, we show that quantum error correction provides the natural framework for addressing this problem. At leading order, we reproduce the known leading-order reconstruction formulas, and by employing the twirled Petz map, we obtain a systematic and explicit method for incorporating subleading 1/N corrections. Beyond the leading approximation, we have obtained an explicit bi-local correction controlled by a subleading Petz kernel. Our results establish a controlled approach to bulk reconstruction beyond large N and sharpening the role of quantum error correction in holography. This framework provides a controlled path toward understanding bulk reconstruction in fully quantum gravitational settings.

</details>


### [205] [Spherical DAHA as an algebra of framed BPS states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07911)
*Kunal Gupta,Pietro Longhi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将4维N=2类S理论中line算符与SL_N skein代数的物理对应推广至GL_N情形，并证明了穿孔环面的GL_2 skein代数同构于GL_2球面双仿射Hecke代数SH^{q,t}_2；该构造基于SU(2) N=2^*理论的q-非阿贝尔化，将skein代数实现为Seiberg-Witten曲线上的量子环面代数；不同Coulomb分支区域给出SH^{q,t}_2的不同表示——弱耦合区对应Macdonald q-差分模（Fenchel-Nielsen坐标），强耦合区对应Fock-Goncharov型cluster实现；这些表示间的过渡由N=2^*理论的vanilla BPS谱通过framed wall-crossing控制，从而为球面DAHA的多种表示提供了统一的物理解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立4d N=2类S理论中line算符与GL_N skein代数的物理实现，并统一描述球面双仿射Hecke代数（spherical DAHA）在不同参数区域（弱/强耦合）下的数学表示。

Method: 利用SU(2) N=2^*理论的q-非阿贝尔化（q-nonabelianization），将GL_2 skein代数实现为Seiberg-Witten曲线上的量子环面代数；结合Fenchel-Nielsen与Fock-Goncharov坐标系，分析Coulomb分支不同区域对应的代数表示；运用framed wall-crossing机制，关联vanilla BPS谱与不同代数呈现之间的过渡。

Result: 证明穿孔环面的GL_2 skein代数同构于GL_2球面双仿射Hecke代数SH^{q,t}_2；揭示SH^{q,t}_2在弱耦合区表现为Macdonald q-差分模，在强耦合区表现为cluster型量子环面代数；framed wall-crossing由N=2^*理论的vanilla BPS谱精确控制，实现不同表示的统一物理框架。

Conclusion: GL_N skein代数可通过4d N=2类S理论的line算符物理实现，且其代数结构（特别是GL_2情形）与球面DAHA深度对应；该对应不仅拓展了Omega背景下量子几何的物理诠释，更通过BPS谱与wall-crossing机制，为spherical DAHA的表示论提供了统一、可计算的物理基础。

Abstract: Line operators of 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ theories of class $\mathcal{S}$ of type $A_{N-1}$ in a half Omega background provide a physical model for $SL_N$ skein algebras. In this paper we extend this correspondence to $GL_N$ skein algebras and prove that the $GL_2$ skein algebra of the punctured torus is isomorphic to the $GL_2$ spherical double affine Hecke algebra ${{\mathbf{S}\ddot{\mathbf{H}}}}^{q,t}_2$. The construction is based on $\mathfrak{q}$-nonabelianization for the $SU(2)$ $\mathcal{N}=2^*$ gauge theory, which realizes the skein algebra as a quantum torus algebra associated with the Seiberg-Witten curve. Different regions of the Coulomb branch yield distinct presentations of ${{\mathbf{S}\ddot{\mathbf{H}}}}^{q,t}_2$. These range from the Macdonald $q$-difference module in Fenchel-Nielsen (weak coupling) charts to cluster-type realizations in Fock-Goncharov (strong coupling) charts. Transitions between these descriptions are governed by the vanilla BPS spectrum of the $\mathcal{N}=2^*$ theory via framed wall-crossing, which provides a unified physical framework for several representations of spherical DAHA.

</details>


### [206] [ABJM/BMN Index Matching in the Plane-Wave Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07872)
*Chi-Ming Chang,Kangning Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明了ABJM理论与BMN矩阵模型在平面波极限下、任意固定正Chern-Simons水平k处的精炼超对称指标在系数层面严格相等；该等式适用于固定多重性的多膜族（即固定有限ABJM规范秩），并在所有纵向动量趋于无穷且其两两差值保持固定时成立；ABJM中带标量场修饰的等磁通单极子对应BMN模型中模糊球膜的真空态，其中多重性计数共形膜数目，磁通大小给出每膜沿M理论圆周的动量；指标层面，相关Higgs留数导出ABJM平面波贡献的对角全纯积分，其单极谐波核与BMN矩阵谐波核一致（仅差一个有限BMN上自旋截断）；有限动量下，完整指标差可分解为非平面波余项与该截断之上的谐波尾项；增大k将余项推向更高fugacity阶数，但不改变剩余核或截断贡献；从而得到早先单膜指标关系的系数级、多膜推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立ABJM理论与BMN矩阵模型在强耦合/大N极限下的精确对偶映射，特别是通过超对称指标这一受保护可观测量，在平面波背景下验证AdS4/CFT3对应中的膜动力学实现。

Method: 运用精炼超对称指标（refined supersymmetric index）作为受保护量，在ABJM理论中采用单极子分形（monopole flux expansion）与Higgs分支留数计算，在BMN模型中采用矩阵谐波展开与模糊球膜真空构造；关键技巧包括对角全纯积分表示、平面波极限下的纵向动量缩放分析、以及谐波核在有限自旋截断下的逐系数比对。

Result: 在任意固定k≥1下，ABJM多膜族（固定多重性）的精炼指标与BMN矩阵模型指标在平面波极限下逐系数相等；ABJM等磁通单极子与BMN模糊球膜真空一一对应；Higgs留数导出的ABJM平面波积分核与BMN矩阵谐波核完全匹配（至BMN上自旋截断L_max = k−1）；指标差被严格分解为非平面波余项与截断外谐波尾项，且k仅平移余项的fugacity展开阶数。

Conclusion: ABJM理论与BMN矩阵模型在平面波极限下存在严格的、系数级的多膜指标对偶，该结果超越了早先单膜情形，证实了M2膜在强耦合下以模糊球构型实现，并为AdS4/CFT3中膜-矩阵对应提供了精确、可计算的定量证据。

Abstract: We establish coefficientwise equality between the refined supersymmetric indices of ABJM theory and the BMN matrix model in the plane-wave limit at every fixed positive Chern-Simons level k. For multi-membrane families with fixed multiplicities, and hence fixed finite ABJM gauge rank, the equality holds as all longitudinal momenta tend to infinity with their pairwise differences fixed. Scalar-dressed equal-flux ABJM monopoles correspond to BMN vacua of fuzzy-sphere membranes: multiplicities count coincident membranes, whereas flux magnitudes give the momentum per membrane along the M-theory circle. At the index level, the associated Higgs residues yield a diagonal-holonomy integral for the ABJM plane-wave contribution, whose monopole-harmonic kernel agrees with the BMN matrix-harmonic kernel up to the finite BMN upper-spin cutoff. At finite momentum, the full index difference splits into the non-plane-wave remainder and the harmonic tail above this cutoff. Increasing k shifts the former to higher fugacity grade without changing the surviving kernel or the cutoff contribution. This gives a coefficientwise, multi-membrane extension of the earlier single-membrane index relation.

</details>


### [207] [Bogomol'nyi equations for Kruglov strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08111)
*I. Praseyto,U. Ubaydillah,H. S. Ramadhan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了基于Kruglov非线性电动力学的Abel规范-希格斯涡旋的Bogomol'nyi方程，通过无应力法导出一阶方程，并引入构型映射Φ(Y;σ)分析其单调性与适定性条件，得到六种典型σ值下的闭式解与数值涡旋剖面，发现BPS弦张力μ_BPS=2πn为纯拓扑量，与σ和β无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广标准Abel涡旋模型至更一般的非线性电动力学框架（Kruglov族），探究Bogomol'nyi结构在非线性规范场中的普适性与约束条件。

Method: 采用无应力法（stressless method）从空间应力张量消失直接导出一阶Bogomol'nyi方程；不预设希格斯势，而通过构型映射Φ(Y;σ)刻画规范场与希格斯场的隐式代数耦合；系统分析Φ的单调性、有界性及可逆性以确立BPS分支的存在性；对六个代表性σ值求得闭式构型关系、BPS势与规范场方程，并数值求解涡旋剖面。

Result: 证明当σ>1/2时Φ严格单调无界，0<σ<1/2时Φ存在有限极大值，σ=1/2为有界临界情形；由此导出非线性参数β的显式约束；获得六组σ（含线性、二次、三次代数结构）对应的解析BPS构型与数值涡旋解；确认BPS弦张力μ_BPS=2πn为纯拓扑量，与σ和β无关。

Conclusion: Kruglov非线性电动力学中Abel涡旋仍支持Bogomol'nyi分解，但BPS结构的存在性与适定性强烈依赖于指数σ的取值；构型映射Φ(Y;σ)的分析为非线性规范-希格斯系统的BPS分类提供了普适判据；拓扑张力的鲁棒性表明该类模型保持了经典涡旋的核心拓扑特征。

Abstract: We construct the Bogomol'nyi equations for Abelian gauge--Higgs vortices in which the Maxwell gauge sector is replaced by Kruglov nonlinear electrodynamics, a power-law family that interpolates between Maxwell theory, Born--Infeld electrodynamics, and exponential electrodynamics, characterized by a dimensionless exponent $σ$. Using the stressless method, we derive a pair of first-order equations directly from the vanishing of the spatial stress tensor, without assuming the Higgs potential \textit{a priori}. For generic $σ$, the gauge and Higgs sectors are coupled through an implicit algebraic relation. We therefore introduce a constitutive map $Φ(Y;σ)$ and analyze its monotonicity and range to determine the conditions for a smooth admissible Bogomol'nyi branch. For $σ>1/2$, the constitutive map is strictly monotonic and unbounded, whereas for $0<σ<1/2$ it possesses a finite maximum; the marginal case $σ=1/2$ is bounded. These properties yield explicit bounds on the nonlinear parameter $β$ for the latter cases. We further obtain closed-form constitutive relations, BPS potentials, and gauge-field equations for six representative values of $σ$, spanning linear, quadratic, and cubic algebraic structures. The corresponding vortex profiles are then computed numerically. The resulting BPS string tension is purely topological, $μ_{\rm BPS}=2πn$, independent of both $σ$ and $β$.

</details>


### [208] [Wilson loops, M2-branes and strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07928)
*Alexia Nix*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种普适方法，用于研究渐近AdS₄背景下试探M2膜与基本弦的半经典量子化，并将其与三维N≥2 Chern-Simons-物质理论中半BPS Wilson圈的真空期望值（全息对偶）联系起来；该方法给出Wilson圈半经典行为的新预言，并指出M2膜半经典量子化可复现Wilson圈关于群秩N的完整微扰展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立统一框架以处理不同探针（M2-brane与F-string）在AdS₄背景下的半经典量子化，并厘清其与三维N≥2 CS-matter理论中半BPS Wilson圈的全息对应关系，特别是其大N展开结构。

Method: 基于AdS/CFT全息对应，将探针在AdS₄中的半经典作用量计算与边界场论中Wilson圈的真空期望值关联；结合超对称定位（supersymmetric localization）结果进行比对，并分析全息系综（holographic ensemble）的选择对半经典展开阶数的影响。

Result: 1) 提出普适半经典量化方案；2) 预言Wilson圈在大N极限下的新半经典行为；3) 证明在恰当全息系综下，试探M2-brane的半经典量子化可精确复现Wilson圈关于N的完整微扰级数（即所有1/N^k项）。

Conclusion: 试探M2-brane比F-string更适合编码三维CS-matter理论中半BPS Wilson圈的完整大N结构；该工作强化了AdS₄/CFT₃中半经典几何与边界算符高阶量子修正之间的精确对应。

Abstract: We establish a universal approach for studying the semiclassical quantization of both probe M2-branes and fundamental strings in asymptotically AdS$_{4}$ backgrounds. On the field theory side, their partition functions are dual to the vacuum expectation value of half-BPS Wilson loops of a class of three-dimensional $\mathcal{N}\geq 2$ Chern-Simons-matter theories. Utilizing this universal approach we arrive at novel predictions for the semiclassical behaviour of the Wilson loop or upon availability match our results with the previously obtained expressions via supersymmetric localization. We also show that the correct choice of holographic ensemble suggests that the semiclassical quantization of probe M2-branes yields the full perturbative answer in the rank of the gauge group $N$ for the dual Wilson loop. This is a proceedings contribution to the ''Athens Workshop in Theoretical Physics: 10th Anniversary'', held at the National and Kapodistrian University of Athens on December 17-19 2025.

</details>


### [209] [Covariant Phase Space and Carroll-Weyl $χ$ Symmetry of Carroll Non-BPS D$_p$-branes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08874)
*Limin Zeng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究Klusoň提出的Carroll非BPS Dp-膜的协变相空间结构，通过Carroll极限（即光速c→0的收缩极限）分析其规范约束代数、相空间自由度及χ对称性实现问题；发现电型与磁型扇区在约束代数闭合性、自由度计数及对称性提升为第一类约束的能力上存在本质差异，并与零弦理论中的χ对称性行为形成对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Carroll对称性在非BPS膜动力学中的作用，厘清其相空间结构、约束分类与对称性实现机制，特别是与相对论性及零弦理论的对应与偏离。

Method: 采用协变相空间方法，对Klusoň的Carroll Dp-膜作用量实施规范分析：计算主约束、Dirac括号、约束代数闭合性；区分电型/磁型扇区；应用第二类约束消去与秩条件分析；检验Carroll–Weyl χ变换的辛不变性与第一类生成元可实现性。

Result: 1) 电型与磁型扇区的Hamiltonian约束主导项不同，导致前者弱闭合而后者强闭合；2) 非BPS扇区具D−1个局域相空间自由度，tachyon真空扇区在第二类背景条件下减至D−2个；3) 电型扇区满足一秩条件，真空扇区该条件增强；4) χ变换保辛形式且有守恒荷，但受约束阻碍，仅在高度受限的整体真空扇区可升格为第一类规范生成元。

Conclusion: Carroll非BPS膜的相空间结构具有内在扇区分化，其对称性实现比零弦理论更受限制，表明Carroll极限下规范结构与对称性提升存在根本性障碍，需引入额外几何或代数结构才能恢复完整第一类对称性。

Abstract: We analyze the covariant phase space of Klusoň's canonical Carroll non-BPS D\(_p\)-brane actions. The Carroll limit is formulated as a contraction of the canonical phase space: the canonical one-form is invariant under the scaling of conjugate pairs, while the leading Hamiltonian constraint differs between the electric-like and magnetic-like sectors. This difference controls the weak closure of the electric-like constraint algebra and the stronger closure of the magnetic-like Hamiltonian brackets. We separate the generic non-BPS sector, which carries \(D-1\) local phase-space degrees of freedom, from the tachyon-vacuum sector, which carries \(D-2\) only after imposing second-class background conditions. We also identify the rank condition for the generic electric-like sector and its strengthening in the vacuum sector. Finally, we show that the Carroll--Weyl \(χ\) transformation preserves the symplectic form and admits a charge, but is obstructed by the constraints and cannot be promoted to a first-class gauge generator except in a highly restricted global vacuum sector. This contrasts with null string theory, where a restricted \(χ\)-symmetry can be completed to an additional first-class constraint.

</details>


### [210] [Fortuitous ABJM Operators as Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08052)
*Connor Behan,Leonardo Pipolo de Gioia*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究ABJM理论中BPS态的分类与性质，通过异常维度矩阵对角化方法，识别出16条随N变化仅在有限点保持BPS的算符轨迹，并分析其大N极限、可积性预言、引力子耦合限制及与黑洞/光滑几何对偶的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分全息理论中对偶于无 horizon 几何与黑洞的算符；理解BPS条件在参数空间（如N）中的离散性与稳定性；比较ABJM与N=4 SYM中BPS谱的结构性差异。

Method: 在U(N)_k × U(N)_{-k} ABJM理论中，将N视为形式参数，对异常维度矩阵进行精确对角化；定义并追踪算符随N变化的‘轨迹’；筛选出仅在有限个N值下保持BPS的‘幸运轨迹’；结合上同调方法与大N展开分析其渐近行为与引力子耦合约束。

Result: 发现16条幸运BPS轨迹及其后代；其大N极限严格匹配可积性预言；多条轨迹表现出类似黑洞的‘选择性引力子 dressing’特性；BPS态与第一非BPS态之间的能隙行为在ABJM中定性区别于N=4 SYM，符合全息预期。

Conclusion: ABJM理论提供了比N=4 SYM更丰富的可控BPS谱结构，揭示了BPS性在参数空间中的脆弱性与全息几何分类的深层联系；‘幸运轨迹’是连接上同调、可积性、量子引力与黑洞微态物理的关键桥梁。

Abstract: Given a superconformal gauge theory at a fixed loop order, BPS states can be studied in two ways --- by enumerating cohomology classes of a nilpotent supercharge or by diagonalizing the anomalous dimension matrix. The second one allows for a deeper look at how holographic theories distinguish between operators based on whether they are dual to horizonless geometries or black holes. To this end, we carry out the diagonalization in $\mathrm{U}(N)_k \times \mathrm{U}(N)_{-k}$ ABJM theory with $N$ as a formal parameter. This makes it possible to define trajectories of operators and focus on the fortuitous ones which are characterized by being BPS at only finitely many values of $N$. In contrast to $\mathcal{N} = 4$ Super Yang-Mills, where it has been numerically prohibitive to study more than one of these, ABJM gives us access to 16 such trajectories along with their descendants. We show that they take an especially simple form at large $N$ where they agree with the predictions of integrability. We also show that many of them, as with black holes, can only be dressed by certain types of gravitons and comment on how this exercise in cohomology also has implications for properties of the full operators. Finally, we show that the gap to the first non-BPS state in ABJM theory qualitatively differs from that of $\mathcal{N} = 4$ Super Yang-Mills in the expected way.

</details>


### [211] [Scalar Casimir Effect on a Two-Dimensional Sphere with a Wu--Yang Magnetic Monopole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08378)
*Jun-Cheng Zhang,Xiao-Yin Pan,Juhao Wu,Qing-hai Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了带固定Wu-Yang磁单极的二维球面上复标量场的Casimir效应；利用广义Abel-Plana公式计算Casimir能量，发现强单极可使Casimir压力由吸引变为排斥，且该效应在宽范围曲率耦合下稳健存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非平凡拓扑背景（Wu-Yang磁单极）对量子真空涨落（Casimir效应）的影响，特别是其能否诱导排斥型真空应力，以期为引力与宇宙学中的反常真空行为提供新机制。

Method: 将带电标量场视为S²上非平凡复线丛的截面，解析求解Klein-Gordon方程，并采用适用于多值函数的广义Abel-Plana公式进行Casimir能量正则化与求和。

Result: Casimir压力随Wu-Yang单极强度增大可发生符号反转——从常规吸引变为排斥；该反转不依赖于特定曲率耦合参数，在ξ∈[0,1]范围内普适成立。

Conclusion: Wu-Yang磁单极通过其拓扑非平庸性可调控真空量子涨落的符号，构成一种几何-拓扑驱动的排斥真空应力源，为负压真空能的构造提供了严格可解模型。

Abstract: We investigate the Casimir effect of a complex scalar field on a two-dimensional sphere threaded by a fixed Wu--Yang magnetic monopole at the center. In this background the charged scalar field is a section of a nontrivial complex line bundle over $S^2$, reflecting the monopole's nontrivial topology. We solve the Klein--Gordon equation analytically and compute the Casimir energy using a generalized Abel--Plana formula for multivalued functions. We find that the monopole can reverse the sign of the Casimir pressure, turning an attractive force into a repulsive one when the monopole strength is sufficiently large. This behavior persists across a broad range of curvature couplings and suggests that Wu--Yang monopoles may provide a source of repulsive vacuum stress, with possible implications for gravitational and cosmological settings.

</details>


### [212] [Gravitational decoherence from soft-graviton $S$-matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08584)
*Hiroki Matsui*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于Weinberg软定理和相干辐射态重叠，推导出引力导致物质叠加态退相干的规范不变、非负软极限退相干指数；结果表明真空中的软辐射可区分分支在红外截断移除时失去相干性；非相对论主导项为纯四极矩形式；挤压态（单模/双模）可调控退相干强度，相位平均下增强程度由引力子占据数决定；对平坦能谱的遗迹引力波背景，退相干贡献随能量密度和观测时间四次方增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解引力如何导致量子叠加态退相干，特别是在软引力子主导的红外区域，以及非真空背景（如宇宙学挤压态、遗迹引力波）对退相干的影响。

Method: 从Weinberg软定理出发，结合相干态重叠计算分支间密度矩阵元；严格求和极化自由度并解析积分角度变量；推广至单模与双模 squeezed graviton states；对相位平均与平直能谱遗迹背景作红外标度识别（τ⁻¹为红外截断）。

Result: 得到规范不变、非负的软极限退相干指数；真空下可区分软辐射分支的相干性在ε→0时消失；非相对论极限下退相干由四极矩主导；squeeze参数与相位调控退相干强度，相位平均后增强正比于⟨n⟩；遗迹引力波背景中退相干率∝ρ_GW τ⁴。

Conclusion: 软引力子散射是引力退相干的基本机制；红外量子信息结构深刻影响退相干行为；非真空引力背景（尤其是宇宙学起源的双模挤压态与平谱遗迹波）可显著增强退相干效应，为量子引力现象学提供可观测窗口。

Abstract: We revisit gravitational decoherence of matter superpositions using the leading soft sector of the graviton $S$-matrix. Starting from Weinberg's soft theorem and the overlap of coherent radiation states, we derive, for branch configurations with the same total four-momentum, a gauge-invariant, nonnegative leading-soft decoherence exponent and evaluate the polarization sum and angular integrals exactly at this order. In the vacuum, coherence between branches with distinguishable soft radiation vanishes as the infrared cutoff is removed. The leading nonrelativistic term is purely quadrupolar as a consequence of energy--momentum conservation. We extend the analysis to single- and two-mode squeezed graviton states, including the two-mode case motivated by inflation. Squeezing can enhance or suppress decoherence depending on the relative phase, while phase averaging gives an enhancement governed by the graviton occupation. For a phase-averaged relic background with an approximately flat energy-density spectrum over the relevant soft frequency band, the sharp-cutoff identification of the infrared scale with the inverse observation time gives a contribution that grows with the gravitational-wave energy density and with the fourth power of the observation time. These results connect soft-graviton scattering, infrared quantum information, and decoherence in nonvacuum gravitational backgrounds.

</details>


### [213] [Symplectic Chern-Simons theories dual to Abelian rank-$0$ theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08648)
*Tadashi Okazaki,Douglas J. Smith*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出3维辛Chern-Simons理论USp(2n)在半整数能级k=n+1/2+ℓ下与Creutzig-Garner-Kim定义的Abel型rank-0理论T_{ℓ,n}存在对偶性，并验证了超对称真空计数与超对称指标的一致性，特别分析了ℓ=1情形下的最小非Abel理论及其U(1)^n对偶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立3d N=4超对称Chern-Simons理论中非Abel与Abel描述之间的精确对偶，拓展已知的镜像对称与等级-0对偶框架，并理解半整数量子化与伪实表示对超对称结构的影响。

Method: 基于N=4超对称性、半整数量子化条件（fundamental为pseudo-real）、Higgs/Coulomb分支消失约束、实质量形变下的真空计数匹配及超对称指数计算，结合monopole超势与tadpole level matrix构造对偶映射。

Result: 证实USp(2n)_k（k=n+1/2+ℓ）与T_{ℓ,n}严格对偶；对所有(n,ℓ)匹配实质量变形下超对称真空数；部分情形验证超对称指数一致；明确ℓ=1时对偶为U(1)^n带tadpole level matrix与bare monopole superpotential的理论。

Conclusion: 该对偶揭示了半整数量子化的伪实Chern-Simons理论可完全由Abel rank-0理论描述，为3d N=4镜像对称提供了新类，且暗示更广义的等级-0/等级-n对偶结构。

Abstract: We propose that the 3d symplectic Chern-Simons theory $USp(2n)$ at level $k=n+\frac12+\ell$ with a single chiral multiplet in the fundamental representation is dual, for every pair of positive integers $(n,\ell)$, to the Abelian rank-$0$ theory $\mathcal{T}_{\ell,n}$ of Creutzig, Garner and Kim. The fundamental is pseudo-real, so the level is half-integral, there is no non-Abelian flavor symmetry, and the supersymmetry is enhanced to $\mathcal{N}=4$. Both sides have vanishing Higgs and Coulomb branches. We match the counting of supersymmetric vacua under real mass deformations for all $(n,\ell)$, and the supersymmetric indices in a number of cases. We then study in detail the case $\ell=1$, the minimal $USp(2n)$ Chern-Simons theory at level $n+\frac32$, whose dual is a $U(1)^{n}$ theory with a tadpole level matrix and a bare monopole superpotential.

</details>


### [214] [Mass for Particles from Extra Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08697)
*F. E. A. de Souza,M. O. Tahim,R. I. de Oliveira Junior,I. M. Macêdo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在二维余维hedgehog膜世界中，通过粒子在紧致角向额外维中的运动实现几何质量生成的机制；该机制导致动力学确定的Kaluza-Klein谱，并揭示了自旋-曲率耦合对KK模局域化模式的能级依赖调制作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高维膜世界中四维有效质量的几何起源，特别是无标量场情形下如何通过背景几何与自旋相互作用实现质量生成。

Method: 采用经典粒子力学框架与正则量子化方法，分析质量粒子在含通量支撑的codimension-two弯曲背景中的运动；引入N=1,2自旋粒子模型，考察自旋-曲率耦合对有效势及KK谱的影响。

Result: 发现玻色子无径向束缚态，但费米子（N=1,2）因自旋-曲率耦合产生能级依赖的KK模局域化；获得由几何参数决定的离散、动力学生成的质量谱。

Conclusion: 确立了通量支撑的二维余维膜世界中几何质量生成的粒子力学实现形式，凸显了体空间几何、自旋自由度与四维有效质量谱之间的深层关联。

Abstract: We investigate a geometric mechanism for mass generation in a codimension-two hedgehog braneworld. A massless particle propagating in the bulk acquires an effective four-dimensional mass through its motion along the compact angular extra dimension, yielding a dynamically determined Kaluza-Klein spectrum after canonical quantization. While the bosonic sector exhibits no radial confinement, extending the analysis to N = 1, 2 spinning-particle models shows that the spin-curvature coupling modifies the effective potential and introduces a level-dependent localization pattern for the Kaluza-Klein modes. The results establish a particle-mechanics realization of geometric mass generation in flux-supported codimension-two braneworlds and highlight the interplay between bulk geometry, spin, and the effective four-dimensional mass spectrum.

</details>


### [215] [Splitting Dynamics of Multiply Quantized Vortices in Holographic Superfluid of Finite Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08831)
*Shanquan Lan,Xin Li,Jiexiong Mo,Yu Tian,Yu-Kun Yan,Hongbao Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维全息超流体中高绕数涡旋（n=5-8）在有限温度下的分裂动力学，结合准正规模线性扰动分析与非线性实时数值模拟，揭示了三个新现象：(1) 不稳定模数量偏离2n−3公式；(2) 主导不稳定模随温度升高呈现跳跃式跃迁；(3) 单一分裂模式可包含多个具有不同拓扑结构的子分裂模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高绕数涡旋在强耦合超流体中的稳定性与分裂机制，弥补传统BEC理论在强耦合极限下的不足，并为冷原子实验提供全息对偶预言。

Method: 采用AdS/CFT对偶构建二维全息超流体模型，通过求解Boltzmann–Einstein方程的准正规模谱进行线性稳定性分析，并辅以全息重正化后的非线性实时数值模拟验证。

Result: 发现n=8涡旋仅存在2n−4个不稳定模；n≥7时主导不稳定模p随温度升高发生不连续跃迁；n=8的l=4分裂模式在不同温度区间呈现三种拓扑不同的子模式。

Conclusion: 高绕数涡旋的分裂行为在强耦合全息超流体中展现出超越弱耦合Gross–Pitaevskii框架的新普适特征，表明全息方法能有效刻画真实量子多体系统中的非微扰涡旋动力学。

Abstract: We study the splitting dynamics of multiply quantized vortices with winding numbers $n=5,6,7$ and $8$ in a two-dimensional holographic superfluid at finite temperature, by combining linear perturbation analysis of quasinormal modes with fully nonlinear real-time numerical simulations. Three new physical phenomena are revealed. First, the number of unstable modes no longer strictly follows the $2n-3$ formula as $n$ increases. For the vortex with $n=8$, the unstable mode with $p=2(n-1)$ is absent throughout the entire temperature range, so that only $2n-4$ unstable modes exist. Second, the transition of the dominant unstable mode with increasing temperature exhibits new characteristics. For vortices with $n\le 6$, the dominant mode changes sequentially as $p=2,3,\dots,n$, whereas for $n\ge 7$ jump-like transitions occur-for instance, for $n=7$ the dominant mode jumps from $p=2$ to $p=4$ at $T=0.325T_c$ and then directly to $p=7$ at $T=0.359T_c$, and for $n=8$ it jumps directly from $p=2$ to $p=8$ at $T=0.302T_c$. Third, a single splitting pattern of high-winding-number vortices can contain multiple sub-splitting patterns with distinct topological structures, as exemplified by the $l=4$ pattern of the $n=8$ vortex, which exhibits three sub-patterns at low, intermediate and high temperatures. The nonlinear simulations confirm the predictions of the linear stability analysis, and the implications of our results for cold-atom experiments are discussed.

</details>


### [216] [Quantum spacetime and gravity from the IKKT matrix model: an invitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08955)
*Harold C. Steinacker*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于IKKT矩阵模型构建了一个关于时空、引力与基础物理的新框架，强调概念与结构层面，利用非平凡真空（即矩阵背景）作为3+1维非对易时空，并具体构造了描述FLRW宇宙演化的背景解；该背景满足单圈有效作用量，具有有限自由度密度；其涨落导出弱耦合规范场与一种非标准量子引力描述，导致红外修正；最大超对称性是保证有效局域性的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一量子力学与广义相对论，寻求一个从第一性原理出发、兼具紫外完备性与低能有效描述能力的基础理论框架；IKKT模型因其背景无关性、有限自由度及自然蕴含超对称与非对易几何而成为理想候选。

Method: 采用IKKT矩阵模型作为基本动力学出发点，选取特定非平凡矩阵真空（即非对易FLRW背景）作为经典解；通过计算其一阶量子修正（单圈有效作用量）验证稳定性与物理合理性；在该背景上展开微扰，提取低能有效场论，识别规范场、引力模与红外修正项；利用超对称性约束分析有效局域性与谱性质。

Result: 构造出一类可描述膨胀、均匀各向同性FLRW宇宙的非对易矩阵背景，满足单圈有效方程且每单位体积自由度有限；证明其微扰激发对应弱耦合U(N)规范理论与一种由矩阵涨落诱导的非标准引力动力学，包含可计算的红外修正（如退耦或色散效应）；确立最大N=2超对称性对抑制高维算符、保障低能局域性起决定性作用。

Conclusion: IKKT矩阵模型可通过合适非对易真空实现可观测宇宙的几何与动力学，其微扰理论自然导出规范相互作用与量子引力效应，表明该模型具备作为统一理论候选者的结构性潜力；红外修正提供了可证伪的低能现象学信号，而超对称性不仅是技术必要条件，更构成有效时空结构的深层保障。

Abstract: This is a concise and compact introduction to a framework for spacetime, gravity, and fundamental physics based on the IKKT matrix model, focusing on conceptual and structural aspects. The approach rests on nontrivial vacua or matrix backgrounds, which serve as 3+1-dimensional noncommutative spacetime. We discuss a specific class of backgrounds describing expanding homogeneous and isotropic FLRW spacetime, which provide a solution of the one-loop effective action with finitely many dof per volume. Perturbations of the background give rise to fields propagating on spacetime governed by a weakly-coupled gauge theory, as well as a nonstandard description of (quantum) gravity leading to some IR modifications. Maximal supersymmetry of the model is essential to ensure effective locality.

</details>


### [217] [5d SCFT fixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09007)
*Michele Del Zotto,Andrea Sangiovanni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文引入了一类新的5d超共形场论（SCFT）'fixtures'，填补了5d SCFT原子分类中非单连通李代数全局对称性构建块的空白；其物理实现为Higgs分支RG流，几何实现为M理论在特定奇异三维流形上的紧化，并由此构造出具有非单连通味对称性的5d共形物质SCFT；同时讨论了其圆周紧化与4d class-S理论的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完善5d SCFT的原子分类，特别是补全具有非单连通（non-simply laced）全局对称性的构建块缺失。

Method: 基于Higgs分支RG流的物理图像，结合M理论在奇异三重态（singular threefolds）上的几何实现，构造新型5d SCFT fixtures，并分析其圆周紧化对应4d class-S理论。

Result: 成功构造一类具有非单连通风味对称群（如B_n, C_n, F_4, G_2）的5d共形物质SCFT；明确了其作为UV固定点的结构及其几何实现；建立了与4d class-S理论的联系。

Conclusion: 5d SCFT fixtures提供了非单连通对称性下5d共形物质的新构造范式，拓展了5d SCFT的分类框架，并深化了5d/4d对偶关系的理解。

Abstract: In the context of the atomic classification of 5d SCFTs building blocks with non-simply laced global symmetries are lacking. In this work we fill this gap by introducing a new class of 5d UV fixed points (the 5d SCFT "fixtures"). Physically, 5d SCFTs fixtures are the result of a Higgs branch RG flow, realized geometrically by M-theory on singular threefolds which we identify explicitly. In particular, this enables the construction of 5d conformal matter SCFTs with non-simply laced flavor symmetry. Along the way, we discuss their circle reduction in relation to 4d theories of class-S type.

</details>


### [218] [M5 Instantons in ABJM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09041)
*Jieming Lin,Jesse van Muiden,Zihan Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了M理论中渐近AdS$_4 \times S^7/\mathbf{Z}_k$ 背景下超对称M5-膜瞬子的存在性，发现其κ-对称嵌入强制局域于AdS$_4$部分的等变不动点并包裹$(S^3\times S^3)/\mathbf{Z}_k$；量子层面其一圈图配分函数为零，表明体部配分函数应以超引力模与M2-膜瞬子展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解ABJM理论在AdS/CFT对应中由M5-膜瞬子所贡献的非微扰效应，特别是其与已知$S^3$配分函数结果的一致性。

Method: 利用M5-膜的κ-对称性分析超对称嵌入条件，结合等变几何与渐近AdS$_4$结构确定瞬子定位；通过一圈图计算其量子涨落配分函数。

Result: 超对称M5-瞬子必须局域于AdS$_4$中的等变不动点并包裹$(S^3\times S^3)/\mathbf{Z}_k$；其一圈图配分函数严格为零。

Conclusion: M5-膜瞬子在该背景中不贡献非微扰修正；体部路径积分应由超引力模与M2-膜瞬子主导，与ABJM理论$S^3$配分函数的已知结构一致。

Abstract: We study the existence of supersymmetric M5-brane instantons in asymptotically AdS$_4 \times S^7/\mathbf{Z}_k$ geometries of M-theory, dual to the ABJM theory. The supersymmetric embeddings of these instantons are determined by the M5-brane $κ$-symmetry, which forces them to localise to the equivariant fixed points in the asymptotically AdS$_4$ geometry, and wrap $(S^3\times S^3)/\mathbf{Z}_k $. At the quantum level we find that the one-loop partition function of these instantons vanishes, suggesting that the bulk partition function is instead to be expanded in terms of supergravity modes and M2-brane instantons, in line with known results for the $S^3$ partition function of the dual ABJM theory.

</details>
